### [52] [Understanding Developer Pain Points in Federated Learning: Insights from Stack Overflow and GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19621)
*Sahand Saed,Khairul Alam,Banani Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项针对联邦学习（FL）开发者的实证研究，通过分析495篇Stack Overflow帖子和9,116个GitHub问题/拉取请求（来自92个FL相关项目），系统刻画了FL开发者面临的实际困难。研究采用BERTopic主题建模与难度指标（未解决率、中位解决时间），并结合How/Why/What/Other问题意图分类，揭示了栈溢出与GitHub两大平台上FL开发挑战的共性与差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习因分布式执行、框架快速演进、隐私与治理要求等特性，使开发者面临独特的工程门槛。然而现有FL研究多聚焦于算法、隐私、系统层面的理论分析，缺乏对开发者日常实践中安装、配置、调试、部署、维护等痛点的实证洞察。研究旨在以开发者为中心，挖掘公开讨论中反复出现且难以解决的技术难题，为框架设计者、文档作者和教育者提供改进依据。

Method: 采用混合方法：（1）数据采集——通过SO数据快照按`federated-learning`及相关标签（如`pysyft`、`tensorflow-federated`、`flower`）筛选FL帖子；通过GitHub REST API获取300星以上FL仓库（含≥2016年创建的活跃项目）的问题与PR。（2）数据预处理——正则清除HTML/链接/代码块，NLTK停用词移除，lemmatization。（3）主题建模——使用SentenceTransformers（multi-qa-MiniLM-L6-dot-v1）生成嵌入，UMAP降维，HDBSCAN聚类，CountVectorizer生成unigram+bigram表示，最优一致性得分SO=0.63，GitHub=0.64。（4）问题意图分类——基于规则的分类器与facebook/bart-large-mnli零样本分类混合管道，并通过440篇手工标注（κ=0.78）验证。（5）难度评估——SO采用未接受答案比例与中位接受答案时间；GitHub采用未解决比例与中位关闭时间。（6）跨平台比较——Pearson卡方检验、Cramér's V、odds ratio与Holm校正。

Result: 研究识别出9个Stack Overflow主题与13个GitHub主题。SO主题围绕TFF实现、API/兼容性错误、收敛与评估不匹配、模型状态管理、安装环境兼容性、数据分区、PySyft设置、差分隐私（DP）、框架范围与部署现实；GitHub主题覆盖部署运营失败、数据分区管道、调试配置、可复现基准、Wiki/文档/协作、训练不稳、SecureBoost特征工程、KubeFATE/Docker/GPU/RPC等技术运维层面。意图分析显示SO以"How"类问题为主导（51.11%），GitHub以"Why"类为主（43.86%），且差异在统计上显著（χ²=224.30, p<0.001）。难度分析表明"TFF安装与环境兼容性"、"PySyft设置与API破坏"、"联邦特征工程与SecureBoost"、"联邦Wiki文档"以及运行时/RPC故障等主题具有高未解决率或极长中位解决时间。主题演化显示讨论从基础概念逐渐转向部署、运维与隐私集成等复杂工程问题。

Conclusion: 研究证实FL开发者在环境配置、API迁移、non-IID训练不稳定性、评估正确性、隐私机制集成等方面面临持续性挑战；SO与GitHub在开发者支持角色上互补——前者偏向程序性指导，后者偏向根因诊断。建议框架设计者加强兼容性管理、配置验证、可观测性与部署支持；文档作者应更新面向任务的操作指南和可执行示例；教育者应在算法之外补充分布式调试与隐私工程实践。论文还提供了可复现的分析流程，可作为持续监测FL开发者痛点的方法论基础，并将在未来扩展至LLM辅助开发、访谈与领域案例研究。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without centralizing raw data, but building and operating FL systems remains difficult due to distributed execution, rapidly evolving frameworks, and privacy and governance requirements. In this paper, we present an empirical study of FL developer challenges by independently analyzing 495 Stack Overflow posts and 9,116 GitHub issues and pull requests from 92 FL-related projects. Using BERTopic-based topic modeling and difficulty indicators such as unresolved rates and median resolution time, we characterize recurring problem areas and compare how they manifest across the two support platforms, Stack Overflow and GitHub. Our analysis surfaces nine dominant Stack Overflow topics and thirteen GitHub topics, with persistent difficulties concentrated in environment setup and dependency compatibility, API breakages and migration, training instability under non-IID data, evaluation and metric correctness, and the integration of privacy-preserving mechanisms. We also categorize posts by question intent to understand the kinds of help developers seek; this intent analysis shows that "How"-type questions dominate, reflecting strong demand for procedural guidance. Several topics, such as "TFF Installation and Environment Compatibility" and "Federated Feature Engineering and SecureBoost Issues," exhibit high unresolved rates and long resolution times, suggesting shortcomings in tooling, documentation, and debugging support. Based on these findings, we provide actionable implications for FL framework designers, documentation authors, and educators. Although our results are constrained to public discussions and a subset of widely discussed frameworks, the study offers a scalable method for continuously monitoring developer pain points and improving the usability, reliability, and deployability of FL systems.

</details>


### [53] [PerfAgent: Profiler-Guided Iterative Refinement for Repository-Level Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19653)
*Ryan Deng,Yuanzhe Liu,Bastian Lipka,Yao Ma,Xuhao Chen,Tim Kaler,Jatin Ganhotra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PerfAgent 是一个 profiler-guided、verifier-in-the-loop 的代理工作流，通过使用 py-spy 分析器精确定位瓶颈、强制多轮迭代优化（保留最佳而非最后的正确补丁），以及基于 pytest-testmon 的选择性测试反馈，将通用代码代理转化为仓库级代码性能优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 代理虽然在 SWE-Bench 等正确性任务上表现出色，但在仓库级代码优化（GSO、SWE-fficiency）上仍难以匹敌人类专家的速度。作者识别出三大失败模式：(1) 未能通过 profiling 定位真实瓶颈（尤其是抽象层之下和原生扩展内部的热点）；(2) 在第一个通过的补丁处过早终止，止步于浅层加速；(3) 对复杂改动测试不充分，导致补丁在目标工作负载上虽快却在边缘场景中静默失效。仅仅通过测试是不够的，优化必须同时保证正确性、实现性能改进并接近专家加速比。

Method: PerfAgent 基于 Mini-SWE-Agent 构建，在每次迭代中注入三类反馈：(1) **精选 profiler 摘要**——使用 py-spy 采样剖析器（覆盖 Python 与原生扩展）收集栈帧，过滤 setup 帧后聚合成带 self/total time、调用上下文和 native 标记的热点，再由一次 LLM 调用压缩为自然语言摘要；(2) **目标驱动的循环控制器**——在每次提交后拦截 STOP 信号，重新构建、运行测试、重新剖析，将更新的热点摘要与实测 speedup 反馈给代理，强制迭代最多 θ=5 次，并保留所有迭代中最快且正确的补丁；(3) **选择性验证**——使用 pytest-testmon 基于代码覆盖率仅运行受改动影响的测试，减少 47%–99% 测试开销以低成本捕获回归。该工作流与具体 harness 解耦，可移植到 OpenHands、Trae Agent 等代理。

Result: 在 GPT-5.1 上，PerfAgent 将专家匹配率较 OpenHands 提升超过 2 倍：GSO 上 Opt@1 从 19.6% 提升至 39.2%，SWE-fficiency-Lite 上从 26% 提升至 74%（hack-adjusted）。逐任务胜负分析显示在 GSO 上 PerfAgent 赢 27 场 vs OpenHands 7 场，在 SWE-fficiency-Lite 上 52 vs 4。消融实验表明，性能提升主要来自 profiler 反馈，单独加入测试反馈反而拉低 GSO 表现，而两者结合达到最佳；profiler 反馈使代理对 48% 的 GSO 实例触及 C/C++/Cython/Rust 低层代码（OpenHands 仅 31%）。在 Kimi-K2 等开源模型上亦观察到一致提升。值得注意的是，PerfAgent 以更低成本超越了 oracle best-of-5 测试时缩放基线（GSO 上 2.88 美元 vs 11.01 美元），证明增益来自更优反馈而非更多采样。

Conclusion: 作者结论是，性能工程是智能体软件工程中一个独立且高要求的维度，奖励能推理"代码如何运行"而不仅是"代码是否正确"的代理。PerfAgent 通过 (1) 精选 profiler 摘要、(2) 强制多轮目标驱动迭代、(3) 选择性测试三个机制针对性解决三大失败模式，使通用代码代理能够跨抽象边界优化真实仓库（包括原生扩展），并保持正确性。未来工作包括扩展到 Codex、Claude Code 等更强大 harness、覆盖原生扩展的选择性测试、抵御新型 reward hacking，以及推广到更多语言和模型。作者强调，性能优化需要鲁棒的测量与反作弊评分机制，否则缓存类"伪加速"会污染评估。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now perform well on correctness-oriented repository-level tasks, including SWE-Bench issue resolution and feature implementation in real codebases. However, they still struggle with repository-level code optimization, which requires preserving behavior while improving runtime performance. Passing tests is not enough in this setting; a patch must preserve behavior, implement code optimization, and approach expert speedups. Current agents often miss bottlenecks hidden behind abstraction layers and native extensions, stop after shallow speedups, or insufficiently test the code patches that thus may silently break edge cases. We present PerfAgent, a profiler-guided, verifier-in-the-loop workflow that gives an off-the-shelf coding agent the feedback needed to find real hotspots, improve beyond the first passing patch, and use profiler evidence rather than timing alone to decide what to optimize next. On two challenging optimization benchmarks, GSO and SWE-fficiency-Lite, PerfAgent more than doubles the rate of expert-matching patches over OpenHands with GPT-5.1, improving from 19.6% to 39.2% on GSO and from 26% to 74% on SWE-fficiency-Lite. It also surpasses an oracle best-of-five baseline at substantially lower cost, showing that the gains come from better feedback rather than additional test-time sampling.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Fail-to-Pass: Iterative Hardening of Co-Generated Bug Reproduction Tests and Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19843)
*Yuhao Tan,Zhibang Yang,Fangkai Yang,Yuan Yao,Yu Kang,Lu Wang,Pu Zhao,Xin Zhang,Xiaoxing Ma,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoHarden框架，超越传统F→P标准评估自动程序修复中的Bug复现测试(BRT)。通过引入突变补丁评估(MPE)将BRT分为严格(Rigorous)、宽松(Lax)和错位(Misaligned)三类，发现F→P通过测试中只有严格类真正提升修复成功率。CoHarden采用两阶段共生循环（lax-init先写测试 + 迭代硬化），在SWE-bench Verified上达到69.4% Resolved和78.9% F→P。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动程序修复(APR)工作中，Bug复现测试(BRT)主要使用fail-to-pass(F→P)准则评估，但F→P仅验证测试能否在有bug代码上失败、在正确修复上通过，无法衡量测试是否能有效排除看似合理但错误的补丁。此外，共生(Co-generation)方法中存在测试-修复错误耦合问题，导致自检通过但实际未修复bug。亟需更精细的BRT质量评估与生成方法。

Method: 1. 突变补丁评估(MPE)：基于5种语义突变算子（vanilla、症状抑制、不完整修复、输入特定捷径、行为替换）构建突变补丁池，用混淆矩阵将BRT分为Rigorous/Lax/Misaligned三类，Laxity率β/total为关键指标。
2. CoHarden两阶段共生框架：
   - Phase 1 (Lax-init)：先生成复现测试t₀，禁止修改源码，避免早期错位
   - Phase 2 (Hardening)：迭代执行Mutate→Evaluate→Harden，使用Temporal Matrix（用上一轮测试t_{k-1}替代golden test作为参考）检测Lax回归，当ℓ_k≤τ=0.2且测试非平凡变化时收敛
3. PluggableCritic机制：结合skill的可插拔性与critic的轨迹访问能力，在agent的finish动作处实现verify-and-feedback边界

Result: SWE-bench Verified∩SWT-bench Verified (433实例, GPT-5-mini)：Resolved 69.4%（超越最强fix-only基线+9.6pp，超越最强共生基线+7.9pp），F→P 78.9%。跨backbone（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.5）和跨基准（SWE-bench Lite）均保持领先。消融实验：去除lax-init降至62.4%，去除硬化降至61.0%，Ralph自优化基线仅63.8%。开销$0.84/实例。Rigorous测试提升修复+8.5pp，Lax测试Δ=0，证实质量维度独立性。

Conclusion: F→P作为BRT评估准则是必要但粗糙的；修复端增益完全集中在Rigorous子集中。共生方法因测试-修复耦合产生Lax测试与相关错误，削弱了in-trajectory F→P信号的有效性。通过将MPE的Lax信号作为in-loop收敛标准（Temporal Matrix），CoHarden实现了无需golden artifact的参考无关硬化机制，在多个LLM backbone和基准上一致优于现有fix-only与共生方法。局限：突变算子覆盖依赖LLM对"似是而非错误修复"的概念，仅验证Python SWE-bench场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made automated program repair (APR) increasingly practical for real-world bugs, but repairing directly from bug reports remains underconstrained. Bug reproduction tests (BRTs) help close this gap by turning a bug report into an executable, bug-specific signal that can guide repair and validate candidate patches. Existing work has therefore studied BRT generation as a core subproblem in APR and mainly evaluates a generated BRT using the fail-to-pass (F->P) criterion, which requires the test to fail on the buggy code but pass on the golden fix. We show that F->P alone is insufficient when the goal of a BRT is to improve downstream repair. In particular, some F->P BRTs are lax, reproducing the observed symptom yet still admitting plausible-but-incorrect patches. We formalize this missing quality dimension by separating F->P BRTs into rigorous and lax ones, and show empirically that only the former consistently improve repair success. We further find that co-generation introduces test--fix error coupling, where the in-trajectory fail-to-pass (F->P) check can pass even when both the generated patch and generated test are wrong. Based on these findings, we propose CoHarden, a co-generation framework that uses the Lax signal as an in-loop convergence criterion. CoHarden first generates a test before any fix, then iteratively hardens the test and fix against surviving mutation patches until the generated test no longer admits Lax regressions. Experiments show that CoHarden reaches 69.4% Resolved and 78.9% F->P on SWE-bench Verified, outperforming the strongest fix-only and cogeneration baselines by +9.6 and +7.9 percentage points in Resolved, respectively, with consistent gains across LLM backbones and benchmarks.

</details>


### [55] [Don't Trust the Label: License Laundering in AI Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20300)
*James Jewitt,Hao Li,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对AI供应链（数据集→模型→应用）中许可证"洗白"现象进行了大规模实证测量。通过追踪Hugging Face与GitHub之间232,270条供应链，量化了"未知洗白"（无许可证资产获得确定性下游标签）和"类别洗白"（许可证类别在传递过程中被替换）两类风险，发现62.3%的链路经过至少一个无许可证资产，且所有义务性许可证类别的端到端存活率均低于7%，而宽松许可证存活率高达95.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工件（数据集、模型、应用）在多平台供应链中流通，每个工件均携带许可证，但其法律义务在跨平台、跨范式传递时是否被"剥离"或"替换"尚无系统性研究。开源软件领域已有成熟的SCA工具（FOSSology、Black Duck等）用于许可证审计，但AI供应链依赖元数据标签而非锁文件、跨Hugging Face与GitHub平台，因此现有合规工具无法识别"许可证洗白"风险。缺乏这种度量，业界无法判断该问题是偶发还是系统性的，也无法定位风险集中点。

Method: 研究构建了数据集→模型→应用的供应链追踪方法：(1)通过Hugging Face元数据中的数据集依赖关系链接数据集与模型；(2)通过GitHub代码搜索与AST分析识别调用这些模型的应用；(3)使用ScanCode提取各仓库的许可证标签。初步收集得到294,012条候选链路，经筛选后保留232,270条链路（涵盖3,120个数据集、5,556个模型、24,076个应用）。将观测到的765个唯一许可证字符串映射到7个基于施加义务的类别（宽松型、Copyleft、Sharealike、ML许可证、CC限制型、公共领域、未知）。对每条链路分别测量"未知洗白"（Unknown→Known的过渡比例）与"类别洗白"（上游许可证类别在下游是否消失），并在每个过渡点及端到端层级构建转换矩阵，并使用Lorenz曲线评估洗白集中度。

Result: 研究发现：(1)62.3%的链路（144,631/232,270）经过至少一个无许可证声明的资产，且集中于少数基础数据集——前10%的未知数据集产生了89.5%的数据集→模型洗白过渡（ImageNet-1K传播至1,709个应用）；(2)在双向流动中，88.8%含未知数据集的链路到达已知模型，80.3%含未知模型的链路到达已知应用，但24.9%的已知许可模型链路终结于无许可应用；(3)在87,639条完全已知链路中，37.5%至少发生一次类别洗白；(4)宽松许可证端到端存活率95.1%，而所有义务性类别均低于7%（Sharealike 4.7%、ML License端到端仅4.2%、Copyleft端到端远低于7%、CC-Restrictive 0%）；(5)消失的义务几乎都"塌缩"为宽松许可证（如93.1%的Copyleft过渡到宽松型）。典型案例包括Books3（盗版数据集以MIT标签流通）导致Anthropic赔偿15亿美元，以及StarCoder的ML许可证在314/321个下游应用中被剥离。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：AI供应链中许可证义务的系统性丢失是普遍且高度集中的现象，现有两十年历史的开源合规基础设施（SCA工具、SPDX 3.0、CycloneDX）无法覆盖跨平台、跨范式的AI供应链。文章向四类利益相关方提出具体建议：(1)实践者在集成模型前应人工核验上游数据集许可证；(2)模型发布者应在Hugging Face model card中声明训练数据集及其许可证类别（仅7.1%的模型目前这样做）；(3)权利持有者不能依赖标签传播，应在元数据外叠加合同或门控访问等强制机制；(4)平台所有者和工具开发者应构建跨平台自动追踪器，自动从元数据生成AI BOM，并在Hugging Face与GitHub中提示上游类别冲突。许可证标签在当前AI生态中已成为不可信赖的合规信号，亟需新的基础设施支持。

Abstract: AI artifacts move through a multi-platform supply chain, spanning datasets and models on Hugging Face and applications on GitHub. While each artifact carries a license whose obligations should propagate through redistribution, no study has yet measured whether those obligations survive the chain or are stripped and replaced as artifacts move downstream. We trace 232,270 dataset$\rightarrow$model$\rightarrow$application chains and quantify two forms of license laundering: when artifacts with no declared license acquire definitive labels downstream, and when one declared license category replaces another during redistribution. We find that 62.3% of chains pass through at least one artifact with no declared license (concentrated in a small set of foundational datasets), and that every obligation-bearing license category falls below 7% end-to-end survival while the Permissive category reaches 95.1%. Based on these findings, we provide actionable recommendations for practitioners, model publishers, rights holders, and platform owners.

</details>


### [56] [Context Matters: Improving the Practical Reliability of LLM-Based Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19682)
*Junjie Chen,Ziqi Wang,Lin Yang,Chen Yang,Xiao Chu,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Qianxiang Wang,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇经验报告,提出了一种面向工业场景的上下文感知LLM单元测试生成工作流CATGen。该工作流通过显式检索项目级上下文、确定性构建测试类骨架、以及基于程序分析的轻量级后处理,显著提升了LLM生成测试的编译成功率、结构覆盖率和生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者在工业部署LLM单元测试生成工具时发现,现有方法在真实项目中频繁出现三类问题:(1)项目级依赖上下文不足导致编译失败;(2)测试类骨架脆弱易失效;(3)迭代式LLM修复代价高昂且效果不稳定。这些问题使得研究中的优异结果难以转化为实际可用性。论文认为根源在于过度依赖LLM隐式推断上下文,缺乏系统性的工程支撑。

Method: CATGen采用四阶段工作流:(1)上下文检索——通过AST解析和构建配置解析,显式收集内部类、焦点方法、外部调用、测试框架与Mock框架五类上下文;(2)上下文感知骨架构建——基于框架-模板映射,确定性生成包含注解、生命周期方法、Mock字段与导入语句的测试类骨架;(3)骨架条件补全——以预填充骨架为锚点,让LLM仅完成测试方法体,减少结构漂移;(4)基于程序分析的后处理——包含8条确定性修复规则(包声明补全、导入补充、注解校正、引用解析、私有成员反射、签名对齐、异常规范、兜底断言)和覆盖增强模块(针对未覆盖的决策点生成Given-When-Then测试)。整个流程仅需单轮LLM调用,后处理完全基于静态分析,不依赖LLM。

Result: 在工业基准(183个焦点方法,8个专有项目)上,CATGen在CSR上达到91.83%(比基线提升24.72%-38.05%),CovL提升17.27%-22.17%,CovB提升15.31%-18.24%;时间减少51.27%-83.28%,token消耗减少66.83%-83.86%。在开源基准Defects4J上,CATGen同样在CSR、CovL、CovB和变异分数(MS)上全面超越所有LLM基线。消融实验表明三个核心组件各自承担不可替代的角色;将CATGen的后处理模块替换到ChatTester、ChatUniTest、HITS中也带来显著增益,验证了确定性分析优于LLM迭代修复。

Conclusion: 论文得出核心结论:可靠的LLM单元测试生成在实践中更依赖于系统性工程支持(显式上下文检索、稳定骨架构建、确定性后处理),而非单纯依赖提示工程。CATGen通过将LLM作为工程系统中的一个组件而非独立解决方案,实现了更高的编译鲁棒性、结构覆盖和效率。其经验对工业部署LLM测试生成具有重要指导意义,并为未来跨语言扩展和测试多样性优化提供了方向。

Abstract: Automated unit test generation has recently benefited from advances in large language models (LLMs), yet our industrial deployments reveal a persistent gap between promising research results and practical usability. In real-world projects with complex frameworks and cross-file dependencies, LLM-generated tests frequently fail to compile, require costly manual repair, or provide unstable coverage improvements. This paper reports our experience in designing, deploying, and evaluating CATGen, a context-aware workflow for LLM-based unit test generation, informed by repeated industrial failures and refinements. Rather than relying on LLMs to infer incomplete project context, we found that compilation robustness critically depends on making project-level dependencies explicit, stabilizing test class scaffolding, and replacing iterative LLM-based repair with lightweight static analysis. These experience-driven insights shaped CATGen's multi-stage design, which combines structured context retrieval, deterministic test skeleton construction, and program analysis-based post-processing. We evaluate CATGen on real-world complex focal methods from proprietary industrial projects and additionally on the Defects4J benchmark to assess generalizability. Across both settings, CATGen substantially improves compilation success and structural coverage while significantly reducing generation time and token consumption compared to existing LLM-based approaches. Our results demonstrate that reliable LLM-based unit test generation in practice depends less on prompt engineering alone and more on systematic engineering support grounded in real-world development constraints.

</details>


### [57] [Bridging Behavior and Implementation: Automated Java Glue Code Generation for Behavior-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19703)
*Xinyu Shi,Zhou Yang,An Ran Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoGlue，一种用于行为驱动开发（BDD）的层次化多智能体框架，可自动从自然语言步骤生成 Java 胶水代码。该框架将行为解释、上下文检索和代码生成分离，由 Behavior Interpreter 推断步骤意图，Developer Agent 协调 BDD 上下文检索器与项目代码检索器生成最终胶水代码。在 8 个开源 Java 项目的 1307 个步骤上评估，相比少样本提示，API F1 提升 58.7%，CodeBLEU 提升 43.7%，46.1% 的生成代码可直接使用。消融实验表明三个组件均对生成质量有显著贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDD 中胶水代码需将每个自然语言步骤手动映射到项目代码，既需理解业务意图又需熟悉代码库，是 BDD 中最劳动密集的部分。随着需求演变，步骤和胶水代码频繁修改维护成本高。先前自动化工具依赖人工规则或预定义映射，无法端到端生成胶水代码；尽管 LLM 在代码生成上表现出色，但其在胶水代码生成上的应用尚未被探索。该任务需联合推理未充分指定的行为、BDD 工件和大型项目代码库，比传统代码生成更复杂。

Method: AutoGlue 采用层次化多智能体设计，包含三个顺序阶段：(1) 行为解释——Behavior Interpreter 分析目标步骤及其所属 feature/scenario 上下文，生成描述意图的行为摘要；(2) 上下文检索——Developer Agent 监督两个子智能体，BDD Context Retriever 基于嵌入相似度检索语义相似的步骤及对应胶水代码，Project Context Retriever 探索项目代码库结构、识别相关源文件与符号树；(3) 胶水代码生成——Developer Agent 综合步骤、行为摘要与检索上下文生成最终 Java 胶水代码（含注解、方法签名、参数与方法体）。框架基于 LangGraph 实现，使用 GPT-5 mini 作为底层 LLM，并通过严格防数据泄露措施确保评估公正性。

Result: 在 8 个开源 Java 项目（metasfresh、CxFlow、PSM 等）的 1307 个 step-glue-code 对上评估：(1) 功能对齐方面，AutoGlue API F1 达 0.660，相比少样本提示（0.416）提升 58.7%，相比零样本提示（0.284）提升 132.4%；API Recall 达 0.714；(2) 代码相似度方面，CodeBLEU、METEOR、ROUGE-L 分别达 0.641、0.655、0.614，相比少样本提示分别提升 43.7%、51.3%、55.1%；(3) 实用性方面（LLM-as-a-Judge），46.1% 生成代码为 Exact Match（可直接使用），35.0% 为 Partial Match（仅需小幅修改），Mismatch 仅 18.9%；Partial Match 主要因 Missing Action（18%）和 Parameter Mismatch（10%）；(4) LLM 评判与人类评估高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.84，Cohen's κ=0.71）；(5) 消融实验表明，去除 BDD 上下文检索导致相似度指标最大降幅（ROUGE-L -24.1%），去除项目上下文检索导致 Exact Match 最大降幅（-21.4%），去除 Behavior Interpreter 导致各项指标持续下降；(6) 效率上每步约 $0.05，90% 以上在 60 秒内完成。

Conclusion: AutoGlue 是首个直接从 BDD 步骤自动生成 Java 胶水代码的方法，证实 LLM 可有效桥接自然语言行为规约与项目代码，支持规约驱动的软件开发。其层次化多智能体设计中，行为优先的工作流（先理解意图再检索上下文最后生成代码）能有效减少步骤歧义，分离异构信息推理可避免混合推理与错误传播。研究表明 BDD 可作为规约到代码自动化研究的良好起点，该范式可推广至用户故事、工单、验收标准等更广泛的敏捷工件。未来工作方向包括：从单步骤生成扩展到场景/特性级合成以保持跨步骤状态一致性；引入基于项目反馈（编译错误、测试失败等）的迭代修复机制；探索人机协同工作流；以及将框架适配至 JavaScript、Ruby、Python 等其他 BDD 生态系统。

Abstract: Behavior-Driven Development (BDD) helps technical and non-technical stakeholders share a common understanding of software requirements through natural-language scenarios. Glue code makes these scenarios executable by mapping each step to the corresponding project code. However, developing and maintaining glue code requires knowledge of both the intended behavior and the underlying codebase, making it a labor-intensive part of BDD as requirements evolve. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong code generation capabilities, their use for automated glue code generation remains unexplored. This task requires reasoning over underspecified behavior, related BDD artifacts, and large project codebases. We present AutoGlue, a hierarchical multi-agent framework for automated Java glue code generation. AutoGlue follows a behavior-first workflow that separates behavior interpretation, context retrieval, and code generation. A Behavior Interpreter derives the intent of a step from its scenario context, while a Developer agent retrieves relevant BDD artifacts and project code before generating the final glue code. We evaluate AutoGlue on 1,307 steps from eight open-source Java projects. Compared with few-shot prompting, AutoGlue improves API F1 by 58.7% and CodeBLEU by 43.7%. It produces directly usable glue code for 46.1% of the evaluated steps, while most partially correct outputs require only minor revisions, such as adding missing actions or refining parameters. Ablation results show that behavior interpretation and project-aware context retrieval both contribute substantially to generation quality. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively connect natural-language behavior specifications with project code and support specification-driven software development.

</details>


### [58] [Towards Automated Formal Verification of zkEVMs Using LLM-Guided Constraint Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19795)
*Shichen Huang,Zhenghe Jiang,Yi Jiang,Ling-I Wu,Jingyang Li,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriSynth框架，利用LLM作为形式化前端，将zkEVM的Rust源码自动合成为可执行的Python/Z3验证模型，结合SMT求解器作为最终正确性仲裁者，以自动检测zkEVM操作码实现中的语义缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: zkEVM为以太坊Rollup提供零知识证明以保证链下执行正确性，但实现层面的细微缺陷（如错误的gas计算、缺失的状态更新）可能导致有效证明认证语义错误的状态，悄无声息地破坏密码学保证。现有的SMT求解器验证受限于规范建模瓶颈：zkEVM开发缺乏将Rust操作码处理器自动翻译为验证模型的方法，当前实践依赖不可持续的手工规范，而基于LLM的方法存在幻觉且缺乏形式化保证。

Method: VeriSynth采用"LLM提议，求解器决定"的混合范式：(1)语义分解将Rust源码按操作码语义族（算术/位运算、内存/存储、gas/退款、调用/创建路径、环境/上下文）切分为独立验证单元；(2)检索增强提示从参考数据库中检索已验证示例作为语义锚点，通过代码嵌入余弦相似度与族类别加权相似度匹配top-k示例；(3)LLM引导的约束合成将源码翻译为固定宽度位向量、对称数组等符号约束，并编码frame条件防止遗漏状态更新；(4)验证引导的自动修复通过四阶段验证（解析、类型/排序、执行、语义）将编译器诊断和求解器反例作为结构化反馈迭代修复模型，按错误类别优先级应用最小化修改的修复动作。

Result: 在自构建的95个负样本基准测试上，VeriSynth检测率达91.6%（87/95），显著优于LLM-only基线（46.3%）和会话式LLM基线（61.1%）；五个操作码语义族检测率均超过85%（算术/位运算95.5%、内存/存储92.3%、gas/退款93.3%、调用/创建路径85.7%、环境/上下文90.9%）；消融实验显示自动修复贡献最大（移除后降至66.3%），检索与分解分别提升至71.6%和75.8%；相比Scroll手工编写的Rust变异测试套件（57.9%），VeriSynth多检测32个bug；平均每样本消耗约100K tokens，总计约9.5M tokens。

Conclusion: VeriSynth通过将LLM定位为形式化前端而非正确性预言机，结合SMT求解器作为最终仲裁机制，有效弥合了zkEVM源码到验证模型之间的语义鸿沟；其模块化、可检索、可修复的闭环流水线为安全关键型zkEVM实现提供了一种可扩展的自动化形式验证路径，补充而非取代现有手工测试；未来工作包括正样本误报率的系统评估、跨项目验证以及通过模型蒸馏降低成本。

Abstract: Zero-Knowledge Ethereum Virtual Machines (zkEVMs) secure Ethereum rollups by generating zero-knowledge proofs that guarantee off-chain execution correctness. However, subtle implementation bugs (e.g., incorrect gas accounting) can lead to valid proofs certifying semantically faulty states, thereby silently defeating cryptographic guarantees. Formal verification via SMT solvers can prevent this, but is bottlenecked by specification: current zkEVM development practice lacks automated methods to translate Rust opcode handlers into verification models. Current practices rely on unsustainable manual specifications, while LLM-based approaches suffer from hallucination and lack formal guarantees. To address this, we propose VeriSynth, a framework that synthesizes executable Python/Z3 verification models from Rust zkEVM code. VeriSynth enforces a hybrid paradigm: an LLM acts strictly as a formalization frontend to translate code into symbolic constraints, while an SMT solver serves as the correctness arbiter. To handle complex multi-component state transitions, VeriSynth integrates semantic decomposition, retrieval-grounded prompting, and verification-guided auto-repair into a closed-loop pipeline. We evaluate VeriSynth on the first source-level zkEVM verification benchmark, encompassing both correct and faulty opcode implementations. VeriSynth achieves a bug detection rate of over 90%, substantially outperforming direct and conversational LLM baselines, as well as a production-grade handwritten mutation-testing suite. Ablation studies confirm that each pipeline component is critical to the framework's overall effectiveness.

</details>


### [59] [Towards Reliable C-to-Rust Translation with Rule-Guided Reasoning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19966)
*Feng Luo,Jiachen Liu,Cuiyun Gao,Jia Feng,Kui Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAVEL框架,通过规则引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)和双奖励强化学习(GRPO)来提升LLM在C到Rust代码翻译中的规则合规性和语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C到Rust翻译对提升软件内存安全至关重要,但现有LLM方法存在两大局限:1)对Rust特有规则(所有权、借用、生命周期)遵循能力不足;2)难以准确捕捉复杂代码语义,导致翻译结果与原C程序行为不一致。

Method: TRAVEL包含两个核心模块:(1)规则引导推理路径搜索模块——利用MCTS在Rust规则库(如malloc→Box<T>、NULL→Option<T>、指针→引用等)指导下探索候选推理路径,结合可验证组件(自动生成测试用例)进行语义验证,生成(C, P, R)三元组训练数据;(2)双奖励强化学习优化模块——通过GRPO联合优化执行奖励(编译通过率+测试通过率)和推理质量奖励(由奖励模型评估路径质量),最终奖励为两者的加权和(λ_exec=λ_reason=0.5)。

Result: 在xCodeEval上,TRAVEL相比最强提示基线IRENE在CA上提升26.22%,CSR提升18.77%;在HW-Bench和OS-Bench上,CSR平均提升18.28%和16.51%,UR平均降低13.06%和13.08%。7B模型经TRAVEL增强后,性能可媲美GPT-4o等大型专有模型。消融实验验证了推理路径构建、RL阶段以及两种奖励的互补贡献。

Conclusion: TRAVEL通过将规则引导搜索与强化学习相结合,有效解决了LLM在C-to-Rust翻译中的规则合规性和语义保真问题,在公开基准和工业场景(华为HW-Bench、Linux内核OS-Bench)上均显著优于现有方法。未来工作将扩展到仓库级迁移和跨语言支持。

Abstract: The migration of legacy C programs to Rust has become an important direction for improving software memory safety while alleviating the high cost of manual rewriting. Leveraging large language models (LLMs) for automated C-to-Rust translation has emerged as a promising direction. However, existing LLM-based approaches remain limited. On the one hand, LLMs exhibit limited capability in identifying Rust-specific rules, and inadequate handling of Rust syntax often results in incorrect translations. On the other hand, existing LLMs often struggle to accurately capture the semantics of complex code, resulting in incorrect translations. To address these challenges, we propose a Translation fRAmework Via rule-guided reasoning and rEinforcement Learning, namely TRAVEL, consisting of two modules. The first module employs Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based reasoning path construction guided by Rust-specific rules, steering the search toward translation steps that respect the syntactic rules that LLMs frequently violate. The second module introduces reinforcement learning that couples execution feedback with reasoning-quality signals, encouraging the model to construct reasoning paths that accurately capture program semantics, thereby ensuring that the generated Rust code preserves the intended behavior of the original C program. We evaluate TRAVEL on three datasets: xCodeEval (a public benchmark), OS-Bench (functions collected from the Linux kernel), and HW-Bench (an industrial dataset from Huawei). On xCodeEval, TRAVEL outperforms all baselines across three backbone LLMs. In particular, compared to the strongest prompting baseline IRENE, TRAVEL improves computational accuracy (CA) by 26.22% and compilation success rate (CSR) by 18.77%. On HW-Bench and OS-Bench, TRAVEL further improves CSR by 18.28% and 16.51%, respectively, while reducing unsafe rate (UR) by 13.06% and 13.08%, respectively.

</details>


### [60] [SequenceFI: Non-intrusive Temporal Fault Injection for Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20050)
*Yuzhen Tan,Jian Wang,Bing Li,Shaolin Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SequenceFI是一个非侵入式的微服务系统时间故障注入框架，通过sidecar代理在通信层拦截消息事件、传播紧凑的时间证据，并基于trace合成occurrence-sensitive的After时间守卫，实现精确的时间窗口触发，无需修改应用代码或序列化库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有请求级故障注入技术只能控制故障注入的位置（where）和类型（what），但无法控制何时（when）触发，这使得难以复现时间敏感的故障场景，如副作用后的失败、顺序敏感的并发响应、以及重复下游调用中的部分故障。此外，序列化层注入方式受限于多语言异构性（170个序列化库跨9种语言），且TFIC配置空间随时间事件数指数级增长（3事件守卫可达4.04M候选）。

Method: 方法包含四个组件：1) TFIProxy sidecar拦截消息级send/receive事件；2) 通过x-fic-evidence header传播紧凑的二进制时间证据向量；3) TFIC生成器将静态目标选择（基于FastFI/LDFI请求lineage）与时间守卫生成分离，使用minimum hitting set算法从trace中合成After守卫；4) 通过Kubernetes mutating webhook自动注入sidecar。时间守卫形式为After(conjunction of occurrence-count atoms)，覆盖post-effect、order-sensitive、k-of-n三种模式。

Result: 在四个微服务基准（Online Boutique、Hotel Reservation、Sock Shop、Train Ticket）的9个时间故障场景和450次有效试验中，SequenceFI达到100.0%时间成功率（无premature或multiple注入），平均1次尝试即可找到有效配置，比H-Random减少95.91%端到端搜索时间；运行时开销仅损失2.7%吞吐量，sidecar CPU和内存仅为Istio Envoy的41.5%和2.3%。

Conclusion: SequenceFI通过将interposition point放在通信层而非应用代码或序列化库中，实现了实用的云原生时间故障注入；其trace-guided的TFIC生成算法避免了穷举枚举；未来工作将扩展更丰富的时间操作符和协议支持。

Abstract: Fault injection is widely used to evaluate the resilience of microservice systems, where client requests often span multiple services and execution stages. Existing request-level techniques usually control where and what faults are injected, but not when they are activated within a distributed execution. This limitation makes it difficult to reproduce timing-dependent failures, such as failures after state-changing side effects, order-sensitive concurrent responses, and partial failures among repeated downstream calls. This paper presents SequenceFI, a non-intrusive framework for temporal fault injection in microservice systems. SequenceFI observes message-level send and receive events, propagates compact temporal evidence along request executions, and triggers faults only when occurrence-sensitive temporal guards are satisfied. It further synthesizes temporal guards from traces, reducing the need for exhaustive enumeration of temporal fault-injection configurations, while requiring no modifications to application code or serialization libraries. We implement SequenceFI on Kubernetes and evaluate it on four widely used microservice benchmarks. Across nine temporal-fault scenarios and 450 valid trials, SequenceFI achieves 100.0\% temporal success without premature or multiple injections, finds effective configurations in one attempt on average, and reduces aggregate end-to-end search time by 95.91\% compared with H-Random.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-stage Dynamic Selection for Cross-Project Defect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20151)
*Juscimara G. Avelino,Juscelino S. A. Junior,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-DES框架，将多分类器系统(MCS)选择与动态选择(DS)相结合，采用两阶段选择策略（项目级配置选择+模块级实例选择），并通过聚合秩最小化(ARM)策略进行多指标综合选优，在不使用目标项目信息的严格跨项目缺陷预测(CPDP)场景下，于82个项目、4个基准数据集上验证其性能优于现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件缺陷预测(SDP)受限于目标项目标注数据稀缺，因此引入跨项目缺陷预测(CPDP)，但CPDP面临训练项目与目标项目之间的分布偏移(distinct software characteristics)问题。已有方法多采用全局建模(global modeling)视角，对整个目标项目施加同一模型/集成，未能捕捉特征空间中不同区域(local regions)的差异；同时部分方法依赖目标项目信息进行数据变换或实例加权，存在数据泄露风险且难以适用于全新项目。

Method: 1) 提出Multi-DES两阶段框架：第一阶段在项目级评估多种MCS配置组合(基础分类器×动态选择技术×池规模)的笛卡尔积(共4×8×10=320种配置)，利用Bagging构造分类器池；第二阶段在测试时按模块级(实例级)动态选择最胜任的分类器子集。
2) 提出聚合秩最小化(ARM)策略：对每个评价指标(F1、AUC、False Alarm)分别排序后计算平均秩，选平均秩最低的配置，以兼顾多指标泛化能力，避免单指标过拟合。
3) 采用8种动态选择技术(KNU、KNE、KNOP、META-DES、OLA、LCA、MCB、Rank)与4种基分类器(DT、LR、NB、RF)，遵循Herbold等的严格CPDP分类，全程不接触目标项目分布。
4) 留一项目法(leave-one-project-out)评估，82个项目跨4个数据集(PROMISE、RELINK、NASA、AEEEM)，采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验。

Result: 在82个项目的4个CPDP基准数据集上：①配置分析表明RF作为基分类器被选中69/82次，LCA/OLA/META-DES为最常选DS技术，池规模无明显偏好；②ARM策略在多数情况下优于或匹配单指标选择策略(p<0.05)；③与CC09-DT、EASC-NB、M11-RF、T09-DT、W08-DT等基线对比，Multi-DES在AUC指标上取得70+次胜利(最大82)，False Alarm指标也大幅领先；F1指标在AEEEM和RELINK上最优，整体胜率超过70%；④AEEEM数据集上Multi-DES在所有三个指标上均取得最优，且从不是最差方法；⑤不利用任何目标项目信息即可达到或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: Multi-DES为严格CPDP场景下提供了一种不依赖目标项目分布的多阶段动态集成选择方案，通过ARM多指标选优和实例级动态选择增强了对分布偏移的鲁棒性；未来工作将聚焦于通过配置预测(configuration prediction)降低计算开销、扩展至在线场景，并探索在更广泛缺陷预测设置中的应用。

Abstract: Cross-Project Defect Prediction (CPDP) involves building models using data from external projects, called training projects, to predict modules from the target project. However, traditional CPDP methods suffer from the distribution shift between training and target projects that affects the model's performance. This paper proposes a novel CPDP framework that addresses this issue by proposing a two-stage multiple classifier system (MCS) selection scheme: one working at the project level and another at the module level. In the first stage, the framework evaluates multiple possible MCS configurations to find one that covers and generalizes well across multiple training projects. Consequently, the proposal is likely to obtain a diverse set of classifiers, each specialized in tackling software modules with distinct characteristics. The second selection stage operates at test time, selecting the most competent classifiers to predict each new module in the target project. Unlike previous approaches that apply the same classifiers to the entire target project, the proposed framework performs module-level model selection. This way, the system is more robust to changes in distributions between training and target projects because the selected set of classifiers is module-dependent. Our experimental results using 82 projects from four different CPDP benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms the state-of-the-art CPDP methods in most scenarios. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [62] [How Developers Use Relation Chains in Gerrit-Based Review Ecosystems: An Empirical Study Across Three Open-Source Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20189)
*Ahmed Belhouchette,Moataz Chouchen,Marouene Chaieb,Mohammad Hamdaqa Abdelwahab Hamou-Lhadj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Gerrit代码评审平台中"关系链"(relation chain, 即通过parent-SHA依赖链接的变更序列)进行了跨三个开源生态系统(OpenStack、Wikimedia、ONAP)、15个仓库、涵盖401,256个变更和29,580条关系链的实证研究,揭示了依赖型评审工作流作为持久协调单元的采用模式、评审开销及链内结构特征,主张评审分析应从变更级转向链级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工程任务(重构、迁移、多组件特性)促使开发者将工作分解为相互依赖的"堆叠变更"序列,在Gerrit中形成"关系链"。尽管这类依赖结构日益普遍,现有代码评审研究多以孤立变更作为分析单元,缺乏对关系链作为一等评审制品的实证刻画:依赖图演化、基础变更对后继变更的影响传导、合并顺序约束、链位置的差异化开销等结构特性均被忽视,导致评审分析、评审者推荐与AI辅助工具在生态层面系统性低估了协调成本。

Method: 研究分三阶段:(1)数据抽取——通过Gerrit REST API五个通道获取15个仓库的变更元数据、补丁集修订与父SHA、文件级diff、评审消息(区分人类与CI机器人)与评审标签;(2)链检测与指标计算——按Gerrit惯例通过补丁集级parent-SHA匹配链接变更,为每个成员标记base/middle/top/solo位置,计算6个变更级指标(修订次数、rebase次数、评审时长、讨论消息、内联评论、CI/CD任务)和4个链级指标(深度、提交间隔、合并间隔、结构演化),按Gerrit尺寸分桶控制规模混淆;(3)统计分析——采用非参数方法:Mann-Kendall趋势检验配Sen's斜率评估链采用的时间趋势,Mann-Whitney U检验配Cliff's δ进行链vs单独变更对比,Spearman ρ分析基础变更-后继变更的依赖关联。

Result: (1)关系链采用广泛且增长:跨项目占比5%-49%(排除ops/puppet异常后中位数17%),15个项目中14个呈显著单调趋势(10个上升、4个下降,主要在ONAP);深度中位数多为2,以成对结构为主,深度尖峰对应协调工程活动(如pylint清理)。(2)链带来变更级不可见的开销:链成员合并时间中位数为同等大小单独变更的2.6×,开销集中于超大变更(Cliff's δ=0.49);中间成员承担最高修订与rebase工作量,充当同步瓶颈。(3)CI放大效应:严格门禁项目(OpenStack)链成员每个位置运行10-23个CI/CD作业,而单独变更少于2个。(4)基础效应:基础变更评审活动与后继变更均值正相关,评审时长ρ=0.61、讨论消息ρ=0.60、修订次数ρ=0.43,在14-15/15项目中显著。(5)动态依赖重构:33.5%链成员在合并前经历parent-SHA结构性变化(OpenStack 25.0%、Wikimedia 39.7%、ONAP 50.2%),最长合并间隔达2.85年。

Conclusion: 关系链是持久、生态塑造的协调单元,其内部结构(链位置、基础变更活动、结构演化)与独立变更分析无法观察的评审动力学差异显著。本研究主张评审分析、评审者分配系统及AI辅助评审工具应转向链级推理。核心实践启示:基础效应表明将评审资源集中投入基础变更可惠及全部后继成员;中间成员的同步瓶颈特性支持链感知的评审者优先级排序与CI调度;链的动态重构(1/3成员在评审中改parent)要求工具在每次补丁集上传时重新计算依赖拓扑而非假定结构稳定。未来工作应在非Gerrit平台(Phabricator、Graphite、GitHub堆叠PR)上复现,并评估针对性干预(基础评审检查点、链感知评审者分配、依赖图感知CI调度)的实际效益。

Abstract: Background. Developers increasingly coordinate dependent review workflows by submitting sequences of related changes rather than monolithic ones. In Gerrit, these dependencies form relation chains: structured review units that link changes together. As chains become more common, they shape review activities through synchronization overhead, CI amplification, and merge-ordering constraints. Aim. We investigate how developers adopt relation chains and how these dependency structures influence review dynamics and outcomes. Method. We analyze 29,580 relation chains from 15 repositories across three Gerrit ecosystems (OpenStack, Wikimedia, and ONAP), comprising 401,256 changes, using Mann-Kendall trend tests, Mann-Whitney tests with Cliff's delta for chain-vs-solo comparisons, and Spearman correlations for base-descendant dependencies. Results. Chain prevalence ranges from 5% to 49% across projects, increasing in 14 of 15. Chain changes take a median of 2.6 times longer to merge than size-matched solo changes, with the gap widening for very large changes. Review effort propagates through dependency-linked review workflows: base-change review activity co-varies with descendant review activity (rho = 0.43-0.61 in 14-15 of 15 projects), and 33.5% of chain members undergo structural evolution during review. Conclusions. Relation chains operate as durable, ecosystem-shaped coordination units with internal structure that change-centric analyses cannot capture. Future review analytics, reviewer-assignment systems, and AI-assisted review tools should reason over chains rather than isolated changes.

</details>


### [63] [Multi-Source and Cross-Scenario Strategy-Guided Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20353)
*Yuwei Zhao,Qianyu Xiao,Ye Cui,Yijun Yu,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MoST框架，通过引入"证据对象"统一表征多源异构知识（如优化commit、教科书、手册等），并通过自平衡加权聚类和跨场景示例迁移，生成目标场景下的Semgrep静态分析规则来引导LLM进行代码优化。在351个历史优化任务和15个真实项目上的实验表明，MoST相比现有SOTA方法SemOpt在优化准确性和实际性能提升方面均有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略引导（strategy-guided）代码优化方法（如SemOpt）存在两个核心局限：(1)策略库仅依赖历史优化commit这一单一知识源，无法利用教科书、手册、网页等其他来源的优化知识；(2)策略形式化仅针对源commit所属的场景（如特定编程语言和CPU架构），无法跨场景复用，导致低资源语言（如Rust）策略覆盖不足。这些限制使LLM难以稳定、准确地进行自动化代码优化。

Method: MoST是一个基于LLM的代码优化框架，包含四个阶段：(1) 证据对象构建——将不同知识源（commit、文档等）的数据项统一表征为 e = ⟨P, E, T, y⟩，包含自然语言描述、修改前后代码示例、场景标签和来源类型；(2) 自平衡加权策略聚类——采用加权密度聚类，并通过最小化场景级策略计数偏差的自平衡准则选择文档权重、相似度阈值和最小簇大小三个超参数（选定值为3、0.76、5）；(3) 目标场景规则生成与验证——采用示例迁移程序将其他场景的示例转移到目标场景，并通过对规则匹配修改前后代码的功能验证保证规则质量；(4) 优化器——使用Semgrep规则扫描目标代码并提示LLM生成优化补丁。共构建了48,440个commit来源证据对象（27,463 C、10,073 Python、7,098 Java、3,806 Rust）和189个文档来源证据对象。

Result: 在历史优化任务复现实验（151个C/C++、150个Python、50个Rust任务）上，MoST相比SemOpt在EM指标上提升24.44%–180.00%，在SemEqv指标上提升21.88%–37.50%；相比Direct Prompt提升480%–1300%，相比RAG提升93.94%–250%。在15个真实项目优化实验上，MoST在所有项目的最大性能提升和14个项目的平均性能提升上均排名第一，最大性能提升范围19.72%–717.42%，平均性能提升范围4.44%–258.17%。消融实验显示，去除加权聚类使成功率降低8.97%–10.71%，去除可靠规则生成使成功率降低43.59%–50.00%。文档信息出现在21.93%的有效优化结果中（C/C++中达45.73%），跨场景迁移候选规则占55.47%的有效结果。MoST的C/C++优化建议中89.70%在实际应用中可接受。

Conclusion: MoST通过整合多源知识（commit+文档）和跨场景策略迁移，有效扩展了LLM代码优化的策略覆盖范围。实验证明：(1) 自平衡加权聚类能有效平衡不同频率和质量的知识源；(2) 示例迁移加功能验证的规则生成策略对跨场景策略复用至关重要；(3) 多源策略构建和可靠的规则生成能够互补单一历史commit策略库的覆盖局限。这为策略引导的自动化代码优化提供了新的范式，特别有利于数据稀缺语言（如Rust）的优化。

Abstract: Automated code optimization improves program performance by refactoring source code, and recent studies use LLMs to generate optimization patches. The newest approaches are strategy-guided: they summarize strategies from historical optimization commits as static analysis rules, and use these rules to match code locations for LLMs to optimize. However, these approaches have two limitations: (1) the strategies may come from other knowledge sources, such as textbooks and web pages, but the existing approaches cannot utilize them; (2) a strategy may be applicable to different scenarios, e.g., different programming languages, but existing approaches can only formalize strategies for the scenario to which the source commit belongs. To address these limitations, we propose MoST, an LLM-based code optimization framework that integrates multiple knowledge sources across scenarios. MoST uniformly represents items in different knowledge sources as evidence objects, clusters them in a cross-source and cross-scenario manner to identify strategies, and transfers them to the target scenario when necessary for generating static analysis rules. To implement this process, MoST employs a novel self-balanced weighted clustering algorithm to balance evidence objects from different knowledge sources, and a novel example transfer procedure to ensure the quality of the generated rules when transferring across scenarios. On a benchmark containing 151 C/C++, 150 Python, and 50 Rust historical optimization tasks, compared with SemOpt, MoST yields 24.44%-180.00% and 21.88%-37.50% more patches that are exactly the same as or semantically equivalent to developer patches, respectively. When optimizing 15 real-world projects, MoST achieves 19.72%-717.42% maximum improvements and 4.44%-258.17% average improvements for the performance tests in the projects, significantly outperforming SemOpt and Codex.

</details>


### [64] [The ICSE 2026 Shadow PC: Training the Next Generation of Reviewers Through Deliberate Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20396)
*Christian Kästner,Michael Hilton,Jonathan Bell,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ICSE 2026 Shadow PC项目，基于"刻意练习"原则重新设计的多阶段评审员培训计划。该项目接收了所有合格的申请者（185人申请，183人参与），其中102人完成了全部流程并评审了117篇论文，97%的参与者推荐该体验，67%的作者认为评审意见有帮助。项目创新性地引入校准阶段、同行评审评审、Zoom同步讨论、与主PC的严格隔离，并提出了影子PC领域主席机制以实现可持续扩展和领导力培养。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域的同行评审至关重要，但评审员培训仍主要依赖隐性学习——早期研究者通过接收质量参差不齐的评审意见或偶尔的指导来学习。传统影子PC项目存在两大局限：（1）名额限制（如ICSE 2025仅录取50/299申请者）；（2）培训浅层化，仅提供初始指导而缺乏基于"刻意练习"的系统性反馈。随着投稿量增长，急需建立培养合格评审员和领域主席的明确路径。

Method: 项目基于六大循证教学原则设计多阶段程序：（1）先尝试后指导（generation before instruction）；（2）对比案例与校准（contrasting cases）；（3）反馈配以修改（feedback paired with revision）；（4）纠正误解（undoing misconceptions）；（5）以教促学（learning by teaching）；（6）归属感与社会学习（belonging and social learning）。具体执行包含七个阶段：HotCRP账户设置（1周）、培训与校准（评审2篇植入缺陷的种子论文，3周）、同步Zoom会议（2个时区）、正式评审（3篇论文，3周）、同行评审评审（1周）、终稿评审（2周）、讨论与撰写元评审（2周），以及ICSE评审对比的反思阶段。严格设置利益冲突防火墙，影子PC意见不影响主PC决策。

Result: 项目取得显著成效：102/183参与者完成全部流程（淘汰81人，主要因未完成校准阶段）；共评审117篇真实ICSE投稿；参与者调查显示97%推荐该体验，94%认为整体有用，76%收到有用反馈；35份参与者问卷和24份作者反馈表明校准有效；决策对齐分析显示影子PC可靠识别薄弱论文（68%与ICSE拒稿决策一致），但对录用较为保守（仅2/16影子录用与ICSE一致），且建议大修频率约为ICSE的两倍；23人获颁"杰出影子PC评审员"奖，多人被邀请加入ICSE 2027、ASE 2027、FSE 2028主PC。

Conclusion: ICSE 2026 Shadow PC证明基于循证教学原则的刻意练习能够规模化地提升评审员培训质量。项目的主要挑战在于讨论阶段参与度下降和组织者负担过重，4位主席管理100多位评审员难以为继。为此提出影子PC领域主席机制（每位管理10-30篇论文），建立清晰的领导力发展路径：影子PC评审员→影子PC领域主席→影子PC主席/主PC领域主席。建议将影子PC制度化推广至ICSE、FSE、ASE，建立共享操作手册、可复用校准材料和跨会议协调机制。

Abstract: Peer review is essential to software engineering research, yet reviewer training remains largely implicit. We describe the ICSE 2026 Shadow PC, a redesigned program emphasizing deliberate practice at scale. Key innovations include multi-phase structure with calibration and peer feedback, strict separation from the main PC, and a pathway toward leadership development. With 102 participants completing the program and reviewing 117 papers, 97% recommending the experience, and positive reception from authors (67% finding reviews helpful), the program demonstrates that rigorous reviewer training is achievable at scale. We share lessons learned and propose shadow PC area chairs as a mechanism for sustainable scaling and leadership development.

</details>

### [65] [Near-Optimal Dimension Lower Bounds for Single-Vector Embeddings of Maximum Inner Product Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20393)
*Rajesh Jayaram,Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对单向量嵌入近似最大内积相似度（MAX-IP）和 Chamfer 相似度的维度下界问题，将先前 $1/\varepsilon$ 的指数上界改进到接近最优的 $1/\varepsilon^2$，几乎弥合了与 MUVERA 上界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索（如 ColBERT）通过点云和 Chamfer 相似度进行细粒度匹配，但计算昂贵。MUVERA 将多向量压缩为单向量的维度上界为 $m^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$，而此前最优下界仅 $(\varepsilon^2 m)^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon)}$，指数上存在 $1/\varepsilon$ 与 $1/\varepsilon^2$ 的差距。本文旨在近乎闭合此差距，确立 MAX-IP 单向量近似的真正维度复杂度。

Method: 核心方法结合 Sherstov 的模式矩阵方法（pattern matrix method）与一类新型布尔函数：对任意 $\delta\in(0,1)$，使用多项式大小、常数宽度 $W$ 的 DNF 公式，其近似度（approximate degree）为 $\Omega(k^{1-\delta})$。通过统一宽度填充（uniform-width padding）和分块编码（block encoding）构造查询向量与文档点云，使所有"真"输入产生相同的最大内积值 $v_1$，所有"假"输入经虚拟坐标（dummy coordinate）调整后统一为 $\eta$，从而将 MAX-IP 矩阵实现为 DNF 模式矩阵的精确仿射像，间隙至少 $8\varepsilon$。再由近似秩下界得出维度要求。

Result: 主定理（Theorem 1.1）：对任意 $\delta\in(0,1)$，存在常数使得对于充分小的 $\varepsilon$ 和 $m\ge(1/\varepsilon)^{A_\delta}$，存在单位查询向量与至多 $m$ 个单位向量的文档点云，使得任何单向量 $\varepsilon$-近似所有 MAX-IP 值的维度必满足 $D\ge m^{c_\delta/\varepsilon^{2-2\delta}}$。由于 $\delta$ 可任意小，指数趋近于 MUVERA 的 $1/\varepsilon^2$。该结果对数据相关（data-dependent）表示和 Chamfer 相似度同样成立（因查询为单例）。

Conclusion: 本文将近似最大内积相似度的单向量嵌入维度下界从 $(\varepsilon^2 m)^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon)}$ 提升至 $m^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon^{2-2\delta})}$，几乎匹配 MUVERA 的上界 $m^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$，基本解决了这一基础问题。技术贡献在于将高近似度常宽度 DNF 精确实现为单位球上的两值 MAX-IP 矩阵，结合虚拟坐标均衡假输入。值得注意的是，证明最初由 Google 内部基于 Gemini 的智能体系统自动生成，作者进行了验证与编辑。开放问题：点云阈值 $A_\delta$ 是否可降低至 $m\gtrsim 1/\varepsilon^2$ 的最优量级。

Abstract: Multi-vector embeddings represent items by point clouds and compare query and document point clouds using Chamfer similarity, whereas single-vector embeddings use ordinary inner products. For singleton queries, Chamfer becomes maximum inner product similarity (MAX-IP). In our setting, MUVERA gives dimension $m^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$~\cite{dhulipala2024muvera}, whereas the previous lower bound $(\varepsilon^2m)^{\Omega(1/\varepsilon)}$~\cite{jayaram2026expressive} left a gap between $1/\varepsilon$ and $1/\varepsilon^2$ in the exponent of $m$. We nearly close this gap. For every fixed $\delta\in(0,1)$, there are constants $A_\delta,c_\delta>0$ such that, for all sufficiently small $\varepsilon>0$ and every $m\ge(1/\varepsilon)^{A_\delta}$, there exist unit query vectors and document point clouds of at most $m$ unit vectors for which every single-vector approximation of all pairwise MAX-IP values to additive error $\varepsilon$ has dimension \[ D \ge m^{c_\delta/\varepsilon^{2-2\delta}}. \] This holds even for fully data-dependent representations chosen after seeing the dataset. It also applies to Chamfer because all queries are singletons. Since $\delta$ can be arbitrarily small, the exponent approaches the $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ dependence of the upper bound. The proof combines Sherstov's pattern matrix method with polynomial-size, constant-width DNF formulas computing functions of approximate degree $\Omega(k^{1-\delta})$. Uniform-width padding and a block encoding create an $\Omega(\varepsilon)$ gap. A dummy coordinate then equalizes all false inputs, yielding a unit-sphere MAX-IP matrix that is an exact two-valued affine image of the DNF pattern matrix with gap at least $8\varepsilon$. This allows the approximate-rank bound to apply.

</details>


### [66] [Packing Linear Programs and Fractional Knapsack using Comparison Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19557)
*Ritabrata Barat,Siddharth Barman,Nirjhar Das,Sukruta Midigeshi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究比较预言机模型下分数背包和打包线性规划的目标恢复问题。核心结论：(1) 给出 O(n log(1/δ)+B²) 查询复杂度的多项式时间算法；(2) 证明 Ω(n log(1/δ)) 的下界；(3) 揭示分数背包在比较预言机模型下与一般打包LP本质等价；(4) 将结果扩展到利润最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆优化和显示偏好框架假设可直接观测最优解，但在许多实际场景（如离散选择调查、偏好反馈学习、RLHF）中只能获得最优解之间的序数比较信息。垄断定价场景中，卖家需推断买家对可分商品的未知估值，仅能获得不同价格下需求量的相对比较。该研究旨在解决这一更困难的序数信息下的目标恢复问题。

Method: 核心方法为三阶段多项式时间算法：阶段1（算法1，O(n)查询）在全1价格向量下识别积分装入集S、唯一分数装入项f和未装入集U，利用单坐标扰动检测物品所属类别；阶段2（算法2，O(n log(1/δ))查询）通过逐步降低未装入物品价格，检测其值价比超过分数项的临界点，结合最简中间有理数算法精确恢复值比；阶段3（算法3，O(B log(1/δ)+B²)查询）针对最复杂的积分装入物品恢复，核心技术贡献是提出"三元组价格"（triad prices）族——参数化标量α设计的价格向量，使预言机响应在α跨越值比阈值v̂/v_s̄时呈现锐利转变，从而支持二分搜索。关键引理14证明三元组项在α≤α*_s̄时被装入而在α>α*_s̄时不被装入，相关命题16刻画预言机对三元组价格的响应。下界通过Index问题的一轮通信归约证明。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 分数背包（KCO问题）算法：查询复杂度 O(n log(1/δ)+B²)，多项式时间运行（定理1）；(2) 查询下界 Ω(n log(1/δ))（定理25，Index问题归约）；上下界差距至多 O(n/log n) 因子，当 B=o(√(n log(1/δ))) 时本质紧致；(3) 打包LP（PLCO问题）：利用关键洞察——通过将约束矩阵某一行作为价格向量、其余行为零——将分数背包算法归约到打包问题，得到上界 O(n log(1/δ)+min b_i²)，同样适用 Ω(n log(1/δ)) 下界；(4) 利润最大化（比较预言机类比的[Amin et al. AAAI 2015]）：O(n log(1/δ)+B²) 查询达到任意ε加性近似最优利润（定理28）；(5) 附录B证明容量上界 B≤(n-1)(min v_i/max v_j - δ²/n²) 的条件本质必要。

Conclusion: 本文证明尽管仅能通过比较查询观测最优解的序数信息，仍能以查询高效的方式恢复打包线性规划（特别是分数背包）的潜在目标函数。技术上，三元组价格的精巧设计是将比较预言机响应转化为值比阈值检测的关键工具。结果凸显了序数信息在线性优化问题中的强大能力，并为偏好反馈学习和显示偏好理论提供了新的算法框架。未来方向包括一般线性规划和凸优化问题扩展、自适应性研究以及噪声预言机模型分析。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the objective of a packing linear program when the algorithm accesses only comparison information about optimal solutions under varying constraint matrices. Motivated by optimization with comparison oracles (Cohen-Addad et al., STOC 2026) and preference feedback (Kaufmann et al., TMLR 2025), this strengthens inverse-optimization frameworks by replacing direct observations of optimal solutions with ordinal queries. We focus on the fractional knapsack problem, where the packing linear program (LP) has a single budget constraint specified by item prices, and the objective is determined by item values. This captures monopoly-pricing where a seller infers a buyer's unknown valuations for divisible items from comparison information. The algorithm queries an oracle with two price vectors, returning which optimal solution has the larger total packing or objective value. Such oracles abstract discrete-choice surveys of buyers choosing between differently priced alternatives. For fractional knapsack, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm recovering item values up to scale using $O(n \log(1/\delta)+B^2)$ comparison queries, where $n$ is the number of items, $B$ is the knapsack capacity, and $\delta$ is the value grid resolution. We complement this with an $\Omega(n \log(1/\delta))$ lower bound. A key insight is that in the comparison-oracle model, fractional knapsack is as general as packing LPs. Our algorithm solves the packing setting by treating a constraint matrix row as the price vector and zeroing the rest. The $\Omega(n \log(1/\delta))$ lower bound continues to hold for packing LPs, making our upper bound essentially best possible, up to a linear-factor gap. Finally, we extend our algorithm to profit-maximization, yielding a comparison-oracle analogue of the revealed-preference result of Amin et al. (AAAI 2015).

</details>


### [67] [An \(O(\log n)\)-Approximation for Three-Terminal Reachability-Preserving Minimum Edge Cut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20114)
*Qi Duan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对三终端可达性保持最小边割问题（RPMEC），提出了一种多项式时间的 O(log n)-近似算法，显著改进了之前的 O(√n)-近似结果。算法的核心创新在于将分解树的根团簇扩展为原图中的连通分量，并构建节点加权辅助图上的最短路径来恢复连通性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RPMEC 问题要求在分离终端 t 与 s₁、s₂ 的同时，保持 s₁ 与 s₂ 之间的连通性，这一连通性约束使其区别于普通最小割问题。已有结果仅能给出 O(√n)-近似，需要新的技术思路来实现对数级近似。关键难点在于：分解树的连通团簇在原图中可能诱导出非连通子集，使得树上的连通解无法直接转化为图上的可行解。

Method: 算法基于 Räcke 的概率"割支配"分解树框架及其容量映射解释：构造一个多项式规模的分解树分布，每个支撑树支配所有图割，且每个固定图割的期望树割容量至多为 O(log n)·OPT。对于每棵以 t 叶为根的分解树 T：将每个根团簇 Cₑ 扩展为 G[Cₑ] 的所有连通分量 K，每个分量对应一个权重为 c_G(δ_G(K)) 的辅助节点；构建节点加权辅助图 H_T，通过节点相邻（分量相交或被图边连接）关系建立边；用节点分裂+Dijkstra 算法求 s₁ 到 s₂ 的最小节点加权 a-b 路径 Q_T，对应的顶点并 W_T 即为候选源侧。核心结构引理（分量预算引理）：∑_{K∈𝓚ₑ} c_G(δ_G(K)) = c_G(δ_G(Cₑ)) ≤ wₑ，保证所有从同一树边派生的分量总边界代价可控。路径比较引理将辅助路径代价与树中分离 P⋆ 与 t 的最小树割容量进行上界比较。最终算法枚举分布中的所有树并返回最优候选。

Result: 主要定理（定理 1/17）：加权无向三终端 RPMEC 存在多项式时间 O(log n)-近似算法。其证明结合：1）分量预算引理确保连通性扩展不增加代价；2）路径比较引理将候选解代价上界界定为对最优 s₁-s₂ 路径 P⋆ 的树分离代价 ϕ_T(P⋆)；3）期望分布失真保证 ∑pᵢλ_Tᵢ(U⋆) ≤ ρ·OPT。零代价边通过商图预处理的扰动处理（c_ε）吸收为常数因子。算法运行时间对每棵多项式规模的树为多项式，枚举所有支撑树后取最小代价候选解，从而确定性输出 O(log n)-OPT 的可行割。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 RPMEC 的首个 O(log n)-近似，超越了之前的 O(√n) 障碍。分量扩展原理——将树连通团簇替换为其图连通分量——是核心技术贡献，可推广用于其他"保留侧需满足内部连通性"的割问题，例如多保护终端情形（可能达到多项式对数级近似）。然而该方法本质上依赖无向割与无向分解树，无法直接推广到有向 RPMEC。算法实现方面，实际近似或采样分解树的经验质量是有意义的实验问题。

Abstract: In the three-terminal Reachability-Preserving Minimum Edge Cut problem, the input is an undirected edge-weighted graph with terminals \(s_1,s_2,t\). The objective is to delete a minimum-cost set of edges that separates \(t\) from both \(s_1\) and \(s_2\), while preserving connectivity between \(s_1\) and \(s_2\). We give a polynomial-time \(O(\log n)\)-approximation algorithm. The algorithm uses a probabilistic distribution of cut-dominating decomposition trees. A direct transfer of a connected tree solution to the original graph is not valid because a connected tree cluster may induce a disconnected vertex set in the graph. We overcome this obstruction by expanding every rooted tree cluster into the connected components it induces in the original graph. These components form a node-weighted auxiliary graph. A minimum node-weighted path in this auxiliary graph produces a connected feasible source side. The main structural observation is that the total graph-boundary cost of all connected components of a rooted tree cluster is no greater than the capacity of the corresponding tree edge. This permits the auxiliary path to be compared with a tree cut separating an optimal preserved \(s_1\)-\(s_2\) path from \(t\). Combining this comparison with the expected \(O(\log n)\) cut distortion of the decomposition trees proves the approximation guarantee.

</details>


### [68] [Simple and Almost Non-Adaptive \(\frac{1}{2}\)-Approximation for Matroid Prophet Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20269)
*Sina Kalantarzadeh,Kanstantin Pashkovich*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对拟阵约束下的先知不等式问题，提出了首个"几乎非自适应"的 1/2 近似算法。该算法通过将原始拟阵分解为若干"nice"子拟阵的直和，并为每个部分预计算单一非自适应阈值，同时达到了最优的 1/2 近似比和完全的固定阈值特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先知不等式是不确定性下在线决策的基础模型。在拟阵约束下，Kleinberg-Weinberg 算法的 1/2 近似虽然是最优的，但其阈值依赖于已接受的元素（自适应），这在固定价格机制等应用中难以实现且不适合多维买家。Feldman-Svensson-Zenklusen 给出了几乎非自适应的 1/4 近似，但相较最优保证损失了常数倍。对于一般拟阵，纯非自适应机制在原始拟阵上的近似比只能是 Ω(log n / log log n)。因此，核心问题是：能否在固定阈值的限制下达到最优的 1/2 近似比？

Method: 算法包含预处理和在线两阶段。预处理阶段：(1) 求解 ex-ante 松弛得到拟阵多面体内的最优解 x，将一般实例归约为 Bernoulli 实例 (M, x, v)；(2) 通过迭代"提取过程"将原始拟阵 M 分解为 M₁' ⊕ … ⊕ M_ℓ'（其中 M_i' 是 M 收缩后的限制），每个 M_i' 对应的 Bernoulli 实例满足"nice"性质——即对所有非空子集 S 有 T(S) ≤ T(U)，其中 T(S) = w(S)/(r(S) + x(S))；(3) 为每个 nice 部分预计算单一阈值 T_i。在线阶段：对到达的元素 e ∈ U_j，若 e 激活、v_e ≥ T_j 且保持 M_j' 中的独立性则接受。所有阈值和额外约束均在到达前确定。关键技巧在于利用 nice 性质得到局部下界 E[v(I_i)] ≥ r_{M_i'}(U_i) · T_i，并通过求和与单调递减的阈值序列、非负的累积 D_k ≥ 0（来自 x ∈ P_M）配合"分部求和"证明全局下界 Σ r_i T_i ≥ (1/2)Σ x_e v_e。

Result: 定理 3.5 证明算法的接受集 I 满足 E[v(I)] ≥ (1/2) Σ x_e v_e。由于 ex-ante 松弛值是 prophet 期望收益的上界，因此算法实现了相对于 prophet 基准的 1/2 近似，同时相对于更强意义上的 ex-ante 松弛值也成立。可实现性方面，给定独立集谕示机，整个提取过程可在多项式时间内完成（含子模极小化与参数搜索）；分解涉及的迭代次数不超过 n = |U|。这是首个对一般拟阵达到最优 1/2 近似比且使用完全固定阈值的算法，与 Kleinberg-Weinberg 自适应算法相比避免了 #P-难精确计算问题。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了拟阵先知不等式中自适应算法与非自适应算法之间的长期差距，证明了通过将原拟阵替换为更严格的子拟阵（直和形式），可以在不损失常数倍的前提下使用固定阈值达到最优 1/2 近似。结果结合了自适应算法的最优近似保证与非自适应算法的简单性，对公示价格机制、多维买家等场景具有重要意义。剩余开放问题包括：在不改变拟阵结构的纯非自适应意义下，哪些拟阵类（如 graphic、cographic、laminar 等）能支持常数近似。

Abstract: Prophet inequalities are a fundamental model for online decision-making under uncertainty. For matroid constraints, Kleinberg and Weinberg gave a tight $1/2$-approximation, but their algorithm uses adaptive thresholds that depend on the previously accepted elements. Specifically, the mechanism of Kleinberg and Weinberg accepts an arriving element if and only if it is feasible to add with respect to the matroid constraint and the value of the arriving element passes its threshold; but this threshold depends on the elements accepted so far and on the arriving element itself. Later, Feldman, Svensson, and Zenklusen showed that one can give a $1/4$-approximation for general matroids. Their algorithm is almost non-adaptive, i.e., it uses non-adaptive thresholds but changes the underlying matroid to another ``stricter'' matroid. Feldman, Svensson, and Zenklusen also showed that no constant approximation is possible in general matroids using non-adaptive thresholds if one does not change the underlying matroid. We give a new almost non-adaptive algorithm for matroid prophet inequalities that achieves a $1/2$ guarantee. We change the underlying matroid to a ``stricter'' new matroid that is a direct sum of several matroids. For each part of the new matroid, we precompute a single non-adaptive threshold. Once the elements start to arrive, we accept an arriving element as long as it is feasible with respect to the new ``stricter'' matroid and its value passes the precomputed threshold. In addition, we guarantee that our algorithm achieves the $1/2$ guarantee not simply with respect to the prophet's expected gain, but with respect to the stronger ex-ante relaxation value. Thus, we provide the first almost non-adaptive algorithm for the matroid prophet inequality that achieves the best-possible approximation guarantee of $1/2$.

</details>


### [69] [Distributed Colouring with 4/3 chi Colours for Hyperbolic Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20360)
*Kostas Lakis,Johannes Lengler,Adeline Pittet*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲随机图（HRG）上的分布式顶点着色问题，提出了一种新的顺序径向着色（Sequential Radial Colouring）算法，该算法在CONGEST模型下以接近最优的调色板对HRG进行着色，运行时间为O((log log n)²)轮，使用4χ/3种颜色。论文还分析了关于团（clique）上随机化着色协议的理论结果，给出了颜色数量与运行时间之间的精细权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分布式着色算法分析主要针对最坏情况的一般图，但实际网络通常具有几何或空间结构特征。HRG作为一种能够捕捉现实网络关键结构特征的几何随机图模型，为分析超越最坏情况的分布式算法提供了自然场景。然而，在HRG上进行分布式着色的研究仍存在空白，本文旨在填补这一空白，并改进现有的常轮数着色算法的颜色数量。

Method: 1. **顺序径向着色算法（Sequential Radial Colouring）**：一种CONGEST算法，仅使用高效的局部计算。该算法利用HRG的几何结构，特别是其径向（径向坐标）特性，对顶点进行着色。2. **理论分析**：包括两个主要部分：（a）对HRG上的算法进行分析，证明其性能保证；（b）对经典随机化着色协议在团（clique）上的分析，改进关于附加松弛量s = |Ψ| - χ与所需轮数之间关系的先前结果。3. **变体算法**：在保持基本思想的前提下，通过增加颜色数量换取运行时间的减少。

Result: 1. 算法以4χ/3种颜色对HRG进行着色，并在O((log log n)²)轮内完成（a.a.s.）。2. 一种变体算法使用O(χ log log n)种颜色，将运行时间缩短至O(log log n)轮（a.a.s.）。3. 对于任意常数ε > 0，使用χ^(1+ε)种颜色时，算法可在O(1)轮内完成（a.a.s.），这比Maus和Ruff（SODA 2026）的先前常轮数算法的颜色数量至少减少了n^(1/6)倍。4. 对于团C上的着色，当附加松弛量s = |Ψ| - χ时：仅当s = |C|^(1+Ω(1))时才能实现常轮数着色；而s = |C|/log |C|已经能够给出最优的Θ(log log |C|)轮复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文通过研究HRG上的分布式顶点着色问题，证明了利用几何结构可以获得接近最优的着色性能。所提出的顺序径向着色算法在颜色数量和运行时间之间提供了多种权衡选择，显著改进了现有结果。此外，对团上随机化着色协议的分析也为更广泛的分布式着色理论做出了贡献，特别是关于颜色松弛量与轮复杂度之间的精细关系。这些结果对理解和设计针对具有几何结构的真实网络的分布式算法具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study distributed vertex colouring on Hyperbolic Random Graphs (HRGs), a geometric random graph model capturing key structural features of real-world networks. This provides a natural setting for analysing distributed algorithms beyond worst-case general graphs. We introduce Sequential Radial Colouring, a CONGEST algorithm using only efficient local computation. The algorithm achieves a near-optimal palette, colouring HRGs with $\frac{4}{3}\chi$ colours and running in $O((\log\log n)^2)$ rounds a.a.s. We also give a variant that speeds this up to $O(\log\log n)$ rounds a.a.s., at the price of using $O(\chi\log\log n)$ colours. Finally, for every constant $\varepsilon>0$, it runs in $O(1)$ rounds a.a.s. when $\chi^{1+\varepsilon}$ colours are used. This greatly reduces the number of colours over the previous constant-round algorithm of Maus and Ruff (SODA 2026) by a factor of at least $n^{1/6}$. Our analysis contains a phase in which we consider a classical randomised colouring protocol on a (large) clique of the graph. We also delve deeper into this part of the analysis and improve upon previous results for colouring a clique $C$, bounding the number of rounds required as a function of the additive slack $s = |\Psi| - \chi$, where $\Psi$ is the set of colours used. In particular, constant-round colouring is possible if and only if $s=|C|^{1+\Omega(1)}$, while $s=|C|/\log |C|$ already gives the optimal $\Theta(\log\log |C|)$ round complexity.

</details>


### [70] [Pure-DP Statistical Query Release at the Conjectured Square-Root Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20418)
*Jack Fitzsimons*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了差分隐私领域中一个长期悬而未决的开问题：Nikolov与Ullman提出的纯差分隐私（Pure-DP）统计查询释放的平方根速率猜想。论文证明了对任意数据库大小n和隐私参数ε，存在纯ε-DP机制，其期望最坏坐标误差为O(min{1, √(log(2T)log(2k)/(εn))})，匹配已知下界在标准高维情形下的依赖关系，同时无需额外参数假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询释放问题（Query Release）在差分隐私（DP）框架下，长期存在近似DP（approximate DP, δ>0）与纯DP（pure DP, δ=0）之间的根本性差距：在近似DP下，私有乘法权重（PMW）和迭代数据库构造可达到n^(-1/2)型精度；但在纯DP下，经典方法仅能给出最坏坐标误差的立方根上界O((log T · log k / (εn))^(1/3))。Nikolov和Ullman在DifferentialPrivacy.org上将其列为第1号开放问题，并猜想纯DP下能达到与下界匹配的平方根速率log k · log T / (εn)。技术障碍在于：PMW转录本仅具有有限阶隐私损失矩，适用于集中DP和近似DP，但纯DP要求逐点的似然比不等式；对邻近数据集联合定界会因Hamming球内数据库数量指数级增长而失败。

Method: 机制构造分三步：(1) 仅含选择信息的PMW转录本——从均匀合成分布出发，反复用指数机制选择带符号查询并更新合成分布，转录本仅记录查询索引和符号；(2) 用"距离惩罚似然包络"替换转录本的概率质量函数：对每个可能的转录本，取其在所有数据库下的似然按Hamming距离指数折扣的最大值，归一化后得到满足纯DP的逐点似然比界；(3) 公开确定性解码——从转录本重放整个合成轨迹，输出平均合成分布的查询答案。效用分析的核心技术包括：(a) Maurey引理在似然层面的应用，用(T²+1)^m规模的辅助PMW律族逐点上界Hamming球内的最大似然；(b) 邻近球用整数阶Rényi矩控制和Hölder不等式传递L₂误差界；(c) 远距球用max≤∑混合界控制；(d) 按Θ(1/ε)宽度分块求和，避免逐径求和带来的额外1/ε因子。参数选择为J=Θ(log T/α²)、η=Θ(log k/α)、γ=Θ(α)以平衡Hedge遗憾与选择精度。

Result: 主要定理（Theorem 1.1）：存在通用常数C>0，使得对任意有限数据域D（|D|=T≥1）、任意有限查询族Q（|Q|=k≥1，值域[-1,1]）、任意n≥1和ε>0，存在纯ε-DP机制M，使得 sup_x E‖M(x)-F(x)‖_∞ ≤ C·min{1, √(log(2T)log(2k)/(εn))}。该上界在标准高维情形下匹配已知下界[√(log k · log(T/n)/(εn))的Hardt下界和Lyu-Talwar下界]的标度依赖。具体显式常数C=2²⁰e。机制具有有限输出支撑，是纯信息论的（转录本空间指数级大）。伴随的Lean 4形式化开发机器验证了有限构造、纯隐私性（确定性解码后）以及全参数范围的显示上界。

Conclusion: 论文完整解决了Nikolov-Ullman猜想（DP开放问题第1号），证明了纯DP查询释放在期望最坏坐标误差意义下的猜想上界。核心贡献是"似然包络"技术——将PMW转录本的有用局部行为转化为全局纯DP律，既不付出数据库数量代价，也不产生额外1/ε因子。分析方法上的创新包括：直接对PMW似然使用Maurey经验方法而非对查询并集定界；对邻近/远距Hamming球分别使用矩界与混合界；在隐私尺度Θ(1/ε)上分块径向求和。局限性：(1) 机制是信息论的，不具有多项式时间实现；(2) 给定单次运行的包络分析未建立最优置信依赖——通过重复运行取中值可获得O((log(1/ζ))^(1/2))的附加因子；(3) 在退化参数范围（单元素域T=1或单查询k=1）虽然最小值确保正确，但不声称这些情形下的匹配下界。寻找同一普适率下的高效纯DP机制仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Nikolov and Ullman asked whether k statistical queries on a universe of size T can be released under pure differential privacy with expected worst-coordinate error at the square-root rate suggested by known lower bounds. We prove their conjectured upper bound. For every database size n and privacy parameter $\varepsilon>0$, there is an $\varepsilon$-differentially private mechanism with expected error $O(\min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2T)\log(2k)/(\varepsilon n)}\})$. This matches the lower-bound dependence in the standard high-dimensional regimes where those bounds apply; the shifted logarithms and outer minimum make the upper bound valid without additional parameter assumptions. The construction starts from a selection-only private multiplicative weights transcript, then replaces its probability mass function by a distance-penalized likelihood envelope. To prove that the modification preserves accuracy, a likelihood-level Maurey argument upper-bounds each Hamming-ball maximum by a small family of auxiliary PMW laws. Renyi moment bounds control nearby balls, a direct mixture bound controls distant balls, and grouping radii at the privacy scale prevents an additional $1/\varepsilon$ factor in the error. The mechanism is information-theoretic. A companion Lean 4 development machine-checks the finite construction, pure privacy after deterministic decoding, and the displayed all-regimes upper bound.

</details>

### [71] [Fine-grained Computation-Communication Overlap via Tile-level Signaling and Scheduling for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19539)
*Minyu Cui,Anna Wingkvist,Morgan Ericsson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于tile级信号与调度的细粒度计算-通信重叠方法,用于加速分布式MoE推理中expert计算与第二次all-to-all返回通信的重叠执行。在4×A100平台上,端到端加速比最高达2.64倍,MoE层加速比最高达2.74倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MoE推理中,expert计算后的第二次all-to-all返回通信(将expert输出送回源rank)通常在expert计算完成后才启动,导致通信延迟暴露在关键路径上,GPU利用率低。现有方案要么采用粗粒度分解流水线(小chunk下tensor core效率受限,且存在chunk间同步开销),要么采用侵入式kernel融合(需要定制屏障、跨rank原子协议、针对目标kernel的特殊化,工程复杂度高),无法实现细粒度的tile级重叠。

Method: 提出生产者-消费者协同设计:(1)Remote-Owner-Aligned行布局,将每个expert输入按目标owner rank分组排序并在tile边界填充零行,使每个输出tile映射到单一目的rank;(2)Remote-first tile调度,远程行优先调度、远程行多的expert优先处理,以最大化通信重叠窗口;(3)单个rank范围的持久GEMM核(producer)覆盖所有本地expert,通过CUTLASS epilogue中的信号机制发布tile完成标志;(4)独立持久通信核(consumer)运行在独立的SM分区上,通过NVSHMEM以segment粒度(一个或多个行带)轮询并发起远程写;(5)将分区间通信和计算分配到CUDA流优先级和SM分区中,producer/consumer SM比例可调。两核完全通过device-resident信号协调,关键路径无host介入。

Result: 在4×A100单节点(节点内NVLink)上,针对三个MoE模型(M-GPT、M-BERT、M-Trans-xl)与四个SoA系统(FasterMoE、Tutel、Megatron-CUTLASS、Megatron-TE)对比,在M-GPT和M-BERT上全面优于所有基线,E2E加速比1.04×–1.66×,MoE层加速比最高2.74×(M-Trans-xl相对FasterMoE)。操作符级峰值加速比约2.94×–3.01×,重叠率71.9%–99.9%。敏感性研究表明consumer CTA在10–20区间最优,过少会因背压导致性能崩溃(cCTA=2下退至1.91×baseline)。正确性方面:1440次检查全部通过,最大相对误差1.913×10⁻³,处于FP16舍入噪声范围内。

Conclusion: 通过tile级信号与调度实现细粒度计算-通信重叠,producer/consumer双持久核在分离SM分区上协调,无需修改底层计算算子或通信原语,实用性强。未来工作包括扩展至反向传播训练、自适应SM分区选择、支持更大规模集群部署。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity without proportionally increasing computation cost and have become a key building block for scaling large language models (LLMs) to trillion-parameter regimes. Efficient deployment of these MoE models relies on distributed execution across multiple GPUs, where each MoE layer involves two all-to-all communications: dispatching tokens to expert ranks and returning the expert outputs to their source ranks. Conventional MoE implementations launch this return all-to-all after expert compute completes, exposing communication latency on the critical path and reducing GPU utilization. We present a fine-grained approach that overlaps expert compute with the second all-to-all via tile-level signaling and scheduling. Our producer-consumer co-design combines: (1) a persistent per-rank computation kernel (producer) that covers all local experts on the rank to eliminate repeated kernel launch overhead and prioritizes remote-critical tiles, and (2) a persistent communication kernel (consumer) on a small dedicated partition of streaming multiprocessors (SMs) that issues segment-granular transfers as tiles become ready. Our co-design avoids intrusive changes to the underlying computation operators or communication primitives, making it practical for improving distributed MoE execution efficiency on multi-GPU systems. On a 4-A100 GPU platform, evaluated on three MoE models against four state-of-the-art MoE systems, our approach achieves up to 2.64x end-to-end speedup and 2.74x MoE-layer speedup. Compared with a conventional non-overlap baseline, our approach consistently improves both operator- and MoE-layer-level performance across varying GEMM shapes, router modes, and a broad range of producer/consumer SM partitions, while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [72] [Odin: Primitive-Level Synchronization for Distributed Point-Based Neural Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19893)
*Zhenxiang Ma,Zeyu He,Yuanzhen Zhou,Zhenyu Yang,Yuchang Zhang,Miao Tao,Rong Fu,Jidong Zhai,Hengjie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Odin 是一个针对点基神经渲染（PBNR，如3DGS）的分布式训练系统，通过将全局任务/迭代级同步屏障替换为**primitive级（基元级）同步**来消除关键路径上的同步开销。系统基于相对局部性图（RLG）做前瞻调度，配合运行时发布校验与 Shadow Graph 暂存机制，在不修改渲染核、优化器、训练预算或模型容量的前提下，实现平均 1.22× 加速，并在 MatrixCity 64 GPU 上对 Grendel 取得最高 1.89× 加速，同时保持重建质量在 ±1% 报告带内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBNR 存在"同步边界错配"问题：1) 渲染器不断优化（3DGS→TamingGS→DashGS），通信占比从 22.9% 升至 32.3%，同步而非计算逐渐成为关键路径；2) 视图只读/更新稀疏的、与视图相关的 primitive 子集，但分布式系统仍用全局任务或迭代级屏障同步，使视图被迫等待其观察范围之外的 primitive 更新；3) 现有分布式系统（Gaian、Grendel 等）只优化"在哪里运行"（放置、分区、通信量），而不优化"何时让 primitive 更新对后续视图可见"；4) 通用分布式深度学习调度器工作在层/张量/阶段边界，而 PBNR 没有深层结构，依赖冲突在 primitive-index 粒度，低于现有抽象层级。

Method: Odin 采用"预测-校验"原则，分三阶段设计：(1) **图构建**——构建相对局部性图 RLG（基于 SfM 轨迹的 Jaccard 相似度作边权重），与阶段任务图结合；(2) **图调度（AOT）**——逻辑分区（LP）将 RLG 分成 K 组 → 静态数据调度（SDS）在组间轮转并按低耦合分填充任务单元 → 静态异步调度（SAS）将候选重叠窗口标在阶段任务图上；(3) **图执行（运行时）**——Shadow Graph 给每个飞行中单元一个虚拟逻辑视图（共享物理状态、两槽环形缓冲暂存梯度），质量优先路径在状态读取前校验 WAW/RAW/WAR primitive-scope 冲突，吞吐优先路径额外通过延迟作用域比 ρ 和生产者梯度门控 |g_A(p)| ≤ τ·mean，只放行小、低影响的 RAW 延迟读；失败预测降级为 primitive 级等待而非回滚；DDS 在线调度、DAS 在观察前再校验重叠窗口。

Result: 在 4 个 PBNR 流水线（3DGS、2DGS、TamingGS、DashGS）和 13 个非城市 8-GPU 场景上平均加速 1.22×；在 MatrixCity 单区域混合并行下 32 GPU 加速 1.27×、64 GPU 加速 1.89×（vs Grendel）；隐藏 82% 的关键路径等待时间；在 τ=0.2、K=4 默认设置下，PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS 归一化聚合差值均在 ±1% 报告带内；Shadow Graph 比全逻辑状态复制减少高达 257× 操作开销且额外内存基本持平；50,000 张图像的 AOT 调度开销仅 9.45 秒；稠密场景（如 kitchen）质量优先路径降级至 1.00×（98.6% 重叠被拒）。

Conclusion: Odin 证明全局屏障并非分布式 PBNR 的固有属性；primitive 级发布 + 静态局部性规划 + 运行时校验 + Shadow Graph 暂存的协同设计，可在不改变渲染核、优化器、训练预算和模型容量的前提下显著提升吞吐且保持重建质量。该机制面向更广泛的工作负载类别——具有读/写作用域、发布点与延迟精炼证据的显式可变状态系统，可推广至在线重建、神经建图、object/voxel/world-model 等稀疏显式态 AI 系统。

Abstract: Point-based neural rendering (PBNR) represents 3D scenes as explicit, trainable primitives and underpins high-quality reconstruction and emerging embodied AI and world-model pipelines. Unlike layer-structured neural networks, PBNR has primitive-indexed dependencies: each view reads and updates only a sparse, view-dependent subset of mutable scene state. As large scenes require distributed training and optimized renderers reduce per-view computation, global task- or iteration-level barriers increasingly place synchronization, rather than rendering, on the critical path. We present Odin, a distributed PBNR training system that replaces global barriers with primitive-level synchronization. Its ahead-of-time scheduler uses stable locality and phase order to identify low-conflict overlap windows, while the runtime validates primitive publication before later work observes mutable state. Odin provides a quality-first path that preserves synchronized-training visibility and a throughput-first path that uses overlap and gradient evidence to admit only small, low-impact delayed reads; structural changes and high-impact cases remain synchronized. Across four existing PBNR pipelines and 13 non-city scenes on 8 GPUs, Odin improves throughput by 1.22 times on average and hides 82% of critical-path wait while preserving reconstruction quality. In a MatrixCity mixed-parallel case study scaling to 64 GPUs, Odin improves throughput over Grendel by up to 1.89 times without changing renderer kernels, optimizers, training budgets, or model capacity.

</details>


### [73] [Ascend to Science: Exploration of AI Chips for Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20120)
*Weicheng Xue,Kai Yang,Yongxiang Liu,Baisong Xu,Dengdong Fan,Xianglin Liu,Pengxiang Xu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以华为昇腾910系列NPU为代表性张量中心架构，系统研究AI加速器支持科学计算负载的可行性。通过内核特征化与五个应用研究（HPL-MxP、LRSVD、SGEMM-cube、PQSim、SMC-X），提出弥合执行失配、精度缺口与数据移动缺口的方法，并验证AI原生NPU在数值稳健性、性能与可扩展性方面可作为有效的科学计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI硬件（如TPU、NPU、Tensor Core GPU）以低精度张量引擎为核心进行优化，与科学计算对FP32/FP64数值稳健性、不规则访存和可扩展性的需求存在结构性矛盾。随着旗舰系统日趋AI化，HPC社区面临的关键问题是：在何种条件下AI原生硬件可有效承载科学计算负载？

Method: 1. 识别三大根本差距：执行失配（AI加速器擅长稠密规则内核，科学负载常受内存带宽与归约限制）、精度缺口（FP16/BF16 vs FP32/FP64）、数据移动缺口（多级数据复用与显式层次管理）。2. 基于DaV架构Cube/Vector单元与软件管理存储层次（UB/L0/L1/L2/HBM），结合Bisheng C++编程接口。3. 五类应用分别针对不同映射范式：HPL-MxP采用CPU-NPU异构+通信计算重叠；LRSVD采用阶段敏感异构放置+迭代正交化精化；SGEMM-cube采用尾数拆分FP32仿真+缓存流水线；PQSim采用门融合+UB块化；SMC-X采用双晶格+掩码向量化+UB预取，将不规则MC转换为批量规则执行。4. 内核roofline特征化与代际对比（910A/B/C），对比NVIDIA A800/H800。

Result: 1. 内核层面：hgemm在910C上达~324 TFLOPS（FP16峰值85.5%），sgemm-hf32达~132 TFLOPS（HF32峰值69.7%），而sgemv/ssymv仅0.7-1.5 TFLOPS，呈现代际收益不对称。2. HPL-MxP在910A/B/C集群上呈近似线性扩展，通信开销占主导时通过多级流水线有效隐藏。3. LRSVD在N=10,000时较Intel MKL CPU与cuSOLVER GPU加速约一个数量级，低秩比r/N<0.2区间优势显著。4. SGEMM-cube达到~60 TFLOPS稳定吞吐（约75%峰值），相对误差10^-7匹配FP32标准，并避免大尺寸下厂商sgemm性能骤降。5. PQSim门融合带来约2×加速，910C上30qubit/30层RCS电路11.4秒完成，超A800+cuQuantum 25.4%。6. SMC-NPU（4卡910C，10亿原子）达1.81×10^7 atom·steps/s/芯片，逼近16卡H800的SMC-GPU水平；cal_energy内核经向量化→循环/数据移动重构→低层API优化，时间从83.4秒降至0.015秒/位点。

Conclusion: AI原生NPU可在数值稳健性、竞争性能与可扩展性方面满足科学计算需求，但前提是数值重构、执行放置与数据移动三者协同优化。可迁移的优化原则包括：异构阶段划分、非常规执行向规则化重构、显式层次化数据编排、通信-计算重叠；而Cube/Vector具体分割、UB/L0/L1存储组织、CANN/HCCS软件栈相关洞见则为昇腾专属。未来工作应拓展稀疏/FFT/通用stencil内核，并在编译运行时层面提供混合精度、分块与通信调度的辅助支持，降低专家驱动调优成本。

Abstract: The rapid rise of AI-oriented accelerators has reshaped compute systems around low-precision tensor engines, raising a practical question for the HPC community: under what conditions can such hardware support scientific workloads that demand numerical robustness, irregular memory access, and scalability? Using the Ascend 910 NPU series as a representative tensor-centric platform, we characterize precision, execution, and memory-hierarchy bottlenecks that hinder the direct deployment of scientific codes. We then develop and evaluate workload-specific mappings across five application studies -- HPL-MxP, LRSVD, SGEMM-cube, PQSim, and SMC-X -- combining heterogeneous execution, mixed-precision numerical formulations, precision emulation, hierarchical memory orchestration, and communication--computation overlap. These studies show that AI-native NPUs can achieve numerical robustness, competitive performance, and satisfactory scalability when numerical formulation, execution placement, and data movement are addressed in a coordinated manner. Our results provide a state-of-the-practice case study of how scientific workloads can be adapted to tensor-centric architectures, while distinguishing transferable optimization principles from Ascend-specific implementation details.

</details>


### [74] [Fully Dynamic Rooted Spanning Tree on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20211)
*Abhijeet Sahu,Harmit Singh,Soham Nandy,G. Ramakrishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对GPU架构提出了四种全动态并行算法，用于在批量边插入或删除后高效更新有根生成森林（而非从头重建）。算法结合Supergraph（SG）与Hooking-Shortcutting（HS）两种策略来寻找有向替换边，并结合Broadcasting（BC）与Euler Tour（ET）两种策略进行路径反转，共形成SG-ET、SG-BC、HS-ET、HS-BC四种算法。在NVIDIA A100 GPU上，吞吐量达每秒200万次插入和140万次删除，相比现有GPU并行静态BFS算法最高获得900倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界网络（如社交媒体、交通网络等）持续发生边增删的"churn"现象。生成树是网络维护、路由、双连通分量（BCC）和最小生成树（MST）等关键算法的基础子程序。现有方法在图更新时通常从头重建生成树，效率低下；而现有动态算法要么仅提供理论保证缺乏实用部署，要么针对多核CPU设计不适合GPU的细粒度并行模型。此外，并行动态生成树维护算法的研究在该领域仍属空白。因此亟需为GPU设计的、具有理论保证的、高效的全动态并行算法。

Method: 问题被分解为两个子问题：(1) 在断开的树之间寻找**有向替换边**（Oriented Replacement Edges），通过两类方法解决——Supergraph（SG，构建代表顶点构成的超级图后运行pr-rst）和Hooking-Shortcutting（HS）；(2) **路径反转**（Path Reversal），通过两类方法解决——Broadcasting（BC，使用special ancestor数组）和Euler Tour（ET，使用Eulerian Tour的start/finish时间戳判断路径上的顶点）。两两组合形成四种完整算法。复杂度分析：SG-ET/SG-BC的插入/删除深度为O(log n + log²k)；HS-ET/HS-BC深度为O(log²n)；所有算法工作量均为O(n log n)（插入）或O(E + n log n)（删除）。基于Euler Tour的路径反转还具有O(n)的线性空间优势。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100 GPU上，使用13个真实/合成数据集（边数从1000万到10亿）进行评估：(1) 相比静态GPU BFS，删除操作最高加速530倍（平均160倍），插入操作最高加速900倍（平均200倍）；(2) 相比移植到GPU的多核pr-rst算法，删除平均加速13倍（最高30倍），插入平均加速18倍（最高41倍）；(3) 在不同批量大小（100至100000）下表现稳定，平均吞吐量为每秒200万次插入和140万次删除；(4) 实验分析表明：HS性能取决于顶点数n（删除时n≪m时优），SG性能取决于关键边数m（插入时m≪n时优），ET路径反转比BC快2–3倍且空间效率更高。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在GPU上以增量修复而非重建的方式维护有根生成森林是高效可行的策略。所提出的四种算法为该领域首个GPU全动态并行实现，兼具理论保证和实用性能。提出的子程序（如GPU并行路径反转）具有独立价值，可应用于动态连通分量等更广泛问题。未来工作将扩展到全动态连通性、Euler Tour Tree的批量并行更新以及多GPU扩展。

Abstract: Spanning trees are fundamental structures in graph theory, essential for various applications such as network maintenance, routing adjustments, and many more. The dynamic nature of real-world networks requires efficient updates to these structures as the underlying graph evolves. Maintaining rooted spanning trees dynamically is particularly crucial for algorithms addressing 2-connected components and minimum-weighted spanning trees. In this paper, we address the challenge of maintaining a rooted spanning forest when a batch of edges are inserted or deleted. We present four novel fully dynamic parallel algorithms to update the spanning forest without reconstructing it from scratch. To the best of our knowledge, parallel algorithms for this problem remain largely unexplored. Our experiments on a diverse collection of real-world graphs using a GPU environment demonstrate a throughput of 2 million insertions and 1.4 million deletions per second, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art parallel static algorithms.

</details>
