Motivated by these challenges, we study local causal structure learning in the presence of latent variables and selection bias. Specifically, we first characterize a local region that enables target-specific causal discovery without recovering the entire global structure. We then establish a theoretical bridge between causal information learned from the observed distribution induced on this local region and the corresponding information in the global causal structure. Building on these foundations, we propose LoCaLS, a local causal structure learning algorithm that is sound and complete under standard assumptions and identifies the same direct causes and effects of a target variable as those identifiable by global causal discovery methods, while allowing for latent variables and selection bias. Extensive experiments on random and real-world structures demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves higher structural accuracy than existing local methods while requiring substantially less computational effort than state-of-the-art global methods. Furthermore, applications to two real-world gene expression datasets reveal biologically plausible target-specific causal structures, demonstrating its practical applicability in large-scale biological data analysis.

</details>


### [151] [Nonlinear Bias-Compensated Adaptive Filter and Its Application for Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19902)
*Yi Peng,Haiquan Zhao,Jinhui Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的随机傅里叶偏置补偿广义自适应滤波算法(RFFBCGA)，通过结合随机傅里叶特征(RFF)与偏置补偿(BC)框架，并引入广义自适应(GA)函数作为代价函数，有效解决了现有BCKLMS算法在非线性EIV模型中存在的固定字典表征能力不足及对非高斯噪声鲁棒性差两大局限。该算法在保持固定网络结构的同时，显著提升了对含噪输入信号的建模能力与对各类噪声环境的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性自适应滤波算法大多仅考虑输出噪声，而忽略了实际应用中普遍存在的输入噪声干扰问题（即非线性误差变量模型）。近期提出的偏置补偿核最小均方(BCKLMS)算法虽首次解决了该问题，但仍存在两个关键局限：(1) 采用固定大小字典虽限制了网络增长，却也阻碍了对输入信号特性的全面捕获；(2) 基于LMS（二阶矩误差）的代价函数使其在输出端存在非高斯噪声时鲁棒性严重退化。因此，亟需一种能同时应对输入噪声干扰与非高斯输出噪声、且具备更强输入信号表征能力的非线性自适应滤波方法。

Method: 1. **框架构建**：在随机傅里叶特征空间(RFFs)内构建RFFBC框架，通过Bochner定理将平移不变核的核函数估计转化为有限维随机映射，并采用余弦映射形式G(uᵢ)=√(2/D)cos(wᵀuᵢ+θ)，保持固定网络维度D；2. **代价函数设计**：提出新颖的偏置补偿广义自适应代价函数J_GA，将GA函数J_GA(e,l,δ)与偏置补偿(BC)项耦合，其中BC项为γ(σᵢⁿ⁴/4)(Σ∂²ϑ̂/∂uₘ²)²，用于补偿输入噪声引起的偏差；3. **GA函数灵活性**：通过调节形状参数δ，GA函数可分别退化为LMS(δ→2)、LMLS(δ→0)、MCC(δ→-∞)等经典代价函数，赋予算法对不同噪声环境的自适应能力；4. **理论分析**：推导并证明了两个关键定理(Theorem 1 & 2)，证明Rᵢ=E[G(uᵢ)G(uᵢ)ᵀ]=(1/D)I_{D×D}无论输入是否含噪均成立，极大简化了理论推导；在此基础上完成了均值与均方稳定性分析，分别推导出步长上界μ_GA<4Dξ⁴l²/[2Q^hξ⁴-γσᵢⁿ⁴l²M(M+2)]及更严格的均方稳定条件。

Result: 1. **Example I (M=1)**：在高斯、伯努利-高斯(BG)与α稳定分布噪声环境下，RFFBCGA相比BCKLMS、KLMS、RFFMCC、KMCC均表现出更快的收敛速度与更低的稳态MSE，尤其在非高斯噪声下优势显著；2. **Example II (M=2)**：在多维输入情况下，RFF-based算法普遍快于核方法，RFFBCGA仍保持最优性能；3. **太阳黑子时间序列预测**：对1749年1月至2025年1月的月度太阳黑子数据进行含噪训练集预测，KLMS与BCKLMS因字典随机选取而性能波动明显，RFFBCGA在不同SNR(5dB/10dB)下均表现最优；4. **蔡氏电路混沌时间序列预测**：基于物理蔡氏电路生成的混沌序列，RFFBCGA在不同噪声水平下稳定优于对比算法；5. **定理验证**：通过1000次独立蒙特卡洛仿真验证了Theorem 1与Theorem 2的正确性，确认输入噪声不影响Rᵢ与R̄ᵢ的值。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出RFFBCGA算法，克服了现有BCKLMS算法在非线性EIV模型中字典表征受限与鲁棒性不足的双重局限。该算法通过RFF映射维持固定网络结构，通过BC项有效抑制输入噪声干扰，通过GA函数灵活适配多种噪声环境。理论上的均值与均方稳定性分析以及在两组真实世界时间序列预测任务中的实验结果，均验证了所提算法相对于现有竞争方法的优越性，为非线性误差变量模型下的自适应滤波提供了一种兼具高效性与鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Most existing nonlinear adaptive filtering algorithms only account for output noise, neglecting the fact that input noise is also prevalent in practice. Although the recently proposed bias-compensated kernel least mean square (BCKLMS) algorithm addresses input noise in the nonlinear errors-in-variables (EIV) model, it still suffers from two major limitations. First, the use of a fixed-size dictionary restricts network growth but also prevents it from fully capturing the characteristics of the input signal. Second, as an least mean square (LMS) based algorithm, it exhibits poor robustness in the presence of non-Gaussian noise in the output signal. To overcome these issues, this paper proposes the random Fourier bias-compensated filter under general adaptive function (RFFBCGA) algorithm. Within the random Fourier feature based bias-compensated (RFFBC) framework, the proposed algorithm not only maintains a fixed network structure and effectively mitigates input noise interference through the BC term, but also achieves improved characterization of the input signal. Moreover, by leveraging the flexible form of the general adaptive (GA) function, the algorithm's robustness across various noise scenarios is further enhanced. Extensive simulations, including real-world time series prediction tasks, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [152] [Good Practice Guide for quantifying uncertainties for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19999)
*P. Harris,C. Bench,M. Rinkevičius,V. Marozas,L. Coquelin,A. Thompson,M. Nandi,U. Hackstein,P.J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于QUMPHY项目的实践指南，介绍了针对光电容积脉搏波(PPG)信号应用中机器学习模型的不确定性量化的最佳实践方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可穿戴设备中PPG信号的广泛应用，机器学习模型被用于处理这些信号，但模型预测的不确定性量化问题在医疗等领域至关重要。该指南旨在为研究人员和从业者提供关于机器学习模型选择、不确定性量化方法实施和结果验证的实用指导。

Method: 该指南涵盖了三方面内容：(1) 比较不同机器学习模型在回归和分类任务上的性能；(2) 实施模型依赖和模型独立的不确定性量化方法；(3) 提供六个基准问题及其相关数据集的指引。此外还包含辅助实施的软件工具介绍和伦理问题的简要讨论。

Result: 该指南作为一个综合性的实践参考文档，系统性地总结了针对PPG信号的机器学习应用中的模型选择、不确定性量化和验证方法，并配有相应的软件工具和基准数据集支持。

Conclusion: 该实践指南为PPG信号分析中的机器学习应用提供了全面的方法论指导和实用建议，强调了在医疗应用中不确定性量化的重要性，并给出了具体的实施建议和推荐方法。

Abstract: This Good Practice Guide presents work done in the QUMPHY project (Uncertainty quantification for machine learning models applied to photoplethysmography signals) that considered both machine learning and uncertainty quantification for problems which used photoplethysmography (PPG) signals from wearable devices as input. It provides high-level guidance on what types of machine learning model might be used and how different models compare when applied to both regression and classification tasks. It provides guidance on the implementation of different methods for uncertainty quantification, covering both model-dependent and model-independent techniques, and on the validation of the results provided by those methods. It also describes six benchmark problems together with pointers to different benchmark datasets for each problem. Software is described that can assist practitioners in implementing the methods described herein and there is a brief consideration of ethical issues. It concludes with a summary and recommendations.

</details>


### [153] [Generalized Kalman filter based temporal difference reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20010)
*Vasos Arnaoutis,Eric Lutters,Bojana Rosić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件期望理论的广义时序差分（TD）强化学习框架（GMKF-TD），将值函数和动作值函数视为不确定量，建模为随机推断问题。该方法将经典基于Kalman的TD学习从线性-高斯假设扩展到非线性、非高斯系统，并通过多项式混沌展开（PCE）或集合近似来递归估计值函数的条件期望和二阶矩，实现了完整的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典基于Kalman滤波的TD强化学习依赖线性-高斯假设，仅能估计条件均值，无法有效处理广泛存在的非线性、非高斯随机系统。现有方法缺乏对值函数学习过程中的不确定性量化，且学习率等超参数依赖经验调参。本文旨在建立融合条件期望投影理论与贝叶斯推断的统一框架，扩展TD学习到更广泛的随机动力学系统，并提供理论严谨的不确定性传播机制。

Method: 1. 在L²概率空间中将值函数建模为随机变量，将条件期望解释为正交投影；2. 基于Doob-Dynkin引理和Bellman方程推导贝尔曼信息条件期望，建立更新方程；3. 推导GMKF-TD框架，分别给出均值更新（创新项形式）和协方差更新方程（通过构造保证正半定性的映射）；4. 使用线性参数化和随机游走模型处理非平稳环境；5. 采用集合采样或PCE离散化随机参数；6. 扩展至动作值函数（GMKF-TD-SARSA和GMKF-TD Q-learning）；7. 引入基于后验分布的探索策略（Thompson采样、UCB）。

Result: 1. 线性质量-弹簧-阻尼系统：GMKF-TD相比经典TD-α收敛更快更稳定，θ₁参数约600次迭代即可达到机器精度；2. Q值函数学习：成功恢复由Riccati方程解析给出的最优策略参数；3. PCE离散化（6×28系数）与1000样本集合结果一致，但计算效率提升约5倍；4. 2D封闭方腔热对流控制问题（Nusselt数最小化）：GMKF-TD与TD-α均收敛至≈0.63，但GMKF-TD无需学习率调参且提供方差信息；5. 协方差校正步骤在线性-高斯条件下验证与理论预测一致，但存在数值近似误差问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了TD学习与基于投影的条件期望估计之间的等价关系，将经典TD更新恢复为贝叶斯估计的特例。所提GMKF-TD框架通过显式估计值函数的二阶矩，将TD学习从确定性估计扩展到不确定性感知形式，兼容非线性和非高斯系统。参数先验具有概率解释，Kalman增益充当自适应学习率，消除了对经验调参的依赖。该工作为带不确定性传播的TD学习概率框架迈出了第一步，未来工作将聚焦于高维应用扩展和协方差校正的数值鲁棒性改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a generalized temporal-difference (TD) reinforcement learning framework based on the theory of conditional expectations. The value and action-value (Q-value) functions are treated as uncertain quantities, and their estimation is formulated as a stochastic inference problem. Unlike classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning, which relies on linear-Gaussian assumptions, the proposed formulation is derived directly from the conditional expectation framework and naturally extends to nonlinear models and non-Gaussian probability distributions. The proposed method recursively estimates not only the conditional expectation of the value function but also its second probabilistic moment, thereby quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned value function throughout the learning process. To obtain a computationally tractable algorithm, the stochastic problem is discretized using either polynomial chaos expansions or ensemble-based approximations, providing efficient representations of the underlying random variables. The proposed framework is demonstrated on two optimal control problems: a linear mass--spring--damper system and a nonlinear heat conduction problem in a closed cavity. The numerical examples illustrate the capability of the proposed method to accurately estimate both the value function and its associated uncertainty, while extending classical Kalman-based temporal-difference learning to a broader class of stochastic systems.

</details>


### [154] [Zero-Shot Heart Rate Variability Forecasting from Consumer Wearables Using Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20027)
*Luukas Peräkylä,Fahad Sohrab,Ville Hautamäki,Merja Heinäniemi,Sui Huang,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种时间序列基础模型（TimesFM、Chronos、MOIRAI）在消费级可穿戴设备HRV数据上的零样本预测能力，并提出了一种保留变异性的插补方法。Chronos在较短上下文（32步）中表现最佳，TimesFM在较长上下文（64步）中表现最优，MOIRAI未能显著优于均值基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期心率变异性（HRV）预测可为临床医生提供检测自主神经功能障碍和不良心脏事件的预警时间。但消费级可穿戴设备生成的HRV信号具有碎片化和伪影丰富的特点，传统预测方法难以应对。基础模型有望在无需任务特定微调的情况下处理这类异质性生理信号。

Method: 使用49名健康受试者的Samsung Galaxy Watch Active 2真实HRV数据集（4周PPG采集的5分钟RMSSD片段）。提出变异性保留插补方法（线性插值+局部自适应高斯噪声）。零样本评估三种TSFM：TimesFM-200m、Chronos-bolt-base、MOIRAI-1.0-R-base，对比基线（Mean、EWMA α=0.3、ETS）。测试上下文长度32和64步，预测范围1-24步（最长2小时）。评估指标为MAE、RMSE、MASE。

Result: Chronos在上下文32时表现最佳（MAE=15.74，MASE=0.82）；TimesFM在上下文64时表现最佳（MAE=15.32，MASE=0.81）。所有TSFM的MASE在0.81-0.87之间，优于所有传统基线。MOIRAI未能显著优于均值基线（p=0.953和p=0.794），推测其基于patch的架构对短片段数据敏感。配对t检验显示Chronos和TimesFM与基线差异显著。

Conclusion: Chronos和TimesFM是有前途的候选模型，适合通过领域微调部署到消费级心脏监测流程中。MOIRAI对数据碎片化的敏感性需要进一步研究。主要局限在于仅评估了健康个体，未来需在已知心脏疾病的患者队列上验证以支持临床部署。

Abstract: Short-term Heart Rate Variability (HRV) forecasting could provide clinicians with actionable lead time for detecting autonomic dysfunction and adverse cardiac events. Consumer wearable devices generate fragmented, artifact-rich HRV signals that challenge conventional forecasting approaches. In this study, we evaluated the forecasting ability of three Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs), TimesFM, Chronos, and MOIRAI, against traditional baselines (Mean, Exponential Smoothing, and Exponentially Weighted Moving Average) on real-world wearable data collected from 49 healthy individuals. To address data fragmentation, we introduce a variability-preserving imputation method that augments linear interpolation with locally adaptive stochastic noise, retaining physiological dynamics essential for accurate forecasting. The results show that TSFMs outperformed all baselines without fine-tuning, achieving average Mean Absolute Scaled Error (MASE) between 0.81 and 0.87 across TSFMs and both context lengths (32 and 64 time steps), with Chronos and TimesFM as the top models, though MOIRAI showed limited gains over baselines. With up to a 2-hour forecast horizon, the results establish a baseline for TSFMs' performance on a real-world dataset, highlighting domain-specific fine-tuning as a promising direction for clinical deployment.

</details>


### [155] [Evaluating and Mitigating Gender Bias in Pre-trained Embeddings for ML-based Recruitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20073)
*Farnaz Faramarzi Lighvan,Lynn Houthuys*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于机器学习的招聘系统中预训练文本嵌入可能泄露性别信息的问题，在合成数据集FairCVdb上评估了9种预训练嵌入模型的性别信息泄露程度，并提出一种基于梯度反转的多任务对抗学习框架结合Pareto前沿多目标模型选择策略，来平衡预测效用与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于历史CV数据训练的AI招聘系统可能延续和放大社会偏见。虽然显式性别指标（如姓名、代词、称谓）可以被移除，但预训练语言模型的嵌入仍可能从词义、写作风格等隐式线索中推断出性别。此外，现有关于对抗去偏的研究（如Pena等人）多使用单一嵌入模型和固定的公平性权重，缺乏对多种嵌入模型的系统比较以及原则性的准确性-公平性权衡策略。本文旨在探究：多任务对抗训练是否可以替代或补充预处理阶段的性别清洗。

Method: 方法包含三个核心部分：(1) 在FairCVdb（24,000份合成简历，含性别偏置与无偏置得分）上，对原始和性别清洗后的传记分别评估9种预训练嵌入（fastText、GloVe、BERT、RoBERTa、MPNet、sentence-transformers、OpenAI text-embedding-3等）；(2) 采用中融合MLP架构，先独立处理传记嵌入再与结构化特征拼接，用于单任务性别分类和分数预测；(3) 多任务对抗学习框架，通过梯度反转层（GRL）联合训练分数预测头和性别预测头，迫使共享编码器抑制性别信息；模型选择采用基于Pareto前沿的多目标优化，在MAE容忍度约束（原始传记δ=0.3，清洗后δ=0.1）下选择KL散度最低的配置；评估指标包括MAE、KL散度、P%规则和基于无偏真实得分的Recall@100。

Result: 实验显示：(1) 所有嵌入模型在原始传记上的性别预测准确率均达0.969-0.995，清洗后仍保持在0.745-0.802（远高于随机），其中OpenAI text-embedding-3-large泄露最严重；(2) 多任务对抗学习在原始传记上一致改善公平性（KL降低、P%升高、Recall Gap减小），其中RoBERTa取得最佳平衡；(3) 在清洗传记上，对抗学习效果不一致，取决于嵌入模型，microsoft/mpnet-base综合最优；(4) 关键发现：文本级性别清洗带来的公平性改善比对抗训练更强且更一致。

Conclusion: 本文证实预处理阶段的性别清洗虽不能完全消除性别泄露，但比对抗训练产生更强且更稳定的公平性提升；多任务对抗学习应作为文本级去偏的补充策略而非替代方案。嵌入模型的选择对预测效用和公平性均有显著影响，在公平敏感的招聘应用中应谨慎选用。此外，限制包括仅基于FairCVdb合成数据集以及仅关注二元性别，未来需扩展至真实数据和其他受保护属性（包括交叉性分析）。

Abstract: AI-based recruitment systems that rely on machine learning models trained on historical CV data, risk perpetuating and amplifying social biases. A key challenge arises in unstructured CV text, where pre-trained language model embeddings may infer sensitive attributes such as gender even after explicit indicators are removed. In this paper, we evaluate nine pre-trained embedding models on the synthetic FairCVdb dataset, analyzing the informativeness of their embeddings for applicant scoring and their susceptibility to gender leakage, on both original and gender-scrubbed biographies. We further use a multi-task adversarial learning framework with gradient reversal to predict applicant suitability while suppressing gender information from learned representations. Finally, we use a multi-objective Pareto-front-based model selection to balance predictive utility and fairness. Our experimental results show that explicit gender scrubbing substantially reduces but does not eliminate gender leakage, while adversarial learning improves fairness mainly on original biographies and acts as a complementary strategy rather than a substitute for text-level debiasing.

</details>


### [156] [Autonomous Collaborative Learning Among an Ensemble of Tsetlin Machines with Consensus-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20124)
*Yehuda Rudin,Osnat Keren,Michal Yemini,Alexander Fish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Tsetlin Machine（TM）的去中心化、分层式协作学习框架。该框架在垂直特征划分（vertical feature-partitioning）场景下，通过gossip式通信协议和基于共识的推理机制，使多个异构TM代理在不交换原始数据和模型参数的前提下协同完成分类任务，并在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST及合成传感器网络数据集上取得了与集中式模型相当的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备受限于算力与能耗，难以承担复杂运算或上传原始数据至云端。联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但现有的TM联邦学习方法（FedTM、TPFL、FedTMOS）均依赖中心服务器进行模型聚合，存在通信开销大、单点故障等问题。此外，分布式/去中心化TM学习领域尚未得到充分探索，且缺乏对异构代理（不同感知模态、计算资源、数据分布）的支持。因此，亟需一种适用于资源受限边缘环境、保护数据隐私且支持异构代理的协作学习范式。

Method: 提出两层分层TM架构：(1) 输入层TM——仅基于本地布尔特征训练子句；(2) 邻域聚合层TM——将本代理及邻居代理的输入层子句输出作为输入，学习更高层次的模式。代理间采用异步gossip随机广播通信协议进行信息交换（仅传输单比特编码的子句输出，不暴露模型参数与原始数据）。推理阶段，代理通过多源全对全扩散协议（multi-source all-to-all dissemination）共享投票和，经Θ(n)时间步达成全局共识。实验在n×n环面网格和一般连通图拓扑上展开，分别测试49/16代理的图像分块场景及25/9代理的异构传感器网络场景，采用分位数分箱（quantile binning）将连续特征二值化。

Result: 在MNIST上：49代理（4×4瓦片）达到94.40%，16代理（7×7瓦片）达到94.65%（经典集中TM为96.73%）；在Fashion-MNIST上：49代理达到83.85%，16代理达到83.99%（经典TM为87.75%）。两层架构显著优于单层。在25代理传感器网络中，TM集成模型达到93%准确率，与聚合全部数据训练的集中式ANN（93%）完全一致；随着参与代理数增加，TM准确率逐步逼近集中式ANN。在9代理一般连通图拓扑下，模型取得76%准确率，与集中式ANN和9代理网格配置相当。系统级TM资源（子句与自动机总数）超过单TM基线，但通过各代理的非重叠私有样本实现了同等精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层TM框架成功实现了去中心化、保护隐私（不暴露原始数据和模型参数）的协作学习，支持异构代理（不同感知模态、计算能力、数据分布）灵活集成，适用于多模态感知场景。通信仅限于代理与近邻之间，具备可扩展性。实验证明其分类性能与集中式模型相当，且模型轻量，适合边缘计算部署。本研究假设理想通信条件，未来工作将评估真实通信环境下的性能（包括丢包、延迟等）并进行正式的隐私泄露分析。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a rule-based machine-learning algorithm comprising collectives of two-action Tsetlin Automata (TAs) that cooperatively form conjunctive logical clauses from Boolean inputs through stochastic feedback. Although few recent studies have examined TM Federated Learning, the broader area of distributed and decentralized TM learning has not received much attention in the existing literature and warrants further exploration. In this work, we propose a paradigm for decentralized collaborative learning under a vertical feature-partitioning setting among an ensemble of Tsetlin Machines using consensus-based inference. Within this decentralized paradigm, each agent maintains its own private TM model, and there is no exchange of raw data among agents. Inference combines individual agents model predictions into a global consensus. The paradigm accommodates heterogeneous TM-based agents with differing data acquisition means, local data distributions, or computational resources, thereby facilitating the integration and fusion of information in settings such as multi-modal sensing environments. Experiments conducted using two-dimensional grid and connected graph network topologies demonstrate that the classification accuracies achieved are comparable to those of centralized models.

</details>


### [157] [CURED: Creating, Understanding, and Repairing Errors Demonstrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20140)
*Nicholas Chandler,Sebastian Jäger,Philipp Jung,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CURED是一个用于表格数据中错误检测、修复与机理理解的Web演示系统，集成了基于机器学习的错误注入、清洗与机理识别三大模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的错误检测和清洗是数据密集型应用的关键前提，但现有的数据清洗工具（如OpenRefine）缺乏与现代化ML清洗方法的集成，且理论研究成果难以转化为直观的实践理解。因此需要一个统一的演示工具来弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 系统由三大核心组件构成：(1) 使用tab-err库注入基于错误类型、错误机理（ECAR/EAR/ENAR）和错误率的现实数据相关错误；(2) 使用Conformal Data Cleaning (CDC) 基于保形预测进行错误检测与纠正，通过将不可靠的单元格替换为ML模型的点预测；(3) 使用MechDetect算法通过比较ML模型预测错误掩码的性能来表征错误机理。后端采用CatBoost等表格ML模型，对每列训练imputer，利用保形预测的不确定性校准生成统计严格的预测集。

Result: 在UCI核电站数据集（OpenML ID 44969）上的实验显示：CDC在三种错误机理下均保持较低的误报率（FPR约0.0065-0.0070）和稳定的检出率（TPR约0.645-0.650），并带来下游模型性能提升（DSI 0.0069-0.0349）。MechDetect在数值和分类列上对ECAR和EAR机理识别准确率超过0.92，但对ENAR机理准确率较低（约0.71-0.73）。

Conclusion: CURED成功将错误生成、保形清洗与机理检测三项研究整合为一个开源Web演示器，使用户能够直观理解表格数据错误及其修复算法。未来工作方向包括将MechDetect集成到清洗流水线、扩展tab-err的错误类型，以及将这些方法应用于工业级场景。

Abstract: Detecting and cleaning errors in tabular data is a prerequisite for data intense software applications. Recent research at the intersection of Machine Learning (ML) and Database Management Systems (DBMS) highlights the potential of statistical learning algorithms for error detection and cleaning. This paper combines our recent work on ML-based data cleaning and error models in a unified demonstrator. The web application allows users to upload tabular data, perturb the data with realistic data dependent errors and use modern ML methods to clean and understand error mechanisms in data. Our demonstrator helps to bridge the gap between theoretical advancements and intuitive practical insights in the context of error models and data cleaning algorithms for tabular data. The demonstrator is available at this https URL

</details>


### [158] [Local Stability and Gaussian Smoothing of Quantized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20153)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯平均作为量化神经模型的平滑替代方法，推导出在有界局部振荡假设下关于 |f-g| 的局部维度依赖界，并给出 ReLU 与 sign 激活函数的闭式高斯平均表达式，最后在高维二元感知器上演示其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化神经网络因离散/不连续激活导致训练与推理不稳定，需要一个理论严谨且可解析处理的平滑代理来近似量化行为；高斯平滑作为一种通用的平滑手段，其误差控制与对特定激活函数的适用性需要被严格刻画。

Method: 将量化模型的输出视为 f，将高斯平均后的平滑模型视为 g；在 f 具有有界局部振荡的条件下，推导关于逼近误差 |f-g| 的局部、维度依赖的解析上界；针对 ReLU 与 sign 激活函数计算其闭式高斯平均表达式；并构造显式的量化噪声代理，通过逐层预激活聚合，在二元感知器上获得推理端的高斯包络与训练端的平滑代理梯度。

Result: 得到维度依赖的局部逼近误差界，ReLU 与 sign 的高斯平均具有闭式表达；在高维二元感知器上验证了“显式量化噪声代理 → 层预激活聚合 → 高斯包络”这一机制在推理平滑与训练梯度代理两方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 高斯平均可作为量化神经网络的理论合理且实用的平滑替代；在有界局部振荡条件下，逼近误差可由维度依赖的局部界控制；对常见激活函数的闭式结果使该方法具备解析可处理性，可同时服务于推理端的平滑化与训练端的稳定梯度。

Abstract: We study Gaussian averaging as a smooth surrogate for quantized neural models. Under bounded local oscillation, we derive a local dimension-dependent bound on |f-g|, linking Gaussian smoothing to the stability analysis of discontinuous networks. We compute closed-form Gaussian averages of the rectified linear unit (ReLU) and sign activation functions, and illustrate the mechanism on a high-dimensional binary perceptron, where layer-preactivation aggregation under an explicit quantization-noise surrogate yields the Gaussian envelope used in inference-side smoothing and training-side smooth surrogate gradients.

</details>


### [159] [Self-organizing Architecture of Receptron Units: a Hardware-Aware Framework for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20162)
*Stefano Radice,Ludovico Casaccia,Riccaro Emanuele Beccalli,Bruno Paroli,Paolo Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Receptron模型的神经形态启发式单单元分类器，通过输入依赖权重函数（由高斯感受野近似）实现非线性可分决策边界，无需多层网络，适用于资源受限MCU的边缘智能场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI部署受限于MCU有限的算力、内存和能耗，传统深度学习模型（基于线性感知机构建多层网络）在此类硬件上不切实际；现有量化、剪枝等压缩方法以牺牲精度或增加延迟为代价，且难以支持设备端在线自适应训练以应对传感器漂移和非平稳环境。

Method: 1）数学形式化：Receptron通过输入依赖权重函数实现非线性内积，利用高斯感受野组合建模非凸决策边界，通过Taylor展开证明其与原始Receptron求和规则的等价性；2）中心分配：采用类条件Kohonen自组织映射（SOM）进行初始化，结合PCA引导投影分配原型中心，使用类内WTA竞争和类Hebbian更新规则；3）推理：K次内积加阈值比较，可通过加权投票（1/σ_k）扩展为多类分类；4）在线学习：支持通过中心坐标的平移适应传感器漂移，无需反向传播。

Result: 在Iris数据集（4特征/3类）上获得90.0%±5.1%的5折交叉验证准确率，在Breast Cancer Wisconsin数据集（30特征/2类）上获得93.5%±1.1%的准确率，与SVC基线（94.7%/94.4%）相当；推理计算量仅为K×n次MAC操作（Iris 48次，Breast Cancer 480次），模型存储约1.9–4KB，可直接部署于ARM Cortex-M4或ESP32等中端MCU。

Conclusion: Receptron架构将竞争学习、阈值化高斯激活和PCA引导初始化融合为可解释、低计算量的分类框架，在保持与经典ML方法相当的精度的同时，具备极低内存占用、确定性时延和在线自适应能力，是动态非平稳边缘场景下神经形态系统的可行替代方案；未来工作将聚焦于基于局部密度的自适应中心数选择及工业级基准验证。

Abstract: The growing demand for intelligent processing at the edge of IoT networks is constrained by the severe computational and memory limitations of microcontroller units, which render impractical conventional deep learning approaches. We propose a neuromorphicinspired classifier based on the Receptron model, a single-unit architecture capable of implementing non-linearly separable decision boundaries, without resorting to multi-layer networks. The model is designed for direct deployment on mid-range MCUs, while supporting continuous on-device adaptation. Experimental evaluation on basic dataset benchmarks yields cross-validated accuracies compatible with standard machine learning method baselines. These results position the Receptron as a viable and interpretable alternative for resource-constrained neuromorphic edge systems operating in dynamic, non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [160] [Instance Hardness-Based Relevance for Imbalanced Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20173)
*Vitor M. Leitao,Juscimara G. Avelino,George D. C. Cavalcanti,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实例硬度（Instance Hardness）的相关性函数 InHaR，用于解决不平衡回归问题中传统相关性函数在双峰分布等复杂场景下无法准确识别稀有实例的局限性。实验表明，InHaR 引导的重采样策略在约 70% 的测试中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不平衡回归方法依赖基于目标变量分布的相关性函数（如 Ribeiro 2011 提出的 Tukey 法），通过全局统计阈值判定稀有性。但在双峰分布等复杂场景下，传统函数会对所有低密度区域赋予近似为零的相关性值，无法区分不同模式间的稀有与正常实例，导致对稀有性的刻画不完整。文献中尚缺乏不依赖于目标变量统计分布的稀有性判据。

Method: 1) 提出 InHaR（Instance Hardness-based Relevance）相关性函数：利用回归模型集合的预测误差计算每个实例的硬度值 IH ∈ [0,1]（公式 1：基于指数距离的归一化预测误差），硬度越高代表实例越难学习；2) 通过相对阈值 τ（本文取 0.7）将实例划分为稀有集 D_R 与正常集 D_N；3) 将 InHaR 嵌入到随机过采样（RO）和高斯噪声（GN）重采样策略中替代传统相关性函数；4) 在 29 个数据集、5 种回归器（RF、BG、XGB、SVR、MLP）上，采用 2×5 交叉验证，以 MAE 和 MSE 作为与相关性无关的评价指标。

Result: (1) IH 与传统相关性函数 φ 之间存在中等至强相关但不重合，存在大量"高 IH/低 φ"实例（即统计上不稀有但难预测）；(2) 在双峰合成数据集上，InHaR 正确识别出模式间难学习的区域，且在噪声水平 0–20 范围内保持稳健；(3) 145 个模型-数据集配对中，InHaR-RO 在 MAE 上获胜 48 次（~50%），InHaR-GN 获胜 20 次；(4) Wilcoxon 检验显示 InHaR-RO vs RO 在 MAE 上 27 胜 2 负（22 次显著），MSE 上 20 胜 9 负（13 次显著）；(5) 在稀有实例子集上评估时，InHaR 引导的方法在其自身定义的稀有区域表现更优，且未对正常区域性能造成显著退化。

Conclusion: InHaR 是基于分布的传统相关性函数的可行替代方案，尤其在双峰目标分布等复杂场景下表现突出。其核心优势在于基于学习难度而非纯统计稀有性来定义相关性，能够更精细地刻画实例重要性，并在改善困难实例预测性能的同时不损害正常区域的准确率。局限性在于计算成本较高（需训练多个回归模型），且阈值 τ 的选择缺乏系统性敏感性分析，未来需研究数据集自适应的阈值调整策略。

Abstract: Imbalanced regression problems arise when the target variable has an asymmetric distribution, resulting in underrepresented value ranges in the dataset. Traditional approaches for identifying rare instances rely on a relevance function that assigns higher importance to specific regions of the target distribution. However, the effectiveness of imbalance-aware learning methods depends strongly on how relevance is defined. In more complex scenarios, such as bimodal distributions, traditional relevance functions struggle to capture rarity, as they assign fixed relevance values based solely on target values, thereby compromising the distinction between truly rare and normal instances. To address these limitations, this study proposes an Instance Hardness-based relevance function (InHaR) for identifying rare instances in regression problems. Unlike traditional relevance functions, the proposed approach incorporates learning difficulty, allowing rarity to be inferred not only from the target distribution but also from the difficulty of instances for the learning algorithm. This property is particularly important in bimodal scenarios, where rarity cannot be accurately inferred from target values alone. Experimental results demonstrate that the InHaR correctly identifies rare regions under bimodal distributions and, when used to guide resampling strategies such as Random Oversampling (RO) and Gaussian Noise (GN), leads to significant improvements in predictive performance compared to traditional relevance-based approaches. The code, dataset, and further details about the proposed method are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [161] [On Optimization Complexity of Second-Order Certified Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20192)
*Nikita Doikov,Anastasia Koloskova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化理论角度研究了认证遗忘（certified unlearning）的算法复杂度问题，提出了一种基于各向异性高斯机制的二阶遗忘算法，针对拟自协损失（quasi-self-concordant losses）建立了当前最优的全局收敛速率，并在logistic和exponential回归上证明了二阶方法相对于一阶方法的可证明优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统需要具备"遗忘"数据的能力（出于伦理、法律要求），但对已训练的大模型进行完全重训练代价极高甚至不可行。因此需要设计高效的遗忘算法，在无需从头训练的前提下移除特定数据的影响。已有工作多关注差分隐私式的认证保证，但对遗忘的**优化复杂度**（即达到同时满足认证保证与模型精度的迭代步数）理解不足，特别是缺乏对二阶（Newton型）方法的理论分析。

Method: 1) 将遗忘目标形式化为同时满足(q,δ)-认证遗忘(定义式3)与ε-优化精度保证(式4)。<br/>2) 引入"预测误差"量π(x)=‖∇u(x)+ψ'(x)‖_*，作为模型在遗忘数据上的泛化度量，并利用一致凸正则化（定义式6）建立两个数据集最优点之间的距离界（引理2.1）。<br/>3) 设计二阶遗忘算法（Algorithm 1）：<br/>   - Hessian近似：H_k := ∇²f(x_k) + M·g_k·B，其中B=Σa_i a_i^⊤，M为拟自协常数；<br/>   - 各向异性高斯机制：在输出端添加ξ~N(0,σ²B⁻¹)；<br/>   - 在强凸正则下可显式写出迭代式x_{k+1}=x_k-(∇²f(x_k)+(Mg_k+μ)B)⁻¹∇f_ψ(x_k)。<br/>4) 利用损失函数的拟自协性质（Assumption 2.2）分析Newton方法的全局收敛。<br/>5) 分析一阶梯度法作为基线（Theorem 3.1）。

Result: 1) **理论界（Lemma 2.1）**：当遗忘数据被遗忘模型良好预测时，两个最优解之间的距离‖x_F^★-x_f^★‖≤[γπ(x_f^★)/μ]^{1/(p-1)}，函数残差被π(x_F^★)控制。<br/>2) **Newton方法收敛率（Theorem 3.2）**：K=O(M[γπ(x_F^★)/μ]^{1/p}+log(γπ(x_F^★)/(με^{p-1})))步达到ε精度，ε以加性对数项出现，不依赖于数据规格‖A‖²。<br/>3) **一阶梯度法（Theorem 3.1）**：K=O(L‖A‖²/μ^{2/p}·[(1/(με^p))^{(p-2)/p}-...])，复杂度依赖于‖A‖²。<br/>4) **认证遗忘保证（Theorem 3.3）**：取σ=(ε/q)·max{1, 2√(2ln(2/δ))}，算法满足(q,δ)-认证且期望精度为√(ε²+σ²d)。<br/>5) **整体复杂度（Corollary 3.4）**：主要复杂度因子γ、μ、π(x_F^★)均处在线性或对数位置。<br/>6) **应用**：理论覆盖logistic和exponential回归，证明了利用二阶信息的可证明收益。

Conclusion: 本文首次为二阶（Newton型）认证遗忘方法建立了快速全局收敛性理论：在一致凸正则与拟自协损失假设下，所提出的各向异性高斯遗忘机制能以对数级迭代步数同时满足认证保证与优化精度。关键洞见是遗忘数据上的"预测误差"π(x)直接决定了优化的难度，从而为遗忘算法的复杂度提供了精细刻画。理论结果可直接应用于广义线性模型（含logistic/exponential回归），并证明二阶信息优于一阶方法。该工作连接了优化理论与认证遗忘两个领域，为高效可信机器遗忘提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study machine unlearning: the removal of memorized training data from a trained model. Specifically, we investigate the algorithmic complexity of certified unlearning from an optimization perspective. We formalize the goal of an unlearning algorithm as simultaneously achieving certified unlearning and optimization accuracy. Utilizing the notion of uniformly convex regularizers, we prove new bounds on the distance between initial and unlearned models using a novel substitute for generalization error. Thus we theoretically demonstrate that if the removed data is well-predicted by the unlearned model, the corresponding optimization problem is simple. Furthermore, we develop a new second-order unlearning algorithm with an anisotropic Gaussian mechanism and state-of-the-art global convergence. We prove fast rates for our method in achieving certified unlearning for linear models with quasi-self-concordant losses. As a direct application, our theory covers unlearning for logistic and exponential regressions and shows a provable benefit of utilizing second-order information compared to first-order unlearning methods.

</details>


### [162] [OLEDLM: A Unified Language Model for OLED Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20194)
*Fukang Wen,Yuchong Tang,Jingyuan Li,Beichen Wang,Yixuan Jiang,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yaxuan Liu,Shunyu Wang,Zuoqiang Shi,Yi Zhu,Yanan Zhu,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OLEDLM框架，首次将大语言模型（LLaMA风格Transformer）应用于OLED有机发光二极管分子的逆向设计。该框架通过领域自适应预训练BERT作为属性预测器，结合条件监督微调（SFT）与组相对策略优化（GRPO）强化学习，实现根据目标光电子属性（如单线态能量S₁、振子强度f）直接生成SMILES序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OLED材料发现面临化学空间巨大、量子化学约束严苛、标记数据稀缺的三重挑战。传统高通量筛选与启发式修改受限于量子力学模拟成本与人类直觉局限。现有生成式AI方法要么难以保证分子有效性，要么无法精确控制光电子属性，且专门针对OLED领域训练的模型极为匮乏。本文旨在填补通用分子生成与光电材料严格结构要求之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用四阶段多阶段策略：
1. **基础化学语言模型**：在900万OLED相关SMILES语料上从头预训练LLaMA风格Transformer（约100M参数，使用RoPE、RMSNorm、SwiGLU）。
2. **领域自适应属性预测器**：在900万SMILES上以掩码语言建模（MLM）目标训练BERT（约86M参数），采用部分微调策略冻结前层，在1万条DFT标记数据（B3LYP/6-31G**）上微调预测S₁与f。
3. **条件监督微调（SFT）**：将连续属性离散化为控制码（如`<s1>`、`<num_X>`、`<dot>`、`<res_YY>`）前缀拼接至SMILES训练，采用混合数据策略（10×无标注数据每epoch重采样）防止记忆化与灾难性遗忘。
4. **GRPO对齐**：使用GRPO（组大小G=20）替代PPO，避免critic网络，以属性预测器作为奖励模型，奖励函数包含有效性惩罚项与KL正则化（β=0.5）。

Result: 在1万样本主基准测试中：
- **GRPO**：有效性98.3%，S₁ MAE=0.25 eV，f MAE=0.15，唯一性67.2%，新颖性94.4%。
- **SFT（3轮）**：有效性82.9%，新颖性89.4%。
- **SFT-20x（20轮）**：S₁ MAE降至0.19但新颖性崩塌至58.0%，证实记忆化问题。
- **DFT验证（20个收敛分子）**：SFT-20x DFT误差最低（S₁ MAE=0.138 eV，f MAE=0.076），但GRPO在保持94.4%新颖性下优于SFT（3轮）。
- **GRPO vs PPO（100样本对照）**：两者有效性均100%，GRPO属性误差更低，PPO多样性更好。
- **属性预测器消融**：领域自适应OLED-BERT（R²≈0.69）显著优于MoLFormer-XL（R²≈0.65）和ChemBERTa（R²≈0.34-0.40）。
- **架构对比**：LSTM因SMILES序列较长崩溃，仅产生"CC"等碎片。
- **KL正则化消融**：β=0.5在属性对齐与多样性间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: OLEDLM首次成功将LLM适配OLED领域，通过GRPO解决分子RL中critic估计难题。扩展的DFT独立验证表明GRPO在20个收敛分子上达到S₁ MAE=0.260 eV、f MAE=0.134，同时保持高新颖性。Scaffold分析显示其与参考集相似度低但片段使用合理。SFT-20x虽DFT误差更低但新颖性损失显著，凸显属性精度与真正新颖发现之间的核心权衡。未来仍需实验合成验证、解决预测器分布偏移问题，并扩展DFT样本规模。

Abstract: The development of organic light-emitting diode (OLED) materials faces the compounded challenges of an astronomically large chemical space, stringent quantum-chemical constraints, and a scarcity of labeled data. Although the question of OLED generation is important, few models have been trained effectively for this specific domain. We propose an inverse molecular design framework based on causal language models: given target optoelectronic properties (e.g., excitation energy, oscillator strength), our model directly generates OLED SMILES sequences satisfying the specified constraints. We employ a multi-stage strategy: first, we establish a foundational chemical language model using a LLaMA-style transformer architecture. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first successful adaptation of LLMs specifically for the OLED domain, bridging the gap between generic molecular generation and the stringent structural requirements of optoelectronic materials. Second, we fine-tune property predictors based on a BERT model pre-trained on our large-scale OLED dataset. Then, we perform Reinforcement Learning on our fine-tuned model, leveraging our property predictor, for better SMILES generation. Finally, through DFT verification, we demonstrate that our framework can efficiently navigate the OLED chemical space, generating novel candidates with high structural validity and optimized optoelectronic properties.

</details>


### [163] [User-Centric Modeling of Transactional Sequences with Explainable State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20228)
*Ivan Palagin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种混合架构,将冻结的CoLES用户嵌入整合到Mamba状态空间模型中,通过隐藏状态初始化和前缀拼接两种方式注入用户先验,实验证明在三个交易序列数据集上均超越单独使用Mamba或CoLES,并加速2-3倍收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户行为建模对欺诈检测、信用评分等至关重要。CoLES对比学习方法能产出高质量用户表示,但依赖RNN(梯度消失)或Transformer(O(L²)复杂度)作为序列编码器,在长交易序列(如平均881事件/用户)上存在效率瓶颈。Mamba等选择性SSM虽具O(L)复杂度和长程依赖建模能力,但在以用户为中心的个性化建模场景中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 数据:Age(4类年龄分组,24K用户/21M事件,平均881长度)、MBD(4个二值产品获取标签,781K用户/156M事件)、Taobao(二值购买预测,18K用户);2) 特征预处理:数值特征经log+sign变换、z-score归一化,时间戳编码为差值δt;3) 基线:Plain Mamba(零初始隐藏状态)与CoLES+线性分类器;4) 提出两种混合方法——隐藏状态初始化(经两层非线性投影h₀=W₂σ(W₁e)),以及前缀拼接(X̃=[Pe;X]);5) CoLES冻结并预训练10万步;6) 使用Optuna(TPE,50次试验)进行逐对超参调优;7) 通过离散化步长图Δₜ和Integrated Gradients进行可解释性分析。

Result: Age上Mamba+init准确率达0.386(较Plain Mamba 0.354提升+3.2pp),MBD上mROC-AUC达0.737(较0.711提升+2.6pp),Taobao上ROC-AUC达0.699(较0.692提升+0.7pp);两种混合方法在三个数据集上均一致超越基线,CoLES+线性分类器表现最弱;收敛速度方面混合模型仅需2-3个epoch达到峰值,而Plain Mamba需6个epoch;可解释性分析显示Taobao上Δₜ出现明显凹陷,反映选择性事件过滤(浏览→购买),MBD上货币特征cat_0主导重要性(0.154),Age上各特征重要性近均匀分布;超参最优配置:学习率约10⁻⁴,3层Mamba,状态空间维度32。

Conclusion: 两种CoLES-Mamba混合架构在三个交易序列基准上一致提升性能并显著加速收敛,验证了用户级先验对SSM的有效性;可解释性分析揭示模型在行为丰富的电商数据上形成选择性事件过滤,而在结构化程度较低的银行数据上更均匀地依赖所有特征;改进幅度有限的原因在于CoLES训练于短随机子序列而忽略全局时序,且线性投影表达能力不足;未来方向包括端到端联合训练、以交叉注意力替代线性投影、评估Mamba-2、扩展到L>1000的长序列,以及与LLM流水线融合以结合用户先验与语言语义。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid approach for user-centric modeling of transactional event sequences that combines contrastive representation learning (CoLES) with State Space Models (SSMs). While contrastive methods yield high-quality compressed user representations, existing encoders -- RNNs and Transformers -- suffer from vanishing gradients or quadratic complexity, respectively. Mamba, a selective SSM, efficiently handles long-range dependencies but remains underexplored for personalized user analysis. We investigate two integration strategies: (1)~initializing the Mamba hidden state with a CoLES embedding, and (2)~prepending the projected CoLES embedding as a prefix token to the input sequence. Both approaches supply the model with an informative user prior from the first step. Experiments on three public datasets -- Age (multiclass age-group prediction), MBD (multi-label product acquisition), and Taobao (binary purchase prediction) -- demonstrate consistent improvements over standalone Mamba and CoLES with a linear classifier, with the hybrid models converging 2--3$\times$ faster than the plain SSM baseline. Explainability analysis via discretization-step maps and Integrated Gradients reveals selective event filtering on behavior-rich datasets and identifies the most informative transaction features.

</details>


### [164] [PIER: Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval for Time-Series Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20230)
*Shiyuan Luo,Runlong Yu,Chonghao Qiu,Yue Qin,Rahul Ghosh,Robert Ladwig,Paul C. Hanson,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIER是一种面向环境时序建模的模型无关检索增强框架，通过引入基于物理通量一致性的检索流来弥补传统嵌入检索无法保证底层物理过程一致的缺陷，并利用权重调整机制自适应平衡两种检索流。在356个湖泊41年数据的水温和溶解氧预测任务上，该方法在多个基线模型上一致取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境系统建模面临观测稀疏与系统间物理动力学差异大的双重挑战。检索增强方法可跨系统迁移知识，但传统嵌入检索仅捕捉统计相似性，无法保证底层物理过程的一致性——嵌入相似的场景可能由完全不同的物理机制驱动，导致检索到的样本引入与目标系统冲突的动力学信息，降低预测性能。

Method: PIER包含两个互补检索流：(1) 表示相似性流——使用全局模型的隐藏状态嵌入计算余弦相似度；(2) 物理感知流——为每个系统ℓ训练仅基于物理过程模型（如GLM）输出的通量特征的局部验证器g_ℓ，通过将目标系统的验证器应用于候选场景的通量特征并测量预测误差（指数映射为归一化分数）来评估物理一致性。此外设计了模型质量保护机制（μ_ℓ>0时才启用物理流）以及双层优化驱动的权重调整门控网络h_ϕ，根据诊断特征（局部验证器质量、候选通过率、流一致性比率）预测每场景的最优混合权重γ_q。最终检索集合用于微调场景特定预测器d_{θ_q^*}（从全局模型初始化）。

Result: 在356个美国中西部湖泊41年数据上，PIER在水温与溶解氧预测的六个季节分层设定（夏季表层/底层/秋冬混合）中RMSE均优于全部基线（含Informer、iTransformer、TSMixer、TimesNet、TimeMixer及纯物理模型GLM），最高改善幅度显著（如夏季底层水温RMSE从基线最优的1.8524降至1.7020）。消融实验表明：简单检索（E1）甚至会损害DO预测；仅使用物理流（E2）对水温效果有限；等权混合（E3）优于任一单流；学习权重调整（E5）取得全面最优，验证了自适应权重机制的必要性。案例研究显示PIER能通过物理流抑制与目标物理机制冲突的候选（如目标湖泊自身历史年中偏离典型模式的高DO年份），提升真正物理兼容的候选（如具有类似低DO通量响应的其他湖泊）。

Conclusion: PIER提出了一种融合物理知识的检索增强框架，通过局部验证器与自适应权重门控解决了嵌入检索缺乏物理一致性的核心问题，可作为通用增强策略适配多种主干网络。实验验证了其在美国中西部大规模湖泊水温和溶解氧建模中的有效性。未来工作将扩展至农业等领域，并探索物理过程与机器学习更深入的融合方式。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of environmental systems is fundamental to scientific understanding and decision-making, yet remains challenging because observations are limited and physical dynamics vary across systems. Retrieval-augmented approaches offer a natural path to transfer knowledge across systems, but standard embedding-based retrieval does not guarantee consistency of underlying physical processes, since scenarios with similar embeddings may arise from different underlying mechanisms. We propose Physics-Informed Environmental Retrieval (PIER), a model-agnostic framework that augments embedding-based retrieval with a physics-aware stream that scores candidates by flux-response consistency with the target, using local verifiers trained on physics-derived flux features. A weight adjustment mechanism then learns per-scenario weights that adaptively balance the two retrieval streams based on diagnostic features summarizing physics-stream reliability. Experiments on 356 lakes across the Midwestern United States spanning 41 years show that PIER consistently outperforms baselines for water temperature and dissolved oxygen prediction, and serves as a general augmentation strategy across diverse backbones.

</details>


### [165] [PhaseAware: Interpretable Human-in-the-Loop Rehabilitation Scoring with Boundary Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20237)
*Yankai Zheng,Yuhe Liu,Yuxin Ma,Tianci Xue,Jiayuan Tian,Yu Fu,Yuxuan Hu,Jianing Wang,Zichun Xiao,Junya Mu,Shaohui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhaseAware是一个用于持续康复质量评估的紧凑框架，通过时间骨干网络结合阶段与身体组描述符，在UI-PRMD深蹲协议上实现了88.9%的RMSE降低，并能跨协议迁移，同时生成可解释的复审线索以支持临床医生监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 康复评分系统的输出若能嵌入临床工作流进行审查和解释将更具实用价值。现有自动化康复评估方法往往缺乏可解释性，难以辅助临床医生进行边界病例监测和人工参与的分诊决策，因此需要一种既准确又能提供结构化复审线索的方法。

Method: 提出PhaseAware紧凑框架，将时间骨干网络与阶段描述符和身体组描述符相结合，通过骨干网络条件化的门控残差通路（backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway）来稳定特征表示，使其适用于资源受限环境。该模型不仅预测评分，还基于阶段和身体级别的敏感性生成结构化复审线索。

Result: 在UI-PRMD深蹲协议上，PhaseAware实现了0.0230的RMSE，相较于已有基线降低了88.9%；在KIMORE深蹲子集上也保持了良好的性能，表明该阶段感知设计可在相关深蹲协议间迁移。模型还能生成结构化的复审线索，突出与每次预测最相关的运动阶段和身体区域。

Conclusion: PhaseAware提供了一种实用且可解释的康复评分方法，有助于将自动化评估整合到信息系统中，同时保留临床医生的监督权。其门控残差机制支持资源受限场景部署，复审线索支持临床审查、边界病例监测和人在环路的分诊，而非用于自主决策。

Abstract: Rehabilitation scoring systems are most useful when their outputs can be reviewed and interpreted within clinical workflows. This study presents PhaseAware, a compact framework for continuous rehabilitation quality assessment that combines a temporal backbone with phase- and body-group descriptors through a backbone-conditioned gated residual pathway. The model was evaluated on the UI-PRMD deep-squat protocol and further tested on the KIMORE squatting subset. On UI-PRMD, PhaseAware achieved an RMSE of 0.0230, corresponding to an 88.9% reduction relative to the accepted baseline. It also maintained favorable performance on KIMORE, suggesting that the phase-aware design transfers across related squatting protocols. In addition to score prediction, PhaseAware generates structured review cues based on phase- and body-level sensitivity, highlighting the movement stages and body regions most relevant to each prediction. The architecture employs a backbone-conditioned gated residual mechanism to stabilize feature representation, supporting use in resource-constrained settings. These cues are intended to support clinician review, boundary-case monitoring, and human-in-the-loop triage rather than autonomous decision-making. Overall, PhaseAware offers a practical and interpretable approach to rehabilitation scoring that may help integrate automated assessment into information systems while preserving clinician oversight.

</details>


### [166] [Breaking the $T^{3/4}$ Barrier for Regret Minimization With Bi-Dimensional CDFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20258)
*Matteo Castiglioni,Anna Lunghi,Alberto Marchesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在[0,1]²域上关于累积分布函数（CDF）的遗憾最小化问题，学习目标形如 g(x)·P(X≤x)，其中 g 是已知的 Lipschitz 函数，分布 D 未知。通过设计新算法，将遗憾上界从之前的 Õ(T^{3/4}) 改进到 Õ(T^{7/10})，部分突破了维度诅咒，但仍与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 下界存在差距，并可应用于双边贸易中的利润最大化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习 CDF 相关目标函数在定价、贸易和决策问题中具有重要应用。现有算法在二维设置下的遗憾界为 Õ(T^{3/4})，未能有效缓解维度诅咒。本文旨在缩小与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 信息论下界之间的差距，提升算法在二维情形下的依赖维度能力。

Method: 针对形式为 g(x)·P(X≤x) 的目标函数，在每轮选择点 x_t 后获得二值反馈 I(X_t ≤ x_t)（其中 X_t ~ D），作者设计了一种新的在线学习算法，能够在二维情形下实现更优的遗憾上界。算法利用了 Lipschitz 函数 g 的已知结构以及二值反馈信号。

Result: 主要结果是将此类 CDF 相关目标的学习遗憾从之前的 Õ(T^{3/4}) 提升到 Õ(T^{7/10})。该结果部分缓解了维度诅咒，但仍存在与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 下界之间的差距。此外，该方法可直接应用于固定价格下的重复双边贸易利润最大化问题，得到相同的 Õ(T^{7/10}) 遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文证明对于 CDF 相关目标函数的遗憾最小化问题，至少可以部分突破维度诅咒，为 Õ(T^{7/10}) 的新上界与 Ω(T^{2/3}) 的下界之间留下了仍有待进一步研究的空间。算法技术亦可成功应用于双边贸易等实际定价场景。

Abstract: We study regret minimization for learning CDF-related objectives of the form \[ g(x)\cdot\mathbb{P}_{X\sim\mathcal{D}}(X\le x), \] over $[0,1]^2$, where $g$ is a known Lipschitz function and $\mathcal{D}$ is an unknown distribution. At each round $t$, the learner selects a point $x_t$ and observes the binary feedback $\mathbb{I}(X_t\le x_t)$, where $X_t\sim\mathcal{D}$. We design an algorithm achieving regret $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$, improving over the previous best-known bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{3/4})$ and showing that the curse of dimensionality can be at least partially lifted for this class of objectives, though a gap remains with the $\Omega(T^{2/3})$ lower bound. As an application, our techniques yield the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{7/10})$ regret bound for profit maximization in repeated bilateral trade with fixed prices.

</details>


### [167] [Interpretable Fuzzy Rule-Based Regression Extension for Ex-Fuzzy Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20277)
*Cayan Deniz Kucuktopana,Javier Fumanal-Idocin,Richard Pitts,Javier Andreu-Perez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ex-Fuzzy 库的回归扩展，通过引入基于模糊 C 均值（FCM）聚类的目标感知分区初始化策略，在增强的输入-输出空间中推导语言变量，实现 Mamdani 模糊推理与标量结论的直接学习。在 KEEL 仓库的 10 个回归数据集上评估表明，高斯分区始终优于均匀梯形分区，达到平均 R²≈0.86，且仅产生 10-15 条可读规则，为黑箱模型提供了透明且具竞争力的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型虽在回归任务中预测精度高，但其黑箱特性阻碍了在安全关键和受监管领域（如医疗诊断、金融风险评估、环境监测）的部署。模糊规则系统虽具语言可解释性优势，但 Mamdani 风格模糊回归在现代机器学习软件库中代表性不足。现有 Ex-Fuzzy 库仅支持分类任务，且 Python 生态中缺乏面向可解释性的模糊规则回归学习管线。

Method: 扩展 Ex-Fuzzy 库，新增三个组件：RuleBaseT1Regression、FitRuleBaseRegression 和 FCMMamdaniRegressor。核心方法包括：（1）修改基因编码，将分类的类索引替换为 [0,100] 整数编码的标量结论，并线性映射至目标范围；（2）采用加权平均去模糊化作为推理机制，无规则激活时回退至训练目标均值；（3）提出目标感知 FCM 分区初始化策略，在增强空间 [X, α·ỹ]（α 默认 2.5）上聚类，将语言项放置于输出相关输入空间区域，构建高斯隶属函数（中心来自聚类质心，宽度由类内散布确定并经 sigma_scale 全局缩放）；（4）作为对比，实现基于均匀间距的约束梯形分区策略；（5）使用 pymoo 遗传算法以验证集 R² 为适应度优化紧凑规则库，并剪除低质量规则。

Result: 在 KEEL 仓库 10 个回归数据集（样本量 34-32614，特征数 2-26）上，使用与 MLP（41-281 参数）、随机森林（约 80 叶子节点）相当的复杂度配置进行对比。高斯分区实现平均 R²=0.86，优于梯形分区的 0.71（相对提升 21%），并超过 MLP（0.80）、随机森林（0.78）和线性回归（0.65）。高斯分区在全部 10 个数据集上均优于梯形分区，差距在 anacalt（0.94 vs 0.54）、pole（0.88 vs 0.56）、friedman（0.77 vs 0.59）尤为显著。在 MLP 失败的小样本数据集 diabetes（R²≈-0.28）上，模糊方法仍能达到 R²≈0.53。规则示例显示模型捕捉到直观的领域关系（如老旧高排量高重量车辆油耗低），加速特征（x3）只在一条高斯规则中出现，提示其预测价值有限。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了 Ex-Fuzzy 库从分类到回归的扩展，验证了基于 FCM 目标感知高斯分区的 Mamdani 模糊回归能以仅 10-15 条人类可读规则达到与神经网络和集成方法相当的预测精度，同时保持完全可解释性。未来工作将探索 Type-2 模糊系统以增强不确定性处理、引入减法聚类等替代分区初始化方法、研究校准驱动的量化不确定性，并将该扩展贡献至官方 Ex-Fuzzy 仓库以促进复用和社区发展。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve high predictive accuracy in regression tasks, but their deployment in safety-critical and regulated domains requires interpretability. While fuzzy rule-based systems offer transparent, linguistically explicit interpretable models, Mamdani-style fuzzy regression remains underrepresented in modern machine learning software libraries. This paper presents an interpretable regression extension for the Ex-Fuzzy library, enabling Mamdani fuzzy inference with scalar consequents learned directly from data. For this, a target-aware partition initialisation strategy based on Fuzzy C-Means clustering is introduced, in which linguistic variables are derived from an augmented input-output space to emphasise output-relevant regions of the feature space. The proposed extension is evaluated on ten regression datasets from the KEEL repository, comparing Gaussian and trapezoidal partition strategies against standard baselines including linear regression, multilayer perceptron, and random forests. Experimental results show that Gaussian partitions consistently outperform uniform trapezoidal partitions, achieving a mean coefficient of determination of approximately 0.86 while producing compact rule bases of 10-15 human-readable rules. The proposed implementation provides a transparent and competitive alternative to black-box regression models, supporting practical interpretability with competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [168] [The Blessing of Dimensionality: How Near-Orthogonality in High-Dimensional Spaces Explains Temporal Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20301)
*Abigail Woodring,Adrian Chan,Rana Muhammad Shahroz Khan,Sukwon Yun,Chau-Wai Wong,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 PortLLM 方法的长期时间可移植性（即在持续预训练多个时间步后，旧 LoRA patch 仍能保持有效性），通过大规模实证研究和两种理论分析，揭示高维空间中向量的近正交性是 PortLLM 时间可移植性的根本原因，将"维度诅咒"转化为"维度祝福"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PortLLM（Khan et al., 2025）是一种无需训练和数据即可使旧 LoRA patch 适配持续预训练后基础模型的方案，但先前研究仅涉及短期（至多4步）验证且缺乏理论解释。本文旨在：（1）扩展实证研究至10个持续预训练时间步，并涵盖多种基础模型与重复实验；（2）从理论上解释为何简单的 PortLLM 方法能取得与逐步微调（逐步再训练 patch）相媲美的性能。三个研究问题分别为：PortLLM 与逐步微调的代价差异（RQ1）、PortLLM 与不打 patch 的优势差异（RQ2）、PortLLM 跨时间步的性能变化（RQ3）。

Method: 方法包含两部分：**(1) 实证研究**——在 Mistral-7B、Gemma3-12B（用 rank=64/128 的高秩 LoRA 近似全预训练）和 Qwen2-0.5B（全预训练）上，对 Fineweb（10个时间块）和 Cosmopedia（8个时间块）进行持续预训练，并在 WinoGrande、BoolQ、ARC-Easy/Challenge、GSM8k、MNLI、SST2 等下游任务上测试 PortLLM、逐步微调和不打 patch 三种策略，各做3次重复。**(2) 理论分析**——提出两种方法：①**一维损失景观切片法**：沿连接两个 patch 的直线定义标量损失函数 g(α)，通过 Taylor 定理将三种性能差异（C_PortLLM、B_PortLLM、V_PortLLM）紧凑表示为"位移-梯度-位移积"（单个内积），并利用 Lemma 1 的上界（由 alignment 和 sharpness 两项刻画）解释其大小；②**基于持续预训练优化步长的迭代分析法**：通过 Taylor 展开和一阶近似，利用准正交性假设（Assumption 1：预训练梯度与微调梯度 ε-准正交）证明 V_PortLLM 的上界随时间步线性增长 It(ηε_orth ℓ_pt ℓ_ft + ε_η)，其中关键参数 ε_orth 因高维空间随机向量近正交而极小。

Result: **实证结果**：（i）Mistral-7B 在 Fineweb 上，PortLLM 与逐步微调性能几乎重合（RQ1 代价可忽略），显著优于不打 patch（在 t=10 时 WinoGrande 提升11个百分点、BoolQ 提升7个、ARC-Easy 提升4个、ARC-Challenge 提升6个，RQ2 优势明显），且跨10个时间步性能近似平稳（统计检验仅12例中3例显著，斜率 ≤ 10⁻³，RQ3 变化小）；（ii）该结论在 Gemma3-12B、Qwen2-0.5B 及 Cosmopedia 数据上一致成立。**理论结果**：（i）三种性能差异均可表示为单一内积，C 和 V 因对应向量近正交而小，B 因沿优化路径景观陡峭而大；（ii）内积 g'_c(α) 的经验值远小于其范数乘积 ||∇ℓ||·||Δ||，证实是近正交性（而非小范数）导致 ε 小；（iii）随机方向上的损失切片验证了逐步微调 patch 处于平坦区域，supporting sharp 与 alignment 两项均小。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统证明 PortLLM 的 LoRA patch 在10步持续预训练下的长期时间可移植性，揭示高维空间向量的近正交性是 PortLLM 有效性的根本原因——预训练梯度与微调梯度方向相互独立，使得持续预训练对下游损失影响甚微。该分析方法（特别是高维正交性论证）具有普适性，可推广至 CAR-LoRA 等其他 LoRA 变体，为理解 PEFT 方法的鲁棒性提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has been widely used to adapt large language models (LLMs) for domain-specific tasks. Parameter efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are frequently used to reduce computational costs. PortLLM is a training-free and data-free scheme used to adapt LLMs after continual pretraining. Although the initial PortLLM results show that LoRA patches exhibit short-term temporal portability, the long-term performance of PortLLM across several updates of continual pretraining remains underexplored. Furthermore, the intriguing effectiveness of PortLLM is not well understood from a theoretical standpoint. We address these two open questions by (1) performing an extensive empirical study of the long-term temporal portability of PortLLM patches across 10 continual pretraining steps using base models Mistral, Gemma, and Qwen; and (2) offering two theoretical analyses to explain our observation that the simple PortLLM method achieves competitive performance. We find empirically that the portability persists across longer time duration, indicating that repeated fine-tuning is not required when the base model is periodically updated. We find theoretically that near-orthogonality of high-dimensional vectors is a key justification for temporal portability. Our analyses also demonstrate a geometric perspective of the loss landscape in facilitating the theoretical comparison of different adaptation options.

</details>


### [169] [Classical Hardware Acceleration of Quantum Autoencoders for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20302)
*Ivan Ge,Sagar Addepalli,Abhilasha Dave,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于对撞机实验中实时异常检测触发器的量子自编码器模型,提出了量子-经典混合VAE和全量子自编码器(QAE)两种架构,并首次将QML模型综合部署到FPGA上,在Alveo U200上实现单事件推理延迟低于1微秒(QAE),且性能与SOTA经典方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC等现代对撞机实验每秒产生数十TB数据,需要多级触发系统在40 MHz事件率下进行实时过滤;传统信号假设驱动搜索难以覆盖未知的BSM物理,异常检测(AD)成为新前沿。QML能高效表征高维长程高阶关联且参数更少,有望在低延迟硬件加速器(FPGA)上部署到L1触发系统中,但缺乏QML在FPGA上实际综合与可行性评估的研究。

Method: 采用LHC模拟的pp碰撞数据集(4M QCD背景+4类BSM信号:A→4ℓ、leptoquark→bτ、h±→τν、h⁰→ττ),每个事件用56个实值特征描述19个重建对象,按"一粒子一量子比特"策略将动量(p_T)、赝快度(η)、方位角(ϕ)映射到单量子比特旋转门R_X(η)R_Y(p_T)R_Z(ϕ)。为适配FPGA资源,将19量子比特事件寄存器按粒子类型分块(1+4+4+5+5),并通过量子互信息将10个喷注拆为两个独立的5量子比特子块以减少态向量存储。提出两种模型:(1)量子-经典混合VAE,用块结构PQC替代编码器第一层,提取32维量子特征向量后接入经典dense层映射到3维高斯潜空间,使用CKL异常得分,含87个量子参数+630个经典参数;(2)全QAE,完全去除经典层,通过CRX环形纠缠器和trash-wire激发概率(z得分取最大两个正值之和)直接输出异常得分,仅含23个量子参数。使用PennyLane+PyTorch在A100 GPU上训练,后经HLS综合至Alveo U200 FPGA(5.5 ns时钟),采用ap_fixed定点格式,通过agentic AI工具优化资源与延迟。

Result: 物理性能:两种模型在所有4类BSM信号上的AUC和TPR@10⁻⁵均与SOTA经典方法具有竞争力甚至更优;QAE因训练目标和得分构造聚焦低FPR尾部,整体AUC略低但TPR@10⁻⁵保持竞争力。定点量化扫描显示,混合VAE在W≤14时因编码器累加溢出导致性能崩塌,W≥18时AUC因饱和被人为抬高但TPR@10⁻⁵相对32位基线波动±5%;QAE在低位宽下得分分布严重量化但AUC和TPR@10⁻⁵均显示对低精度相对鲁棒。FPGA综合结果:混合VAE延迟6.1 μs,LUT 208976、DSP 1528、FF 48043;QAE延迟0.47 μs,LUT 249777、DSP 1234、FF 79429,均可在单个SLR内满足资源与时序约束。跨平台推理延迟对比显示,在batch=1(触发器运行点)时FPGA为最快,随batch增大多线程CPU和CUDA实现超过FPGA,符合FPGA在小量子寄存器仿真中的优势。

Conclusion: 量子自编码器(混合VAE与全QAE)能以少于100个可训练量子参数达到或超越SOTA异常检测能力,经量化与FPGA综合后满足未来对撞机触发器延迟要求(QAE<1 μs),且适配单个FPGA SLR。结果表明,通过经典FPGA仿真部署的量子机器学习有潜力补充经典方法用于实时探测器读出推理。未来工作包括:利用agentic AI协同优化量子门算法实现与HLS语句;探索混合量子门-张量网络实现,在保持性能的同时进一步降低FPGA资源占用。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) algorithms in high energy physics (HEP) can efficiently represent and leverage long-range, high-order correlations in high-dimensional collider data, potentially with fewer parameters and favorable scaling relative to classical models. Deployment of QML in real-time collider applications such as trigger systems requires the ability to emulate and compile quantum circuits classically, then synthesize the resulting quantum gates onto low-latency hardware accelerators, namely field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs). We present a study of variational quantum autoencoder models for real-time anomaly detection triggers in modern collider experiments. The models achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art classical approaches and, after FPGA synthesis, satisfy resource usage and timing constraints consistent with trigger applications in future colliders. This work provides one of the first FPGA implementations of QML models for HEP triggers, enabling higher-capability models in today's classical data acquisition pipelines while advancing quantum readiness of collider experiment infrastructure.

</details>


### [170] [Multi-modal transformer for signal classification in nanopore blockade experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20323)
*Sandro Kuppel,Julian Hoßbach,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态Transformer架构,通过联合处理原始时间序列、小波图像和静态特征向量三种信号表示形式,对纳米孔阻塞事件进行分类。该模型在42种肽段基准数据集上比现有最优方法提升超过10个百分点,并在20种氨基酸数据集上达到接近完美的准确率,展示了多模态融合在纳米孔单分子传感中的强大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米孔传感器能够通过检测离子电流变化识别单分子,但现有分类方法主要依赖静态特征(如停留时间和平均阻塞电流),忽略了信号中的时序相关性信息。不同信号表示(原始时间序列、小波变换图像、统计特征)在不同肽段类别上表现各异,单一模态无法充分提取最具判别力的特征,导致对细微化学差异(如翻译后修饰)的识别能力有限。

Method: 基于Vision Transformer框架扩展的多分支多模态Transformer架构,包含三个独立分支:1) 时间序列分支——将原始电流轨迹分段后通过线性层映射为token;2) 图像分支——对小波变换图像进行patch化处理;3) 特征分支——通过MLP处理catch22描述符。特征分支通过一次性交叉注意力将信息传递到其他两个分支,然后两个分支进入多分支Transformer编码器,通过标准Transformer块与跨分支交叉注意力的交替迭代进行信息融合。训练采用两阶段策略:先进行掩码自编码的预训练,再进行有监督微调。模型约2800万参数。

Result: 在42种肽段数据集上,多模态Transformer达到92.6%宏平均准确率和92.1%微平均准确率,比最佳单模态方法提升超过10个百分点,最差类别准确率提升近20个百分点,最佳类别达100%。在20种氨基酸XR7数据集上,宏平均准确率达99.0%,且通过迁移学习(预训练于肽段数据集后微调)收敛更快、分布更窄(最差类别仍>90%)。注意力分析揭示时间序列模型关注事件进入/退出阶段和中心深阻塞区,而小波模型则关注高频成分,两者关注不同特征。多模态模型整合了各模态在其擅长类别上的优势。

Conclusion: 多模态融合架构显著提升了纳米孔信号的分类性能,接近数据集标签噪声上限。模型在不同类别间表现出更均衡的准确率,提升了诊断可靠性。该架构具有灵活性,可扩展至任意数量的模态输入(如光电联合读出),且预训练特征可跨分析物迁移,为数据稀缺场景提供了基础模型的可能。局限性在于两个数据集均使用同一气溶素孔获得,跨孔型泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: Nanopore devices have emerged as powerful tools for single-molecule sensing, with potential for rapid, portable diagnostics. They detect changes in ionic current as analytes enter nanometer-scale pores, providing a means of identifying diverse biomarkers from their characteristic signal patterns. However, these signals are highly complex, and reliably assigning them to specific molecules remains a major challenge. Here, we address this by introducing a multi-modal deep learning architecture that jointly processes multiple signal representations, including raw time-series data, wavelet-based images, and static feature vectors. Our approach surpasses existing methods by more than 10 percentage points on a 42-peptide benchmark and transfers to a 20-amino-acid dataset with near-perfect accuracy. The model integrates complementary information from these representations, with attention analysis showing that the time-series and wavelet-image inputs emphasize different features of the same event. Together, these results demonstrate the potential of machine learning to enable robust, high-accuracy molecular identification with nanopore sensors.

</details>


### [171] [Interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANN and fPANN) for uncertainty quantification and propagation in constitutive modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20339)
*Somesh Pratap Singh,Govinda Anantha Padmanabha,Jingye Tan,Steven Yang,Reese E. Jones,D. Thomas Seidl,Nikolaos Bouklas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出区间与模糊物理增强神经网络（iPANN和fPANN），用于超弹性本构建模中的非概率性任意不确定性量化。通过学习稀疏的自由能密度下界、均值和上界分支，结合区间分析和模糊集理论（α-截集），实现对应力-变形数据的噪声包络，并通过有限元方法传播不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构建模在面对稀疏、噪声或异质数据时，不确定性量化（UQ）仍是核心瓶颈。概率性方法（如贝叶斯神经网络）需要难以验证的数据分布假设，且推理需蒙特卡洛采样。力学应用中常需要最坏/最好情况包络而非完整预测分布，且实验数据稀缺使分布假设难以成立。因此需要一种分布式自由（distribution-free）、物理一致且可解释的非概率UQ框架，同时支持下游有限元仿真的不确定性传播。

Method: 1. 理论基础：建立区间值自由能密度Ψ(F)=[Ψ下,Ψ上]及对应的应力包络（S=2∂Ψ/∂C），通过命题1证明主应力排序与能量排序的等价性（路径无关）；2. 模糊集与α-截集构造凸模糊集；3. 输入凸神经网络（ICNN）架构保证对所选不变量(I₁, I₂, J)的凸性，促进多凸性；4. 平滑L₀稀疏化（硬混凝土随机门控）获得可解释紧凑表达；5. iPANN通过MSE损失+稀疏正则+基于平滑Macaulay括号近似的边界损失学习三支自由能密度；6. fPANN通过分段线性插值三支实现α-截集隶属度映射；7. 两阶段迁移学习：先训练强L₀惩罚的均值模型，再以弱惩罚微调上下界网络。

Result: 在Gent-Gent模型生成的合成各向同性超弹性数据（500训练样本，经250,000 LHS+FPS-SA采样优选）上验证：1. 基线实验（E1）及噪声扰动实验（E2-E4，不同种子/均值/标准差）下，学习到的应力边界成功包络噪声观测，训练集与测试集一致；2. 边界宽度随噪声水平自适应调整；3. 经验隶属度分数b_emf在α→0时趋近100%，符合高斯残差的sigmoid特征；4. 有限元部署（FEniCS 15×15×15网格，20%轴向拉伸）三个势函数均产生光滑物理可接受的变形配置；5. 恢复出可解释闭式自由能表达（如E1均值：0.594I₁+0.452J+1.879·log(2.920·sp(0.400I₁-0.420J)^0.723·e^(-1.102I₂-2.010J)+1)+…）。

Conclusion: iPANN和fPANN框架为超弹性本构建模提供了轻量、物理一致且非概率的UQ替代方案，无需推理阶段蒙特卡洛采样，便于解释（稀疏闭式表达），可与有限元分析无缝对接。其中iPANN适用于确定性包络，fPANN通过α参数可调节保守性并以隶属度量化覆盖率。未来工作将拓展至全场DIC实验数据集成、多模态实验融合以及黏弹性/塑性/损伤等耗散机制下的路径相关不确定性传播。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling under uncertainty remains a central challenge for reliable mechanics simulations, particularly when the available stress-deformation data are sparse, noisy, or heterogeneous. We propose interval and fuzzy physics-augmented neural networks (iPANNs and fPANNs) for uncertainty-aware hyperelastic constitutive modeling. iPANNs learn sparse lower, mean, and upper free energy density branches whose stresses, obtained by automatic differentiation, ultimately enclose noisy stress observations. In contrast to this deterministic interval description, fPANNs embed the learned iPANN branches into a fuzzy-set representation through alpha-cut interpolation, yielding a nested family of admissible responses. iPANNs and fPANNs encode mechanistic constraints - preserving objectivity, consistency and promoting polyconvexity - and smoothed L0 regularization promotes interpretable energy representations. The bound models are trained through a two-stage transfer-learning procedure in which a sparse mean constitutive response is learned first and then fine-tuned into lower and upper energy branches. We evaluate the framework on synthetic isotropic hyperelastic data with heteroscedastic noise, varying random realizations, shifted noise means, and varying noise magnitudes. The results show that the learned bounds enclose noisy stress observations while generalizing to the test set. Further, we examine the propagation of uncertainty through the mean, upper and lower bound predictions of the learned iPANN models in a finite element setting. The proposed framework provides a compact, physics-consistent route for distribution-free aleatoric uncertainty quantification in hyperelastic constitutive modeling, and propagation in downstream finite element simulations.

</details>


### [172] [Variance-reduced Domain Adaptation using Paired Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20367)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PSDA（Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation），一种针对无监督域适应（UDA）中非可加性损失（MMD和CORAL）的新型随机方差缩减（SVR）方法。PSDA通过构建域内和跨域配对，形成四元组共采样，最小化梯度估计方差，且匹配过程归结为线性分配问题，可高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无监督域适应（UDA）中，CORAL和MMD等分布匹配损失在小批量优化中存在高方差问题，导致训练不稳定，甚至比不使用域对齐效果更差。经典SVR方法要求损失具有有限和结构，而MMD和CORAL缺乏该结构，因此需要专门的方差缩减技术。

Method: PSDA通过两阶段线性分配实现方差缩减：(1) 第一阶段在源域和目标域样本间进行跨域匹配，构建源-目标对的成本矩阵C^{st}；(2) 第二阶段将已匹配的源-目标对再次配对，形成四元组（每域两个样本），构建成本矩阵C^{MM}。成本矩阵基于条件期望梯度误差推导，可利用Welford方法高效计算协方差统计量。匹配后的四元组在训练中始终共采样，诱导样本间的正负相关依赖。

Result: 在仿真实验中，PSDA在小批量规模k较小时比现有方法（k-means++、DPP、VaRDASS、ORDERED等）方差降低最多达3个数量级；在Spawrious、Office-Home和Humpbacks三个域偏移基准上，PSDA平均准确率达到最高或次高（其中Spawrious上MMD+Double-paired达73.7%，Office-Home上CORAL+Double-paired达52.7%，Humpbacks上MMD+Double-paired达92.1%）；训练速度方面，PSDA在SVR技术中属于最快之一（如Office-Home上约600秒，显著快于ORDERED的2579秒）。

Conclusion: PSDA是一种高效、可即插即用的方差缩减采样方法，通过线性分配构造样本依赖关系，在计算成本、方差缩减程度和目标域精度三个维度均表现优异。未来工作可拓展到其他非可加性损失，或与其他SVR技术组合使用。

Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [173] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ARROW（Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting），是首个面向流式数据的、专门针对 MMD 和 CORAL 损失函数的在线随机方差缩减（SVR）算法，通过维护对齐统计量的指数加权移动平均（EWMA）参考并对输入小批量进行自适应重加权，实现了在线、分布式、增量学习场景下的方差缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 域偏移是机器学习模型实际部署中的核心挑战，无监督域适应（UDA）通过最小化源域与目标域之间的分布差异（如 MMD 和 CORAL 损失）来缓解该问题；2. 但在小批量优化设置下，这些差异损失估计噪声极大，常导致训练不稳定甚至表现不如不做域对齐；3. 现存 SVR 方法（如多样化采样、VaRDASS、ORDERED、PSDA）均为离线算法，依赖完整数据集的访问与周期性特征重提取，无法适配在线、分布式或增量学习场景，且在极大规模数据上计算开销难以承受。

Method: 1. 维护 EWMA 参考统计量：对 MMD 维护核均值嵌入差值的 EWMA，对 CORAL 同时维护均值与协方差矩阵的 EWMA（协方差更新中包含均值漂移修正项），以跟踪真实总体统计量随训练或分布漂移的变化；2. 自适应重加权：每个训练步通过求解 min‖D̂_w^m − D̃^m‖²，将小批量加权统计量对齐到 EWMA 参考，使小批量梯度隐式对齐于方差缩减后的"全量"梯度；3. MMD 优化：将加权核均值表示为 RKHS 嵌入的线性组合，得到关于权重 u、v 的凸二次优化问题；4. CORAL 优化：为避免四次（NP-hard）权重优化问题，提出基于外积线性组合的松弛方案，得到可解的线性最小二乘问题。

Result: 1. 估计方差（蒙特卡洛仿真）：ARROW 性能与 VaRDASS 相当，并在较小 k 值下略优于 VaRDASS；2. 目标域精度：在 Spawrious、Office-Home、Humpbacks 三个 DomainBed 基准上，ARROW 与离线 SOTA SVR 方法整体相当；其中在 Humpbacks 数据集上，CORAL+ARROW 取得 93.0% 的最佳平均准确率，MMD+ARROW 取得 92.7%，均超越多数离线方法；3. 训练速度：ARROW 运行时间与数据集大小完全无关，在中等规模数据集上具有竞争力，在极大规模数据场景下具有独特优势。

Conclusion: ARROW 是首个面向 MMD 和 CORAL 损失的在线 SVR 方法，框架基于实例重加权与指数加权移动平均，在方差缩减、训练速度和目标域性能上均与离线方法具有竞争力；未来工作可将该框架推广至 UDA 及其他场景中的更多损失函数。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [174] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PG-KINN（Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network）是一种用于求解正、逆偏微分方程（PDE）的物理信息神经网络框架。它将Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）作为试空间，与独立选取的、紧支撑的分段多项式测试空间相结合，通过Petrov-Galerkin弱形式和Gauss-Legendre求积法构建损失函数。该方法在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、异质介质反演参数识别及复杂几何等基准问题上均优于基于MLP的传统方法和KAN的强形式/能量形式/逆形式（PIKAN）等现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络（PINN）求解PDE时面临两大核心瓶颈：1）函数逼近器方面，以多层感知机（MLP）为主流，但存在频谱偏差（spectral bias）和参数密集、不可解释等问题；2）PDE损失形式方面，强形式需要高阶导数、损失权重需精细调节，能量形式（Bubnov-Galerkin）仅适用于自伴随算子且在反问题中会塌缩到零模量平凡解，边界积分形式依赖已知基本解。作者认为这两个设计决策（表示与度量）应协同进行，而现有方法将二者孤立考虑，导致各种失败模式。Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）凭借局部可学习的B样条激活函数，在结构上与有限元形函数类似，提供了更优的试空间，但需要与之匹配的弱形式度量方式才能充分发挥其优势。

Method: PG-KINN的核心方法包括：(1) 试空间采用KAN，以B样条激活函数提供局部、分段多项式的逼近能力，参数更少且可解释；(2) 测试空间采用独立于试空间的、紧支撑的分段多项式空间（包括节点hat函数和基于移位Legendre多项式的内部bubble函数），定义在背景网格剖分上；(3) 通过Petrov-Galerkin弱形式（试函数与测试函数独立选取）构造弱残差，分部积分降低对KAN的导数阶要求，同时适用于非自伴随、非线性及反问题；(4) 使用Gauss-Legendre求积法精确计算单元积分，并证明了弱残差对未知系数是仿射的，使反问题损失不会出现能量形式中的零模量塌缩现象（Proposition 1）；(5) 训练时采用Adam+L-BFGS优化，分别给出正向（Algorithm 1）和反演（Algorithm 2）算法流程。

Result: 在多项基准测试中PG-KINN均取得最佳精度：(1) Mode III裂纹问题相对L2误差为0.00081（vs PINN-MLP 0.06254、PIKAN_DEM 0.01117、PIKAN_BINN 0.00083）；(2) 中心孔板应力集中问题位移L2误差0.00035、Mises应力H1误差0.00160（均为最优）；(3) Neo-Hookean悬臂梁非线性大变形问题采用Gauss-Legendre求积时误差最低（0.00192），验证了多项式测试空间与GL求积的协同优势，相比Monte-Carlo方法精度提升一个数量级以上；(4) 异质热传导率反演识别（P/C/V类纹理）数据驱动误差分别降至21.1%/3.4%/12.9%，PDE反演误差为31.2%/5.5%/20.2%；(5) 复杂几何（Koch雪花）误差0.00950，优于PIKAN_CPINNs的0.01188。理论证明了PG反演损失不会塌缩到平凡零解。唯一不足是极端复杂几何（如花形域）下轴对齐的样条网格成为瓶颈，PG-KINN在花形问题上与PIKAN_BINN精度相当（均为0.00473）但未能超越。

Conclusion: PG-KINN通过Petrov-Galerkin方式耦合KAN样条试空间与固定多项式测试空间，统一了弱形式降低导数阶的优势与强形式的算子普适性，同时具备反问题鲁棒性（无零模量塌缩）。该方法在固体力学计算的多种基准问题上均优于MLP基线和KAN的强/能量/逆形式（PIKAN），成为AI驱动计算力学的稳健准确路径。未来方向包括：自适应等参精细化与共形映射以处理不规则边界、KAN网格的h-自适应细化、采用Chebyshev多项式等更高效的基函数以降低B样条计算开销、以及测试空间的对抗性/自适应选取与严格求积分析。极端复杂几何的样条网格适配仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Economic Evaluations of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19375)
*Alexander Wan,Stephane Hatgis-Kessell,Tomás Aguirre,Percy Liang,Rishi Bommasani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出 EconEvals，一个开源评估套件，用于衡量语言模型在美国劳动力经济中各类任务、工作活动和职业上的能力，并引入基于模拟的"暴露度"测量方法，以量化当前语言模型在各职业中可节省的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 基准测试与经济任务之间存在严重的分布错位：基准测试集中在数学和编码（占 39.7%），但相关职业仅占美国就业的 3.5%。经济学界对前沿 AI 的经济影响存在巨大分歧（从 Acemoglu 预测的 0.9–1.6% 到 Amodei 预测的 10–20% 增长），但缺乏将模型能力与劳动市场价值直接挂钩的评估工具。OpenAI 的 GDPval 仅覆盖 5% 的美国职业，且成本高昂。论文指出能力确定与经济影响不确定之间的矛盾源于基准与经济任务的脱节。

Method: 1. **数据构建**：基于 O*NET 职业分类体系（1,016 个职业→2,087 个详细工作活动 DWA→18,796 个任务），采用自顶向下方法。真实数据方面，从 4,499,105 条公开对话数据（WildChat、LMSYS 等）中，通过多阶段嵌入检索+语言模型分类流水线，以约 768 美元成本、0.91 精度映射到 143 个 DWA（覆盖 6.9%）。合成数据方面，通过角色扮演生成覆盖全部 2,087 个 DWA 的查询。

2. **基准测试**：在 226 个基准上评估 10 个模型（含 GPT-4.1、o3、Gemini-2.5-Pro、DeepSeek-V3.1 等），使用 LM 评判器进行打分比较，成本比 GDPval 降低 500 倍。

3. **白盒模拟暴露度测量**：创新性地采用"工人模拟"方法，将每个任务分解为步骤，模拟工人使用 LM 聊天机器人完成任务的流程，逐步估算基线时间和节省时间，最终聚合得到任务级暴露度估计，同时提供推理依据。

Result: 1. **基准结果**：公开数据中 gpt-oss-120B 表现最佳，经济基准上其领先优势更明显：在 37 个有显著差异的基准中，35 个由 gpt-oss-120B 领先。合成基准与真实基准的 Spearman 相关性为 0.67，与 GDPval 分数的相关性为 0.67，且优于大多数通用能力基准。

2. **暴露度结果**：当前语言模型可在 46.6% 的美国职业中至少一半任务上节省大量时间，但 79.4% 预测有显著时间节省的任务实际 Claude 使用率较低，表明使用率滞后于潜力。仿真法暴露度估计比传统评分法更保守（46.6% vs 68.7%）。

3. **瓶颈分析**：除固有的物理交互限制外，隐私约束和专有系统是限制 AI 时间节省的主要瓶颈。语言密集型技能（写作、阅读理解、批判性思维、口语）与中等暴露度相关性最强（R²=0.718），但仿真法在高暴露度上难以用技能解释（R²=0.085）。

Conclusion: EconEvals 提供了开放、可扩展的评估基础设施，桥接了 AI 能力与劳动市场影响之间的鸿沟。其统一基准测试和暴露度测量方法，可随技术进步和经济变化持续更新。未来工作应探索这些测量如何预测就业、工资和生产率等下游经济指标，并拓展至当前的 LM 聊天机器人以外的更广泛技术领域以及美国以外的劳动力市场。论文呼吁学术界利用该基础设施帮助实际工作者发现如何从当前语言模型中获得更多工作收益。

Abstract: Language models perform economically valuable work, yet they are not currently assessed for how well they perform every economically valuable task. We introduce EconEvals as an open-source evaluation suite to measure capabilities relevant to tasks, work activities, and occupations in the US labor economy. We ground the evaluation suite in real user queries to language models where possible, and supplement these with synthetic data. Our evaluations improve coverage over OpenAI's GDPval benchmark, which is the existing state-of-the-art that covers 5% of US occupations, at 500x lower cost. Alongside benchmarks, we also introduce a simulation-based exposure measure to estimate how much time current language model capabilities could save across all tasks belonging to all US occupations, with detailed accounting for each estimate. Our estimates indicate that current models could save workers substantial time on at least half of their tasks in 47% of occupations. However, for 79% of tasks where we predict substantial time savings, observed Claude usage is low, suggesting that existing usage lags potential. Beyond inherent constraints of language model chatbots, our data identifies privacy and proprietary systems as the principal bottlenecks limiting further time savings from AI. Overall, we introduce adaptable infrastructure that grounds inferences about language models' labor-market impact in their current capabilities, which can be continually updated as capabilities improve.

</details>


### [176] [Simulating Eutopia: Revisiting Long-term Fairness with Outcomes, Performativity, and Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19389)
*Vedant Palit,Udvas Das,Brahim Driss,Debabrota Basu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究AI决策者在信用借贷场景下的长期公平性问题，提出Eutopia仿真平台，结合performative马尔可夫决策过程框架，验证了考虑performative动态和公平感知效用函数能显著提升长期效率、公平性和包容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期公平性研究存在两大局限：(1)大多假设被动环境（决策不影响群体行为），不适用于信用借贷等主动决策场景；(2)用瞬时预测差异衡量偏差，而非下游财富公平。现代AI决策者（如信贷机构）的决策会主动改变人口行为并影响长期社会经济结果，因此需要新的框架来研究真正的长期公平。

Method: 1. 将信贷ADM与多人口群体的交互形式化为performative Markov Decision Process（MDP），包含ADM级和社会结果级奖励函数；2. 开发Eutopia借贷过程仿真器，内含新颖的performative数据生成器，可学习长期公平策略；3. 在Eutopia上测试performative RL算法与经典RL算法，对比公平感知效用与功利效用。

Result: 实验表明：(a) 引入performative动态学习的算法在长期效率和公平性上均优于传统方法；(b) 基于社会结果设计的公平感知效用函数在效率、公平性和包容性三个维度上均带来更优表现。

Conclusion: 在AI决策者的长期公平性研究中，必须同时考虑performative动态（即决策对环境的主动影响）和基于社会结果而非瞬时预测的公平度量。本文提出的Eutopia框架为长期公平策略学习提供了有效的测试平台。

Abstract: As AI-driven Decision Makers (ADMs) influence our socioeconomic reality, their roles in both enhancing efficiency and amplifying the social biases have drawn attention. In this paper, we revisit the nuances of long-term `fairness' achievable by an ADM, specifically in the context of a credit lending induced wealth process. The literature on long-term fairness mostly (a) considers passive environments, i.e. the outcome of a predictor does not change the population's behaviour, and (b) measures bias in terms of disparity in instantaneous predictions rather than the downstream equity. These are not true for modern ADMs, like credit lenders. To address these caveats, we first formalise the wealth dynamics induced by a loan approving ADM interacting with a multi-demographic population as a performative Markov Decision Process with ADM level and social outcome level reward functions. Then, we mitigate the absence of such a performative test-bed by developing Eutopia: a lending-process simulator enabled with a novel performative data generator to learn long-term fair strategies. Finally, we test performative and classical RL algorithms with different fairness-aware and utilitarian utilities. Experimental results show that (a) learning with performative dynamics lead to better long-term efficiency and equity, and (b) learning with well-designed fairness-aware utility evaluated on social outcomes induces better efficiency, equity, and inclusivity.

</details>


### [177] [Did Alice Do Wrong? Cross-Cultural Differences in Student Perceptions of Generative AI Use in University Computing Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19699)
*Brian Harrington,Irina Zlotnikova,Gayathri Nadarajan,Samuel Ekundayo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究比较了加拿大和韩国大学生对生成式AI（GenAI）在学术中使用（特别是AI辅助编程）的伦理认知差异，发现加拿大学生比韩国学生更倾向于认为使用GenAI是不道德和违反机构政策的，且AI生成代码的占比是影响伦理判断的最强因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在高等教育中的广泛应用，围绕学术诚信和伦理使用的争论日益激烈。现有研究多集中于西方（尤其是北美）语境，缺乏跨文化比较。鉴于全球高等教育的多元性，理解不同文化背景下学生对AI伦理的认知差异对于制定公平、有效的AI使用政策至关重要。

Method: 采用情景式问卷调查（scenario-based survey），于2024年秋季学期在加拿大和韩国两所大学进行。通过让学生对功能相同但AI使用方式不同的编程作业情景进行伦理性和合规性评判，使用Mann-Whitney U检验和相关系数等统计方法分析跨文化差异，并通过Hofstede文化维度框架（包括权力距离、个人主义、不确定性回避等）解释结果。

Result: 1. 加拿大学生在几乎所有情景中均比韩国学生更倾向于将GenAI使用判断为不道德和违反机构政策；2. 尽管两国机构政策功能上相同，但学生感知存在显著差异；3. AI生成代码在作业中的占比是影响伦理判断的最强因素；4. 文化因素（权力距离、个人主义、不确定性回避）显著塑造了学生对GenAI的伦理推理。

Conclusion: GenAI在高等教育中的伦理整合必须具有文化响应性（culturally responsive），充分考虑不同文化对学术诚信的不同理解。研究呼吁制定对本地文化敏感、同时维护学术诚信基本原则的细致AI使用指南，并强调需要持续的跨文化研究来为全球高等教育中的AI伦理政策提供依据。

Abstract: The rise of generative AI (GenAI) in higher education has prompted urgent debates surrounding academic integrity and ethical use. This study examines cross-cultural differences in student perceptions of GenAI use, comparing responses from students at Canadian and South Korean universities. Using a scenario-based survey administered in Fall 2024, we analyzed how students judged the ethicality and rule compliance of AI-assisted coding practices. Results reveal that Canadian students were consistently more likely to perceive the use of GenAI as both unethical and against institutional policies compared to Korean students, despite functionally identical institutional policies. Statistical analysis, including Mann-Whitney U tests and correlation coefficients, demonstrated significant differences across nearly all scenarios. Analysis of the factors used in generating scenarios indicated that the amount of AI-generated code incorporated into assignments most strongly influenced ethical judgments. Findings were interpreted through Hofstede's cultural dimensions framework, suggesting that cultural factors such as power distance, individualism, and uncertainty avoidance significantly shape students' ethical reasoning regarding GenAI. Our results contribute to the growing body of evidence emphasizing that equitable AI integration in education must be culturally responsive, taking into account diverse conceptions of academic integrity. We advocate for the development of nuanced AI-use guidelines that are sensitive to local cultural contexts while upholding fundamental principles of academic honesty. This study highlights the need for ongoing cross-cultural research to inform ethical AI policies and support responsible GenAI use in global higher education settings.

</details>


### [178] [Are Attributions of Consciousness to AI Chatbots Epistemically Innocent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20001)
*Uwe Peters*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对人们向AI聊天机器人（如ChatGPT）赋予"意识"的言论进行概念分析，构建了一个多维度态度分类体系，涵盖从非信念性立场（如假装）到包括妄想在内的不同信念形式，并论证尽管部分意识归因在认识论上无害甚至无辜，但许多归因使归因者负有认识论上的过错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人能够以高度类人化的方式交流，促使众多用户对其赋予包括"意识"在内的心理属性。然而，现有科学证据并不足以支持当前聊天机器人真正具备意识。这引发了一系列关键的认识论问题：这些意识归因究竟是隐喻性的表述，还是表达了一种真正的信念？若这些归因缺乏证据支持，归因者是否在认识论上应受责备，还是说此类归因可能在认识论上是无害的，并带来其他途径无法获得的显著益处？论文旨在通过概念分析厘清这些问题。

Method: 论文采用概念分析（conceptual analysis）的方法，构建了一个关于AI聊天机器人意识归因的多维度态度分类体系（taxonomy）。该分类体系将人们表达的意识归因按照其底层态度进行划分，从非信念性立场（non-doxastic stances，如假装/pretence）到不同程度的信念形式（包括妄想/delusions），从而揭示语言形式相同的意识归因实际上可能反映截然不同的态度和认识论承诺程度。

Result: 论文提出了一个多维度的意识归因态度分类体系，其核心贡献在于：1）避免了将语言学上相同的意识归因混为一谈，揭示出它们可能反映本质上不同的态度；2）明确了不同态度对应着对"聊天机器人具有意识"这一命题不同程度的认识论承诺；3）为实证研究提供了一个操作性框架，使研究者能够对AI意识的不同形式认识论承诺进行操作化定义与测量。

Conclusion: 通过所构建的分类体系，论文得出以下结论：尽管部分对聊天机器人的意识归因在认识论上是无害的（epistemically benign），某些非理性的归因也可能在认识论上是无辜的（epistemically innocent），并可能带来别处无法获得的收益，但许多其他形式的意识归因则使归因者在认识论上应受责备（epistemically blameworthy）。这一结论为评估AI意识归因的合理性提供了精细化的判断依据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) chatbots (e.g., ChatGPT) can communicate in strikingly humanlike ways. This has prompted many chatbot users to attribute psychological properties, including consciousness, to these systems. However, there is little scientific evidence that current AI chatbots are conscious. How, then, should we understand people's consciousness attributions to chatbots? Are they merely metaphorical claims, or do they express genuine beliefs? If these attributions lack evidential support, are users epistemically blameworthy for making them, or might they be epistemically innocent, yielding significant benefits otherwise unattainable? This paper offers a conceptual analysis of consciousness attributions to AI chatbots and develops a multidimensional taxonomy of the attitudes they may express, ranging from non-doxastic stances (e.g., pretence) to different forms of belief, including delusions. This taxonomy helps avoid conflations by showing that linguistically identical attributions can reflect importantly different attitudes and degrees of epistemic commitment to the proposition that chatbots are conscious. The taxonomy also provides a framework for empirical studies to operationalize and measure different forms of epistemic commitment to AI consciousness. Using this taxonomy, I argue that although some consciousness attributions to chatbots are epistemically benign, and even some irrational ones may be epistemically innocent, many others render the attributor epistemically blameworthy.

</details>


### [179] [AI-Increased Talent Retention Strategies: Fostering Long-Term Employee Engagement and Development in Talent Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19733)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI（人工智能）在人才管理中的应用，特别是如何通过预测分析、情感分析和个性化职业规划等工具来改善人才保留（TR）、员工敬业度和可持续的人力资源管理，并讨论相关伦理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着组织面临人才流失和员工敬业度下降的挑战，传统的人才管理策略难以有效应对。AI技术的兴起为组织提供了新的工具来解决这些问题，但同时也带来了伦理方面的担忧。因此，有必要系统性地研究AI如何影响人才保留过程，并提出可持续的实施框架。

Method: 基于系统性文献综述（Systematic Review），综合分析现有研究中关于AI在人才管理中的应用，探讨预测分析、情感分析、个性化学习计划等具体工具的实施方式，并提出一个帮助组织利用AI改进人才管理实践的框架。

Result: 研究阐述了AI如何影响人才保留过程，包括通过预测模型识别可能离职的员工、降低流失率、培养忠诚员工队伍；同时提出了一个支持长期可持续增长的AI驱动人才管理框架，帮助行业从业者和决策者理解新兴的技术变革。

Conclusion: AI能够有效降低员工流失率、创造忠诚的劳动力并促进可持续的人力资源管理，但在实施过程中需要解决隐私和算法公平性等伦理问题。通过构建可持续和包容的生态系统，可以支持未来劳动力发展，但必须谨慎平衡技术效益与伦理考量。

Abstract: The integration of AI in Talent Management is a change in the way that organizations are designing their strategies for Talent Retention (TR), engagement, and future strategy. New and innovative tools such as predictive models, sentiment analysis, and personalized career planning have come up, and they offer better ways of addressing retention issues, workforce engagement, and, in general, sustainability. Through the application of predictive analytics, organizations can determine employees' likelihood of leaving the organization, who is likely to leave, and when to act, thus minimizing the costs and time associated with the recruitment process and improving performance. Furthermore, AI solutions help the development of individualized learning plans that help to define employees' professional goals and link them with the organization's strategy to encourage the employees' continuous growth. This paper explained how AI is impacting the retention process and how it can be used to decrease attrition rates, create a loyal workforce, and promote sustainable management of human and other resources. Furthermore, the author discusses the ethical issues, such as privacy and fairness of algorithms, that are involved in the implementation of AI systems. Thus, the above challenges can be solved by developing a sustainable and inclusive ecosystem that can help in the development of the future workforce. Based on a systematic review of literature, the study presents a framework that can help organizations improve their talent management practices with the help of AI to support long-term sustainable growth. It can be used to help industry professionals and decision-makers understand the new technological shifts that are occurring.

</details>


### [180] [What Does the Credential Still Certify? Cognitive Stewardship for AI-Mediated Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19988)
*Kai Yao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知管理"（cognitive stewardship）框架,将AI介导的教育评估问题重新定义为"教育委托"问题,并通过对30所大学公开AI评估政策的实证审计,揭示当前高校政策在"分类AI使用"方面优于"解释凭证有效性"方面的结构性失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在动摇教育评估的基本前提:提交的作品能否可靠地证明凭证所声称的人类能力。核心问题不在于学生是否使用了AI,而在于当部分认知工作被委托给系统后,关于学习的推断还剩多少有效性。当前高校政策多停留在"许可/禁止"的分类层面,缺乏对证据标准和保护机制的明确规定,导致凭证有效性面临危机。

Method: 1. 概念框架建构:提出"教育委托"(educational delegation)概念和"认知管理"框架,包含四个对齐要素——学习主张、委托边界、证据标准和保障措施。2. 实证政策审计:选取英、美、澳、新、加五国30所大学的公开AI评估指南,使用预先指定的评分编码手册,采用四个开源权重大语言模型作为结构化编码员进行评分,以平均值降低单一模型偏差。分析维度包括学习主张、委托边界、证据标准、八项保障措施,以及六类情景压力测试。
