Conclusion: 库依赖(API、命名约定、自动化、本地辅助引理)是跨ITP翻译最大的可测量难度来源,远超逻辑基础差异;类型检查作为可执行验收信号是必要但远不充分的,需要BEq等语义等价感知度量;陈述翻译是证明翻译的近必要条件但远不充分;未来的跨ITP翻译系统应优先考虑检索、库映射、API对齐与证明风格适应,而非仅仅改进基础逻辑层面的转换;多ITP上下文对自动形式化有潜在增益但尚需更大规模受控消融验证;基准、验证基础设施与评测管线已公开发布,数据(jiayi005/ITPEval)与代码(lean-dojo/ITPEval)均可复现。

Abstract: Formal theorem proving has emerged as a frontier challenge for machine learning, yet the ecosystem is fragmented: proofs remain siloed across incompatible systems, limiting both training data for learning-based provers and the portability of verified results. We present ITPEval, the first benchmark for evaluating automated formal proof translation across four major ITPs (Lean 4, Rocq, Isabelle, and HOL Light), spanning two distinct logical foundations. Our benchmark comprises 1,560 source files and 6,848 theorems organized into a controlled tier of axiomatized files that isolates foundational translation difficulty, and an ecosystem tier drawn from real libraries that exposes API and proof-style mismatches. We release itpeval, a unified multi-ITP verification infrastructure with state-isolated warm backends that preserve per-artifact native checking semantics. We evaluate both statement and proof translation across five frontier and open-weight LLMs on 12 directed translation pairs: statement translation peaks at 29.1% pass@1 and proof translation at 10.5%; controlled theorems reach 29.7% proof pass@1 versus 5.2% for ecosystem-level translations, confirming that library mismatch is the dominant bottleneck. In addition to pass@k evaluation, a deterministic Lean 4 BEq check establishes equivalence for 54.0% of verified source-to-Lean 4 miniF2F statement translations, showing that native type-checking alone can substantially overestimate semantic fidelity; in an autoformalization/auto-informalization round-trip study, Rocq and HOL Light are easier formalization targets than Lean 4 and Isabelle, while multi-ITP context improves pooled Lean 4 success from 4.8% to 10.6%. Our benchmark, verification infrastructure, and evaluation pipelines are publicly released.

</details>


### [207] [FORCE-Bench: A Benchmark, Dataset, and Evaluation Harness for Agentic AI in Enterprise Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19409)
*Wolfgang M. Pauli,Sarah Panda,Kidus Admassu,Said Bleik,Ademola Okerinde,Jeremy Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FORCE-Bench 是微软发布的面向企业运营金融场景的智能体（agentic AI）评估基准，包含 251 条专家标注查询、基于评分细则（rubric）的八维度评估框架、开源评测工具与代码，覆盖三类任务（财务义务研究 ERP 查询、财务实体绩效研究公开数据问答、商业简报生成），用于在统一工具访问与延迟约束下对比专用与通用智能体的金融领域表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM/智能体基准（如 MMLU、AgentBench、τ-Bench、FinBen、FinanceBench 等）侧重通用能力、指令遵循、安全性或公开文档问答，缺乏对运营金融工作流（尤其是 ERP 内部的 AR/AP 数据）的端到端评估，且多采用二元成功/失败指标，无法反映金融从业者所要求的可验证性、合规性、引用质量、时效性、结构化等专业化质量维度。FORCE-Bench 旨在填补"工作流导向、可诊断、面向部署约束"的金融智能体评估空白。

Method: 三项支柱：(1) 查询数据集——251 条专家标注查询，涵盖 100 条 Dynamics 365 Finance 合成 ERP 环境下的 AR/AP 查询、126 条基于 SEC 文件与市场数据的上市公司财务查询、25 条多源公司情报简报请求；(2) 评分框架——八维度（准确性、引用、清晰度、深度、依据性、时效性、相关性、结构性），每维度由一组可测试断言组成，由 GPT-5.2 作为 LLM 裁判（DSPy 实现）对每条断言给出 0.0–1.0 连续评分；(3) 智能体评测工具与统计方法——评估对象包括专用 Finance Agent（Microsoft 365 Copilot）、Anthropic Claude CLI（Opus 4.7 与 Haiku 4.5）、OpenAI Responses API（GPT 5.5），在统一工具访问（web 搜索、ERP MCP 服务器）与延迟约束（60 s/300 s）下运行；采用线性混合模型（LMM）以问题为随机效应、Wald 检验做显著性推断并以 1000 次自助法给出 95% CI。

Result: 专用 Finance Agent 在所有八个维度上均领先，总体得分 0.80，显著高于 GPT 5.5（0.67）、Claude Opus 4.7（0.63）和 Claude Haiku 4.5（0.55）；优势最显著的是财务义务研究的准确性（0.77 vs 0.40–0.67）与财务实体研究的引用、结构、清晰度、相关性、深度（多维度领先幅度约 0.20–0.40，p<0.001）。商业简报上各系统引用得分接近天花板（0.99–1.00），但 Finance Agent 的时效性弱于通用系统。在 60 s 延迟约束下，通用系统性能大幅退化（如 GPT 5.5 准确性 -0.26、深度 -0.29、结构 -0.33），且出现 Claude Opus 因响应更慢被超时截断而落后于 Haiku 的"反转"现象；放松至 300 s 并提高推理强度后差距收窄。裁判偏差分析显示 GPT-5.2 与 Claude Opus 4.6 在排名一致性上稳健，但存在约 -0.04 至 -0.13 的系统校准偏移。

Conclusion: 面向运营金融的工作流导向、多维度评分、在统一工具与延迟约束下的评估，对理解智能体在企业部署中的实际可用性至关重要；专用 Finance Agent 因深度 ERP 集成在准确性上获得显著优势，而通用前沿模型在时间充裕、推理强度更高的条件下质量接近但延迟代价大，推理强度与超时预算存在交互权衡。未来工作将扩展至多轮对话、更多金融子领域（ treasury、FP&A、监管报告）以及针对公开数据任务的准确性测量方法。数据集、评分细则、评测工具与分析代码均已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated deployment of agentic systems in operational finance. Existing benchmarks emphasize measuring general capabilities, instruction following, or safety, but few directly address the operational finance workflows that agentic systems are now being deployed to automate. Finance professionals require agents to not only provide factually sound and properly grounded information, but also ensure that this information is verifiable and consistently adheres to rules and constraints of the operational finance domain. We introduce FORCE-Bench, which contains 251 expert-annotated queries and evaluates responses using a rubric-based framework calibrated to the requirements of the operational finance domain, across eight dimensions: accuracy, citations, clarity, depth, groundedness, recency, relevance, and structure. FORCE-Bench assesses agentic systems on three task types: financial obligation research (querying ERP systems for accounts receivable and payable data), financial entity performance research (answering time-bound questions from public filings and market data), and business brief generation (synthesising multi-source company intelligence reports). To reflect real deployment conditions, we evaluate our purpose-built agent, as well as the general-purpose agentic systems, under common tool access and latency-bounded settings. Results show that general-purpose agentic systems do not consistently meet finance-domain quality requirements under operational constraints, while the purpose-built Finance Agent for Microsoft 365 Copilot is more reliable across dimensions. We release the dataset, rubrics, harness, and analysis code as open-source to support reproducible comparison and adaptation to other enterprise finance environments.

</details>


### [208] [The Chronos Vulnerability: A Taxonomy of Temporal Persistence and Memory-Based Deception in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19433)
*Om Narayan,Ramkinker Singh,Praveen Baskar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Chronos 漏洞"概念，系统性定义并分类了针对有状态自主 AI 智能体的时序持久性与记忆欺骗类安全威胁，包括记忆注入攻击(MINJA)、休眠代理、回话走私与 EchoLeak 零点击泄露等，并基于 BDI 认知架构构建攻击-防御双层分类法，提出融合 AgentDoG 轨迹护栏、Agent-C 形式时序验证、A-MemGuard 免疫共识、GPU 可信执行环境(TEE)与零信任记忆架构的纵深防御体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 从无状态生成模型向有状态自主智能体转变，持续记忆、递归规划与工具调用带来了新的威胁面。传统面向会话的提示注入防御与端点内容过滤器无法应对潜伏于智能体内部信念系统中、可在数周乃至数月后被激活的持久性攻击。研究动机在于形式化此类"解耦攻击向量与灾难事件"的时序持久威胁，并量化智能体在企业级工作流中的"动态盲区"(Dynamics Blindness)，为构建可验证、可追溯的智能体安全基座提供理论基础。

Method: 研究采用威胁建模与实证分析相结合的方法：(1)基于 BDI(Belief-Desire-Intention)认知架构，将攻击面映射到情节/语义/程序/事实/工作记忆五层分层结构；(2)定义灰盒攻击者威胁模型，刻画查询访问与上下文注入能力及注入成功率(ISR)等参数；(3)使用 World of Workflows(WoW)基准对比通用基准 AgentBench，量化表征差距、动态差距与可观测性差距三类性能衰减；(4)综合分类 MINJA 的"指示提示-桥接步骤-渐进缩短策略(PSS)"三段式攻击流、欺骗性工具对齐与多智能体阴影逃逸；(5)梳理纵深防御谱系：AgentDoG(轨迹级诊断归因)、Agent-C(DSL→FOL→SMT 求解器的形式时序合规校验)、A-MemGuard(人工免疫系统共识验证)、GPU TEE(机密计算)+ MemTrust(纪元密钥/垃圾密钥密码学遗忘)等。

Result: 实证结果显示：(1)前沿大模型在通用 AgentBench 上 TSR 为 35.2–42.4%，但在 WoW 约束环境下 TSRUC 崩溃至 2.0–6.0%，平均相对性能下降 87.6%，GPT-4 单模型相对降幅达 90.9%，证实动态盲区的严重性；(2)AgentDoG-FG-4B 在 ATBench 风险源识别任务上达到 82.0% 准确率；(3)Agent-C 将 GPT-5 形式合规率从 83.7% 提升至 100%；(4)基于 NVIDIA H100 的 GPU TEE 对 Llama-3.1-70B 吞吐量开销约 2%，但若 NUMA 布局与 PCIe 加密未优化则延迟开销升至 20–100%；(5)EchoLeak(CVE-2025-32711)作为零点击间接提示注入典型案例，验证了 LLM 作用域违规的现实危害。

Conclusion: 论文结论认为，智能体开发中"凭感觉编程"(vibe-coding)的模式在安全层面不可持续。Chronos 漏洞(涵盖 MINJA、EchoLeak、Sleeper Agent 等)表明企业级自主系统存在系统性风险，其根源在于动态盲区与内部状态的不可质疑持久性。研究主张沿"形式逻辑+硬件信任"双轴构建安全记忆护城河：纵深集成 AgentDoG 轨迹诊断、Agent-C 时序形式验证、A-MemGuard 免疫记忆共识、GPU TEE 硬件锚定信任以及零信任密码学记忆溯源，从而在工业化对抗威胁下实现可验证、可追溯、可审计的智能体安全架构。

Abstract: The transition from stateless generative models in artificial intelligence to stateful, autonomous agents represents an architectural evolution that, while providing the capabilities of long-term planning and the automation of enterprise workflows, also represents the introduction of a new form of security threat, the Chronos Vulnerability. The Chronos Vulnerability represents the threat of memory-based attacks, including the Memory Injection Attack (MINJA) and the sleeper agent, in which the internal belief system of the autonomous agent is compromised, effectively decoupling the attack vector from the final catastrophic event. This study formalizes the threat model for persistence-based attacks and the threat of Dynamics Blindness in the context of the World of Workflows benchmark, demonstrating that traditional endpoint content filters are insufficient for the current stateful architecture. Consequently, this study synthesizes a defense-in-depth landscape, categorizing emerging frameworks such as diagnostic trajectory guardrails (AgentDoG), formal temporal verification (Agent-C), immunological memory consensus (A-MemGuard), and hardware-anchored trust via GPU-based Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and Zero-Trust memory architectures.

</details>


### [209] [Sophisticated Policies from Epistemic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19518)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出，Sophisticated Inference（SI）相较于标准主动推理的优势并非源于树搜索本身，而是源于"认知驱动（epistemic drive）"与"规划视野内的闭环控制（inner-horizon closed-loop control）"的结合。作者在认知先验（epistemic priors）的变分自由能（VFE）框架下展示了这种闭环结构可以通过对联合后验（未来状态与动作不独立）进行变分推断来表示，并通过Reactivity Maze基准实验证明，具有状态-动作联合后验的认知先验主动推理可以达到与SI相同的效果（最优率分别为98%和95%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准主动推理规划使用期望自由能（EFE）目标评估固定动作序列，本质上是开环的，无法在规划视野内根据未来状态调整未来动作。Sophisticated Inference通过树搜索实现了闭环规划，但其计算开销大，且不清楚其成功到底归功于树搜索算法本身，还是某种更本质的结构。作者希望从表示层面厘清：SI成功的核心机制是什么？认知先验VFE框架能否表示同样的闭环结构？

Method: 1. 理论分析：将认知先验整合到生成模型中作为变分目标的一部分，并通过对未来状态与动作的联合后验进行变分推断来获得状态-条件动作分布 q(u_t | x_{t-1})，即闭环策略。2. 基准环境：设计Reactivity Maze——一个随机环境，要求智能体访问信息提示（cue）以揭示目标位置，再根据该信息调整后续动作。3. 对比六种方法：三种共享同一时间分解联合后验的VFE目标（边际VFE、规划VFE、认知VFE）、一个动作-状态因式分解的认知VFE变体、Sophisticated Inference和标准EFE规划，从而正交地分离"认知驱动"与"后验依赖结构"两个因素。4. 推理过程：在JAX中使用Adam优化器对规划视野T=3进行1000步VFE最小化，每步采用滚动时域方案。

Result: 1. 无认知先验的方法（F_marginal, F_planning）不访问提示（F_marginal 0%，F_planning 0%），最优率分别为0%和44%。2. 有认知先验但约束动作-状态独立的方法（认知VFE的因式分解变体和标准EFE）虽访问提示（100%和55%），但最优率仅1%和12%——因为无法将未来信息回传为当前动作的条件。3. 同时具备认知驱动与状态-条件动作依赖的方法表现最佳：F_active最优率98%，Sophisticated Inference最优率95%，二者在95%置信区间内无显著差异。4. 优化诊断实验证实所有VFE目标均可靠收敛，行为差异源于目标本身而非优化失败。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：Sophisticated Inference的优越性是概念性的而非计算性的，其本质在于将认知价值（认知先验目标）与内部视野的闭环控制结构（联合后验中未来动作对未来状态的依赖）相结合。认知先验VFE最小化是这种结构的另一种表示方式，无需专用树搜索即可恢复与SI相同的行为模式。这将"闭环控制"这一需求从特定算法解耦到了变分族的选择上，为主动推理的可扩展消息传递实现提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Sophisticated Inference is a variant of active inference often associated with recursive belief modeling and tree search. We argue that its central computational role is simpler: within a planning horizon, it makes active inference closed-loop by allowing future actions to depend on future states and observations. This closed-loop structure can be represented in the epistemic-prior variational free energy framework. Epistemic priors supply the active-inference objective, while a joint posterior over future states and actions supplies the state-contingent control structure. We evaluate this decomposition in the Reactivity Maze, a stochastic benchmark designed to separate epistemic incentive from inner-horizon closed-loop control. The comparison includes three variational objectives with the same state-action posterior family, an action-state factorized active inference objective, Sophisticated Inference, and standard Expected Free Energy planning. The results show that neither ingredient is sufficient on its own. Methods without an epistemic component do not seek information, while methods that prevent future actions from depending on future states cannot turn information into reliable goal-reaching. By contrast, both Sophisticated Inference and full-joint epistemic-prior active inference solve the environment by combining epistemic drive with closed-loop inference. These results show that the advantage associated with Sophisticated Inference need not be specific to tree search itself. It arises from the closed-loop form of active inference, and this form can be represented in epistemic-prior variational inference when the posterior keeps future actions dependent on future states.

</details>


### [210] [Knowledge-Centric Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19592)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Lauren Hyoseo Yoon,Chengrui Qu,Amanda Zichang Wang,Atharva Sehgal,Eric Mazumdar,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"知识中心自改进"（KSI）新范式，将自改进的持续对象从智能体本身转移到外部策划的知识库。智能体保持通用且可丢弃，通过三阶段策划协议（任务级论坛、跨任务论坛、知识蒸馏）将经验转化为有证据支撑的可迁移知识。在抽象推理、编码和终端任务五大基准上，该方法以更低成本取得最高解题率，且知识可跨任务和跨LLM家族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进AI系统普遍采用"智能体中心"范式——通过优化提示词、工作流、harness甚至智能体自身代码来实现改进。这种范式存在三大缺陷：(1) 改进与特定智能体设计、任务分布和适应运行绑定，难以迁移；(2) 随着智能体持续吸收局部经验，有用行为可能被冲突更新稀释；(3) 维护成本高昂，每次新适应可能损害既有性能。因此需要一种使改进更易检查、可迁移、可移植的新范式。

Method: 设计了一个三阶段知识策划协议，运行在保持智能体通用、无状态、可丢弃的条件下：(1) **任务级论坛**：每个智能体完成任务后将执行轨迹转化为基于证据的局部声明（假设、约束、检查、策略），并在任务线程中互相引用支持或挑战彼此；(2) **跨任务论坛**：智能体讨论跨任务的复发模式，每条声明须有具体证据支撑，且必须对前序帖子明确表态（同意/不同意/综合），将分歧视为有用证据而非不稳定性；(3) **蒸馏阶段**：将存活的局部和全局声明整合为类型化包（含可迁移洞察、确认约束、拒绝假设、陷阱、检查、下一步），作为下一代智能体的种子包。知识库是唯一持续演化的对象，智能体每次均为新实例。

Result: 在五个基准（ARC-AGI-1/2、Polyglot、SWE-bench Pro、Terminal-Bench 2）上系统评估：(1) **vs 智能体中心基线**：KSI在ARC-AGI-1达86.7%（基线最高70%）、ARC-AGI-2达82.7%（基线最高60%）、Polyglot达68.0%（基线最高58%）、SWE-bench Pro达64.0%（基线最高54%），成本仅为基线的1/3至1/2；Terminal-Bench 2达43.8%，超越OpenHands（13.9%）、Meta-Harness（37.6%）等强基线。(2) **vs 提示优化**：在匹配预算下超越GEPA和OpenEvolve（ARC-AGI-1: 86.7% vs 44%/54%；Polyglot: 68.0% vs 36%/46%）。(3) **跨LLM泛化**：GPT-5.4-mini和Haiku 4.5均有效，GPT-5.4-mini在所有基准上表现更优且成本更低。(4) **迁移性**：在20个困难留出任务上，跨LLM家族的知识迁移零样本提升显著（如Polyglot从8.3%→20.0%，ARC-AGI-1从23.3%→43.3%）。

Conclusion: 本文确立了从智能体中心向知识中心自改进的范式转变：AI进步的持续载体是共享的、可策划的知识库，而非智能体内部参数或记忆。通过简单三阶段协议，无状态的简单智能体即可超越智能体中心自改进基线。这表明自主推理的瓶颈可能不在智能体架构复杂度，而在其访问和提炼的信息质量与结构。该范式为可检查、可协作、可插拔的智能体生态奠定基础，不同LLM可贡献并受益于统一的可复用理解。未来方向包括将策划协议与递归搜索或复杂规划模块结合、扩展到长时域任务分解、以及引入人类专家贡献。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems typically treat the agent as the object that improves, by optimizing prompts, workflows, harnesses, or even the agent's own code. This agent-centric view can make improvements expensive to maintain and difficult to transfer, because gains become tied to a particular agent design, task distribution, or adaptation run. We study a complementary paradigm: knowledge-centric self-improvement, in which agents remain generic and disposable while the persistent object is a curated knowledge base that agents can leverage for future tasks. We conduct controlled case studies to operationalize this idea via a simple protocol. Agents attempt one task, then contribute evidence-grounded insights to a shared knowledge base via task-level and cross-task forums, followed by knowledge distillation. Because self-improvement is contained in the knowledge rather than the agent, improvement can be more inspectable, transferable, and portable. Across abstract reasoning, coding, and terminal benchmarks, this protocol improves solve rates while reducing dollar cost relative to agent-centric baselines. The resulting distilled knowledge also transfers to held-out tasks and across LLM families, indicating that the improvement is not merely an LLM- or run-specific behavior. These results support a new view of self-improving agentic systems: progress can be driven primarily by the curated persistent knowledge. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [211] [Edge Intelligence in Civil Aviation: Paradigms, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19676)
*Wenbin Li,Zhongtian Liao,Bolin Liu,Yongjie Zhou,Jingling Wu,Xiaoyong Lin,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了边缘智能（Edge AI）在民航领域的应用，探讨了通过压缩、协同推理和分割学习等技术将AI模型从云端下沉至网络边缘，以应对云端部署在任务延迟、离线能力、数据隐私方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民航是安全关键型领域，运营中产生大量异构边缘数据。云端集中式部署AI模型存在高延迟、通信拒止环境下无法离线运行、以及敏感数据集中化带来的隐私与主权风险。亟需将感知、预测与决策逻辑下沉至数据源附近。

Method: 文章采用综述方法：首先阐述边缘AI在民航中的运营动机；然后回顾边缘推理与边缘学习的最新技术（包括压缩、协同推理、分割学习等）；接着介绍民航环境中的组织计算范式与相应配置；最后描述边缘智能在民航中的新兴应用与未来研究方向。

Result: 该论文提出了一个面向民航领域的边缘智能全景视图与统一认识框架，梳理了边缘推理与边缘学习的核心技术，分析了适用于民航场景的计算范式配置，并展示了边缘智能在民航全生命周期中实现低延迟、隐私保护与韧性AI服务的潜力。

Conclusion: 经过精细设计的边缘解决方案能够与云端基础设施互补，在民航全生命周期内提供低延迟、保护隐私且具有韧性的AI服务，是未来民航智能化发展的关键支撑技术。

Abstract: Civil aviation is safety critical and its operations, from flight decks and towers to ramps and maintenance, generate massive, heterogeneous data at the network edge. Yet cloud centric deployment of large Artificial Intelligence (AI) models often produces high task latency, lacks offline capability in communication denied environments, and requires centralizing sensitive data, raising privacy and sovereignty risks. Edge AI moves perception, prediction, and decision logic closer to the data producers via compression, collaborative inference, and split learning, thereby reducing latency, bandwidth, and exposure while enabling graceful operation during disconnections. This paper provides a panoramic view and a common understanding of edge intelligence tailored to civil aviation. We firstly articulate the operational motivations for edge AI, and then review recent techniques for edge inference and edge learning. We then introduce the organizational computing paradigms and the respective configurations in civil aviation environments; finally, we describe the emerging applications and the future research trends of edge intelligence in civil aviation. We argue that a refined edge solution can complement cloud foundations to deliver low latency, privacy preserving, and resilient AI services across the civil aviation lifecycle.

</details>


### [212] [Symbol and Footprint Database for Electronic Components by Agentic Recognition and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19767)
*Yichen Shi,Yuzhi Liu,Zhuofu Tao,Li Huang,Yuhao Gao,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei Hel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFgen，一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的智能体化流程，用于自动识别和生成电子元器件的原理图符号（symbol）和PCB封装（footprint）。同时构建了首个大规模电子元器件数据库SFnet，包含1000个元器件的数据手册、符号和封装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCB设计中，元器件库的符号和封装需要工程师根据数据手册（datasheet）手工创建，过程耗时且易出错。不同厂商的数据手册格式异构、EDA软件要求多样，导致库资源难以跨平台复用。随着新型元器件不断涌现，亟需一种高效、自动化的元器件库构建方法，以为MLLM理解和生成PCB设计奠定基础。

Method: 1）将符号与封装生成建模为条件文本生成任务；2）使用开源PDF解析工具从数据手册中提取符号图、封装图、引脚功能表等图像并进行分类，归纳关键约束信息；3）基于检索的上下文学习（ICL），从模板库中选取最佳匹配样本；4）符号生成采用少样本ICL提示工程；5）封装生成额外引入视觉提示（VP）为每个焊盘标注数字标识，并结合思维链（CoT）推理；6）通过提示修改（prompt modification）步骤迭代优化封装，对称性和位置等细节；7）使用GPT-4作为MLLM，KiCad 8.0.5进行可视化；8）构建包含100个元器件的基准测试集，按引脚数量分为四组（Basic/Standard/Complex/High-Density）。

Result: 符号生成整体准确率86%，封装生成整体准确率80%。具体结果：Basic组符号和封装ACC_N均为96%；Standard组分别为86%和80%；Complex组封装ACC_N降至46.67%；High-Density组符号ACC_N为0%、封装ACC_N为0%，表明MLLM在处理高密度复杂图像时能力有限。消融实验表明，VP+CoT结合可显著提升焊盘位置精度，后续的提示修改步骤进一步优化结果。单个元器件生成耗时3-5分钟。SFnet数据库包含1000个元器件（IC占722个）。

Conclusion: SFgen是首个公开的MLLM驱动PCB元器件智能体化识别与生成方案，结合ICL、VP和CoT技术，有效实现了符号和封装的自动化构建。所构建的SFnet是首个大规模电子元器件库数据集，为未来PCB设计的自动化生成奠定了基础。未来工作将扩展至更广泛的元器件类型，研究MLLM如何利用元器件库理解PCB原理图并辅助自动化设计。

Abstract: A rich and recognizable component library is the cornerstone of printed circuit board (PCB) design and generation. Traditionally, engineers manually create symbols and footprints and design PCB schematics, which is time-consuming and error-prone. Leveraging multimodal large language models (MLLMs), we develop SFgen, an agentic recognition and generation flow of symbol and footprint for electronic components. SFgen achieves 86% accuracy for symbol generation and 80% accuracy for footprint generation. We use the SFgen method to create SFnet, a database of symbols and footprints. It now has 1000 components and is expanding constantly, which lays the foundation for automatic generation of PCB designs.

</details>


### [213] [Silent Failures in Multimodal Agentic Search:A Diagnostic Taxonomy and Cross-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19793)
*Zhengxian Wu,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态智能体搜索系统中答案正确但推理过程不可靠的"静默失败"问题，提出包含六类失败模式的诊断分类法与轨迹级评估流水线，并在MMSearch-Plus数据集上对四个前沿多模态模型进行实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态智能体搜索系统评估主要关注最终答案的准确性，但忽略了搜索轨迹中潜在的可靠性问题。即便最终答案正确，推理过程仍可能缺乏充分证据支撑、出现幻觉引用或跨模态矛盾等"静默失败"，导致表层准确率系统性高估真实可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出六类静默失败分类法：模态捷径（mod-sc）、幻觉接地（pht-gr）、证据错而答案对（we-ra）、过度检索洗白（or-ld）、跨模态矛盾（cm-ct）、来源幻觉（prv-hl），覆盖检索前、中、后三个阶段。2. 构建轨迹级诊断流水线：所有模型在统一ReAct风格框架下运行，使用相同工具接口（日志格式、上限10次工具调用），通过结构化评分标准的LLM裁判诊断答案正确性和失败标签。3. 引入同族与跨族交叉验证器计算Cohen's κ，评估裁判可靠性。4. 进行空白图像压力测试和工具消融实验（移除reverse_image_search）。

Result: 基于MMSearch-Plus分层采样200个任务、四个模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview、GPT-4o）共800条轨迹的实验显示：（1）真实正确率（TCR）比表层准确率低0.6-7.3个百分点，证实准确率系统性高估可靠性；（2）Gemini 3.1准确率最高（61.3%→54.0%），下降幅度也最大，表明更强模型并未消除静默失败，而是发生分布转移；（3）失败模式随能力发生迁移——Claude/Gemini 2.5/GPT-4o以检索后失败为主，Gemini 3.1则因依赖自身视觉编码器而出现更多模态捷径；（4）空白图像压力测试中206/208条原正确轨迹在去除图像后不再成立，证明任务确实需要视觉信息；（5）跨裁判验证显示答案正确性高度稳定（跨族κ=0.817），但细粒度标签存在裁判依赖性（mod-sc跨族κ=0.031）；（6）添加reverse_image_search使三个检索依赖型模型准确率提升13.9-18.9个百分点，但同时将失败从检索前转移到检索后，失败并未消失。

Conclusion: 研究证明最终答案准确性不足以衡量多模态智能体搜索的可靠性，正确的答案仍可能依赖未充分支持的证据、虚构来源或跨模态矛盾。表层准确率与真实正确率之间存在持续差距，且更强模型和更好工具往往只是转移失败发生的位置而非消除失败。这一工作为多模态智能体的可解释可信评估提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: this https URL

</details>


### [214] [Rewarding Better Thinking for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19824)
*Xubo Liu,Wenya Guo,Ruxue Yan,Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR)，一种面向开放式LLM偏好对齐的流程导向奖励机制。它将偏好数据转换为样本特定的思维检查清单，用于评估推理轨迹，并通过EMA残差公式去除与结果奖励的重叠部分，在DAPO框架下实现对推理质量的补充监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL偏好对齐中的代理奖励多为结果级（outcome-level），主要评估最终响应而忽略推理轨迹。当多个响应获得相似的结果分数时，信用分配变得粗糙，轨迹级偏好未被充分指定。过程奖励模型（PRM）在数学/编码等可验证领域有效，但难以直接迁移到缺乏唯一解路径和局部可验证步骤的开放式对齐任务。需要一种能在开放式场景下提供轨迹级过程监督的奖励机制，同时避免与结果奖励产生冗余。

Method: 1) Thinking Checklist构建：利用GPT-4o从偏好对(q, o⁺, o⁻)中推断样本特定的检查清单C(q) = {c_k}，捕获用户意图、约束、权衡、上下文适用性等偏好隐含考量；2) 原始检查清单奖励：用Qwen3-30B-A3B作为评判模型，按5级量表(2,4,6,8,10)对推理轨迹T_i进行逐项打分，经中心化处理r^chk = [(s̄_i - 6)/4]_+得到原始检查清单奖励；3) EMA残差公式：维护全局指数移动平均α_m = μ·α_{m-1} + (1-μ)·(r^chk_i / (r^out_i + ε))，定义残余思维增量r^sur = [r^chk_i - α_m·r^out_i]_+，仅保留超出结果奖励可预测部分的思维质量；4) 总训练奖励：r_i = (1-λ_fmt)·r^out_i + λ_fmt·r^fmt_i + λ_TCR·r^sur_i，其中λ_fmt=0.1, λ_TCR=0.05；5) 策略优化采用DAPO框架（GRPO组内归一化优势 + 解耦裁剪），无KL项。

Result: 在5个模型（Qwen2.5-3B/7B、Llama3.2-3B/Llama3.1-8B、DeepSeek-LLM-7B-chat，来自3个模型家族）上，使用BPO数据集训练，在4个开放式基准（Vicuna Eval、Dolly Eval、BPO-test Eval、AlpacaEval 2.0）上评估。主要结果：(1) DAPO+TCR在5个模型上一致优于DAPO，ΔWR分别为+8.58、+10.92、+9.25、+7.67、+7.75；(2) 在AlpacaEval 2.0上WR/DWR/LCWR三个指标均取得最优，LCWR提升表明增益非源于答案长度膨胀；(3) 消融实验：去掉EMA残差化（w/o EMA）导致性能在所有模型上下降（+8.00/+2.83/+8.83/+1.67/+5.33），证明残差化的必要性；(4) Qwen2.5-7B上的奖励设计对比显示，TCR优于通用轨迹奖励和全局共享检查清单，证明样本特定检查清单的针对性优势；(5) 使用Gemini-2.5-Pro作为评判者时增益依然稳健存在；(6) 响应长度分析显示推理阶段变长但最终答案长度基本不变。

Conclusion: TCR通过将偏好信息转换为样本特定的思维检查清单并对推理轨迹进行评估，为开放式LLM偏好对齐提供了有效的过程级监督。EMA残差公式成功消除了与结果奖励的冗余，使TCR成为结果级监督的互补信号而非叠加信号。在5个来自3个家族的模型和4个基准上的实验一致证明，TCR作为DAPO中的附加奖励项能持续提升对齐性能，且增益不依赖答案长度膨胀。生成的检查清单具有高度样本特异性（70,359/70,482为唯一项），覆盖意图分解、约束跟踪、上下文锚定、逻辑规划、完整性规划、权衡意识、风险意识、用户适应等10类思维行为。

Abstract: LLM preference alignment aims to optimize models toward human preferences across diverse user instructions. Reinforcement learning has become a major post-training approach for this goal, but existing proxy rewards are often outcome-level, mainly evaluating the final response while providing limited guidance for the reasoning trajectory. This can make credit assignment coarse when multiple responses receive similar final scores, leaving trajectory-level preferences under-specified. To address this limitation, we propose Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR), a process-oriented reward for RL-based preference alignment. TCR converts preference pairs into sample-specific thinking checklists and uses them to evaluate whether the generated reasoning trace addresses the preference-implied considerations. To reduce overlap with outcome-level supervision, TCR further introduces an exponential moving average (EMA) residual formulation to isolate a complementary thinking surplus beyond what is predictable from the outcome reward. Experiments on five models from three model families show that TCR consistently improves alignment performance across diverse benchmarks, with ablations further validating the importance of EMA-based residual formulation and sample-specific checklist supervision.

</details>


### [215] [Know Your Agent: Reconnaissance-Driven Pentesting of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19837)
*Or Zion Eliav,Eyal Lenga,Shir Bernstien,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Know Your Agent (KYA)，一种基于侦察驱动的黑盒自动化AI Agent渗透测试框架，通过在攻击循环中系统性地提取目标Agent的工具、策略、权限、任务上下文等"知识资产"，以提升间接提示注入（IPI）攻击的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM Agent的间接提示注入（IPI）自动化红队方法主要依赖固定模板、单次生成或迭代式载荷优化，将反馈仅用于调整payload而忽略对目标Agent本身的建模。这导致攻击者错失了理解Agent工具、权限、防御机制等目标特定信息的机会，从而无法构造足够有效且具备上下文合理性的注入载荷。作者认为Agent安全测试应借鉴传统渗透测试中的侦察循环，将侦察作为攻击循环中的一等公民。

Method: 1. **形式化Agent侦察**：定义"知识资产"（knowledge asset）为任何能增加IPI攻击成功率的离散操作性信息，并提出两类Agent特有的弱点——表面形式信任（W1：模型将复制性强的文本模式视为可信）与连贯性合法化（W2：模型将上下文一致视为合法意图）。2. **知识资产分类法**：分为Evasion（规避过滤/护栏/结构防御）、Pretext（构建叙事合理性，如代理类型、用户角色、当前任务）和Blueprint（伪造权威形式，如工具名、调用schema、模型身份）。3. **KYA框架**：包含三个模块——Orchestrator（依据目标画像M决策侦察还是攻击）、Reconnaissance（通过Scout子代理主动或被动探测资产）、Exploitation（Strategist+Forger将战术与资产实例化为payload），并配套一个参数化的"战术库"（tactic library）描述常见攻击蓝图（如分隔符伪造、工具调用补全、用户冒充）及其前置资产要求。4. **威胁模型**：黑盒访问，可注册为合法用户交互，分两阶段——渗透测试（Stage 1）和应用（Stage 2，包含恶意攻击与防御者两种角色）。

Result: 1. **基准测试**：在AgentDojo和InjecAgent上对GPT-4.1、Llama 3.3-70B、Gemini 3 Flash评估，KYA在AgentDojo平均ASR达86.0%（最优基线47.7%），InjecAgent达99.3%；相比最强迭代基线提升最高达67个百分点，并在所有模型和基准上一致最佳。2. **消融实验**：在AgentDojo上单独侦察仅能达27.4% ASR，单独战术达62%，完整框架达86%，侦察贡献了大部分增益。3. **知识资产消融**：单资产中Agent's Task最关键，将无防御ASR从12.4%提升至61.9%。4. **防御鲁棒性**：对PI检测器、重复用户提示、数据定界三种常见防御，KYA在所有防御下ASR仍居首（30.4%/53.0%/86.3%）。5. **真实场景**：在OpenHands编码Agent上对70个（任务×目标）测试，KYA整体ASR 21.9%，呈现非单调的tier模式——Risky类最易成功（35.0%），因任务合理性最高。

Conclusion: KYA证明了将侦察作为一类一等的攻击动作能够显著提升对工具型LLM Agent的自动化IPI测试效果，揭示了Agent的关键安全弱点源自其可观察且可复制的"信任线索"。未来需通过运行时出处认证等模型外机制来防御W1（表面形式信任），而提升Agent安全性不仅需要测试攻击是否成功，更需要审视Agent暴露了哪些可被武器化的操作性知识。作者已开源框架、基准与基线以推动可复现研究，并强调该双重用途工作应仅用于合法授权的防御性评估。

Abstract: Traditional pentesting uses reconnaissance at each step to uncover unseen weaknesses, build stronger attacks, and advance the objective; we argue that AI agents require the same treatment. We formalize agent reconnaissance by modeling the process and identifying the knowledge assets it seeks to extract: what they are, how they are used, and which agent weaknesses they exploit to give adversaries leverage in indirect prompt injection attacks. We instantiate these insights in Know Your Agent (KYA), a framework that automates black-box, reconnaissance-driven pentesting by probing agents, building target profiles, and using those profiles to craft stronger attacks. We evaluate KYA on agent-security benchmarks and a real-world coding agent, and release KYA, its benchmarks, and baseline implementations for reproducibility.

</details>


### [216] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DocOps——一种基于层次化分类法的可确定性验证评估框架，将文档操作拆解为原子维度与递增复杂度的多步工作流，用于系统评测AI智能体在真实文档处理任务中的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体（autonomous agents）能力提升，其在通用数字文档上的可靠性操控成为构建通用AI助手和自动化工作流的关键。然而，现有智能体在处理耦合性强、长程依赖的复杂文档任务时表现不足，且缺乏细粒度的可验证评测基准，亟需一个能反映真实业务实践、且具有确定性可验证性的评估框架来揭示其能力瓶颈。

Method: 1）构建层次化分类法（hierarchical taxonomy）：自顶向下将真实世界文档操作分解为原子操作维度（atomic dimensions）和递进式工作流复杂度（escalating workflow complexities）；2）构建DocOps基准：基于该分类法生成可确定性验证（deterministically verifiable）的评测任务集；3）跨智能体框架（agentic harnesses）系统评测：覆盖闭源与开源代表性模型在不同智能体架构下的表现；4）细粒度行为分析：通过操纵行为分类剖析失败模式，归纳为三类关键缺陷：长期状态追踪崩溃（long-term state tracking collapse）、浅层语义验证（shallow semantic verification）、结构性元数据的破坏性编辑（destructive editing of structural metadata）。

Result: 实验表明：即便最先进的前沿模型配置（frontier configurations），在高度耦合、长程依赖的文档任务中仍存在显著能力局限；细粒度分析揭示出三类典型失败模式——长期状态追踪崩塌、语义验证仅停留于表面、以及对文档结构元数据的破坏性修改，表明现有智能体难以维持全局文档一致性。

Conclusion: DocOps框架明确了当前智能体在维持全局文档一致性方面的能力边界，揭示了"非破坏性、鲁棒文档操控智能体"设计的关键挑战，为未来构建面向复杂数字生态系统的可信智能体提供了诊断工具与设计启示。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [217] [JANUS: Foreseeing Latent Risk for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19913)
*Yuan Xiong,Linji Hao,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JANUS 框架,通过"预见式"(foresight-oriented)方法训练长时程智能体安全防护模型 Vanguard。该模型在部分轨迹上预测未来安全风险(anticipation),并结合观察到的前缀进行安全裁决(adjudication),在危险动作执行前进行拦截,在四个智能体安全基准上比基线防护模型平均保护率提升 15.9 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 OpenClaw、Codex、Claude Code 等工具调用型智能体的部署,语言模型的失效已从文本层面转向操作层面,单个不安全动作可能删除文件、泄露隐私或破坏系统。现有防护机制多为反应式(reactive),仅在危险动作已发生或即将发生时检测,难以应对长时程任务中"早期信号在多步后才升级为危害"的场景(如用户要求所有包更改限制在虚拟环境中,但智能体后续修改全局 Python 环境)。因此亟需能在执行前预判延迟风险的预测式防护机制。

Method: 1. **仿真数据构建**:采用多智能体仿真流水线(Manger/Instructor/ToolDesigner/Grader/EnvInjector/Executor/Simulator/Reviewer),基于风险分类法(用户来源、环境来源、智能体来源)合成多样化的长时程轨迹,共生成 75,180 条训练样本(Safe 34,100 / Unsafe 18,665 / Potential Unsafe 22,415)。
2. **CoAA-RL 联合优化**:共享策略 πθ 通过任务指示器 m∈{A,D} 完成两个耦合任务——
 - **Anticipation 任务**:从部分轨迹 (x, τpre) 采样 G_A 个未来摘要 S_k;
 - **Adjudication 任务**:基于 (x, τpre, S_k) 采样 G_D 个裁决 J_{k,j}。
 奖励设计将未来摘要的 NLI 相似度与下游裁决效用耦合(r^A_k = (1-λ_A)·Sim(S_k,s) + λ_A·u_k),并采用 GRPO 进行组内相对优势优化。
3. **推理**:Vanguard 在每步执行两阶段预测——先 Anticipation 生成未来摘要,再 Adjudication 输出安全标签(safe / potential_unsafe / unsafe),仅 unsafe 标签触发拦截。

Result: **主结果(表 1)**:在四个基准(AgentDojo、Agent-SafetyBench、AgentLAB、LPS-Bench)上,Vanguard 平均 ASR 为 0.071,显著优于六个防护基线平均 0.230 和无防护 0.397;保护率提升 15.9 个百分点,同时 AgentDojo 良性任务效用 0.680,提升 5.1 个百分点。**消融实验(RQ1)**:移除 Anticipation 任务使平均 ASR 由 0.072 升至 0.148;推理时去除未来摘要使 ASR 升至 0.123;采用 Oracle 摘要(上限)ASR 进一步降至 0.052。**耦合奖励设计(RQ2)**:仅使用相似度奖励(AgentDojo 效用下降 48.4%)、仅使用效用奖励(效用下降 42.4%)或解耦训练(平均 ASR 由 0.072 升至 0.254)均显著劣化。**超参敏感性(RQ3)**:λ_A=0.5 时取得最佳安全-效用平衡。**早期前缀风险预测(RQ4)**:在 25% 前缀预算下 Vanguard 已优于多数基线,在 50%–100% 预算下三个基准均取得最低 ASR,长时程规划(LPS-Bench)改进尤为显著(0.323→0.075)。

Conclusion: 本文提出了将"预见未来风险"作为长时程智能体安全核心能力的 JANUS 框架,通过仿真数据构建与 CoAA-RL 联合训练,使 Vanguard 能够在危险动作执行前基于部分轨迹预测延迟风险并主动干预。实验证明预测式防护在显著降低攻击成功率的同时保持良性任务效用,确立了"未来风险预见"作为工具调用智能体主动式、轨迹感知防护的关键能力。主要局限包括:训练轨迹源自仿真而非真实部署,以及评估受限于固定的智能体骨干、工具环境和基准定义的风险场景。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [218] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERQDP，一种用于有限时域MDP在根级(resolute)分布风险目标下的无枚举无采样的精确动态规划方法，通过秩-分位数代理、PMF精确评估和任意时刻细化循环实现带显式上下界间隙证书的风险敏感规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融、医疗和安全关键领域，序贯决策的长期回报分布（特别是尾部）至关重要。然而根级风险度量（如CVaR、WOWA等秩依赖函数）对回报分布非线性，破坏Bellman最优性，使得通过场景树枚举的直接优化变得难解。现有的方法要么通过状态增广或鲁棒对偶重塑问题（改变语义），要么限制为DP友好的风险类（如指数效用），要么依赖采样近似，缺乏对原始静态目标的认证保证。

Method: ERQDP包含三个核心组件：(A) 秩-分位数代理规划(RQDP)：将秩轴离散为K个分片，将秩依赖目标近似为切片均值向量的线性泛函，用反向归纳精确求解诱导代理；(B) 精确PMF策略评估：在离散回报网格上通过DP传播PMF计算任何策略的回报分布，评估CVaR/WOWA，并给出显式舍入误差界H/(2c)；(C) 任意时刻细化循环：倍增K，仅接受精确评估下严格改善根目标的策略更新，维护上下界LB/UB产生显式最优性间隙证书。理论保证包括引理1（配额填充计算切片均值分位数）、命题1（RQDP代理最优性）、命题2（PMF评估舍入界）和定理1（扭曲稳定性）。

Result: 在Betting Game (H=10, 1011状态)、GridWorld (5×5, H=20, 4个实例)和Inventory Control (H=10, 4411状态)三个基准上验证：BG上ERQDP-CVaR达到Opt. gap≈0且成本95；GW上对α≥0.10获得78×到928×加速、正的ΔCVaR和Δ均值（Pareto改进），极端尾部α≤0.05时仍报告显式残差间隙；IC上CVaR_0.02成本373.26，比Rigter等基线低约3.4%。敏感性研究表明K（秩分辨率）是证书收紧的主要旋钮，而c（回报网格尺度）影响较小，ERQDP支持CVaR和WOWA两种扭曲，覆盖风险规避与风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP是首个同时满足以下三条件的有限时域MDP根级风险DP忠实规划框架：(i) 支持从尾部主导到全谱段的风险偏好；(ii) 通过避免轨迹枚举和采样近似实现可扩展性；(iii) 提供理论最优性保证。它解决了风险敏感序贯决策中可处理性与目标忠实性之间的长期权衡，为风险参数扫描和证书化规划提供了实用工具。未来工作包括自适应秩网格、更紧的扭曲误差界和代理到原始目标的更严格证书。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [219] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOF-Sleuth是一个用于金属有机框架(MOF)晶体学信息文件(CIF)审计的强化学习引导智能体，通过Forensic Lab工具库提取确定性化学证据，Sleuth推理引擎产出可解释的细粒度错误诊断（15类错误），并提出Chem-GD度量评估化学解释的证据支撑质量，在四个基准测试上超越现有LLM方法和MOF专用ML方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOF数据库的CIF文件中存在细微的化学和结构错误（如缺失原子、电荷不平衡、异常占据率等），会损害下游模拟和机器学习结果。现有方法存在两大局限：(1) 细粒度归因不足——MOFChecker、MOFClassifier、SETC等仅提供固定检查或粗粒度标签，无法提供证据支撑的解释；(2) CIF推理不可靠——直接让LLM审计长而信息密集的CIF文件成本高且不稳定，因为关键化学证据隐式分布于原子位点记录中，需要几何、连接性、占据率和电荷等计算。两者根源在于化学证据与语言模型解释之间的弱耦合。

Method: 提出MOF-Sleuth双模块智能体：(1) **Forensic Lab**——确定性工具库（K=8个工具），执行CIF解析、组成/晶胞核算、占据率分析、周期几何、截断图连接性、官能团识别、配位/桥连分析和电荷账本检查，产出结构化证据报告r=(F, H, S, C, A)，包含客观事实、硬标志、诊断信号、解释上下文和引用别名；(2) **Sleuth**——基于GRPO强化学习的推理策略，产出包含证据支撑解释、15类细粒度错误子集和二值裁决的审计报告。设计6维奖励向量(R_ans, R_type, R_fmt, R_cons, R_grd, R_evid)，其中R_grd验证引用事实真实性，R_evid检验诊断类型与结构信号的一致性，将工具测量转化为化学解释层面的监督信号。提出Chem-GD（化学扎根诊断）度量，作为裁决正确性、引用保真度、类型-答案一致性、诊断支持和可归因链接的类别均衡严格合取，无需模型评判器即可确定性评估解释质量。

Result: 在CoRE-MOF 2019（域内）和CoRE-MOF 2026、ToBaCCo、QMOF（三个域外迁移测试）四个基准上，MOF-Sleuth全面超越所有评估的LLM方法和MOF专用ML方法。关键结果：在ToBaCCo上检测准确率达0.943（vs最强基线0.585），Chem-GD得分0.713（vs最强基线0.227）。消融研究表明工具证据和奖励引导对齐带来互补增益；未使用RL的MOF-Sleuth在Chem-GD上仅得0.229，证明RL对齐对证据扎根解释质量的关键作用。经盲法专家验证证实结果有效性。

Conclusion: MOF-Sleuth首次将MOF CIF审计系统化为超越二值筛选的细粒度归因任务，定义了15类错误类型的可操作归因词汇表，证明了工具扎根的强化学习智能体在科学结构化诊断中的有效性。其核心贡献在于将确定性工具输出转化为化学解释层面的奖励监督信号，以及Chem-GD作为无需LLM评判器的确定性解释质量度量。该工作为信息密集的科学文档审计提供了"证据-解释耦合"的范式，可推广至其他需要客观计算证据与可解释推理结合的科学领域。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [220] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenWorld 提出了一种基于数字孪生的确定性事件溯源仿真框架，通过真实地图、天气、节日、网络数据驱动 16 个角色在完整一天中演化，将每个评估用例的标签设计为指向模拟设备状态的快照指针（而非依赖 LLM 评判或人工标注），为智能手机个人助手提供隐私安全、可复现、分布匹配的评估数据生成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助手需要在纵向个人数据上进行多跳推理与检索，但评估所需的真实设备数据因隐私问题难以共享；现有数据生成方法（手工构造缺乏纵向复杂度、LLM 生成缺乏设备底座且依赖 LLM 评判）难以同时满足隐私安全、可复现与可靠真值三重需求。

Method: SenWorld 将模拟视为状态转移系统：人物（brain + body）、手机、服务、物理世界在同一统一世界中演化；候选人通过事件总线前持久化、由物理世界执行可行性约束检查；通过的事件触发全系统快照（物理、服务、人物、手机四段）；下游只读投影从终态快照导出每部手机的本地数据库与评估用例，标签为指向现有记录的 (表名, 记录ID) 指针而非后验判断；物理世界由时间、地理、天气、节日、网络五个子系统构成，伪随机由固定运行种子驱动以保证可复现性。

Result: RQ1 显示生成数据与 1186 条真实用户记录在类别分布上 JSD=0.070、TVD=0.186，沟通类时间节律 JSD<0.1，但生成记录整体较短、笔记频次偏高；RQ2 显示无脚本交互下 16 个角色自发形成 7 对完全互惠对话子图（密度 0.333、互惠率 1.0），行为类型熵平均 1.846 bits 体现差异化行为模式；RQ3 中 717 个评估用例在某商用助手身上暴露 78 例失败（10.9%），92% 集中于通话与短信记录（通话 35.1%、短信 21.5%），其中 69% 为用户发起的自发起记录，指针回溯证实全部为助手侧检索错误（目标存在但未被选中、助手代之以跨表主题相似的干扰记录）。

Conclusion: SenWorld 提供了隐私安全、可复现、分布校验且真值由构造固定的评估数据生成路径，所有失败均可通过快照指针定位为助手侧缺陷而无需 LLM 评判；未来工作需通过受控消融量化物理锚定的因果贡献，并将世界锚定拓展至更多城市、更长模拟时间窗与多种子以验证规模化下的保真度与涌现特性。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [221] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoThink框架,通过Self-Pruning Training(SPT)迭代剪枝冗余推理步骤并自训练,以及Aha-Moment Preference Optimization(AMPO)借鉴遗传算法从失败轨迹中合成"由错到对"的aha-moment数据进行DPO优化,在数学推理和代码生成基准上同时提升大型推理模型(LRM)的推理效率和推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型(LRM)存在"过度思考"(overthinking)问题:模型反复验证同一问题,超过65%的token消耗在冗余计算上,导致推理延迟增加但准确率无明显提升甚至下降。现有方法存在两大缺陷:(1)快慢思考切换策略依赖二元分类,易因误差传播导致错误推理模式;(2)推理轨迹压缩方法无法细粒度区分有益验证步骤和冗余步骤,可能损害推理能力。此外,现有方法多以牺牲少量推理能力换取效率,忽视了推理能力的恢复与提升。

Method: EvoThink框架包含两个阶段:
1. **Self-Pruning Training (SPT)**:无监督方法,引入"原子推理单元"(Atomic Reasoning Unit)概念,将推理轨迹分解为独立的推理子步骤,通过比较相邻单元的局部结论是否一致来识别并剪枝冗余单元,然后对剪枝后的简洁轨迹进行迭代自训练,直至压缩增益S(i)≤0.1。
2. **Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO)**:借鉴遗传算法,对每个查询生成K个响应,标记全部错误的查询为"困难查询"构造错误答案组;定义基于多样性的适应度函数ℱ(r)=|C(r)|(独特局部结论数量);选择适应度最高的失败轨迹,保留错误推理前缀并以"But is this correct? I think I missed something."引导转向正确答案,合成aha-moment突变数据;通过DPO训练,优化模型学习"由错到对"的推理模式。

Result: 在MATH500、AIME24、AIME25、TACO四个基准上,以DeepScaleR-1.5B、Distill-Qwen-1.5B、QwQ-32B为骨干模型进行评估:
- **EvoThink SPT+AMPO** 在所有设置中均达到性能与效率的最佳平衡;
- 在QwQ-32B上,AIME24的Pass@1从vanilla的46.7%提升至55.8%(AMPO),同时SPT+AMPO在压缩token的同时保持89.5%的Pass@1;
- SPT单独使用即可达到与多数基线相当甚至更好的性能,且为无监督训练;
- 迁移学习实验(MATH→AIME)显示,基于gold solution的SFT/DPO会导致性能大幅下降(如DPO降至vanilla的五分之一以下),而EvoThink AMPO仍能获得性能提升,表明其学习的是可泛化的推理策略而非记忆答案;
- 多样性分析验证了高多样性轨迹作为突变数据能带来更大性能增益(K=6为最佳)。

Conclusion: EvoThink通过SPT和AMPO两个组件,有效解决了LRM的过度思考问题,同时显著提升了推理能力。框架的核心贡献在于:(1)将overthinking重新定义为推理质量问题,提出细粒度的原子单元剪枝方法;(2)通过"由错到对"的aha-moment数据构造,使模型从失败中学习探索新推理路径。实验证明该方法在数学和代码生成任务上同时实现效率提升和性能改进,具有良好的泛化能力。未来工作将深入探究"由错到对"学习模式为何对模型训练特别有效。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [222] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证当前AI以Transformer作为通用架构是一种结构性错误（"巨型海马体"谬误），类比神经科学已确立的"巨型布洛卡区"谬误。作者将Transformer功能性地定位于海马体（关系绑定、情景记忆），而非通用皮层，并提出一种异构拓扑网络（System of Systems），由具有域特定归纳偏置的模块（卷积视觉通路、频谱时态听觉模块、Transformer情景记忆核心、基底神经节启发的执行门控、工作记忆缓冲器）通过标准化接口通信构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机在于揭示AI领域一个可测量的结构性错误：Transformer作为单一架构被复用于文本、视觉、音频等多种模态，而神经科学百年细胞构筑学证据（从Brodmann到Jülich-Brain到Patch-seq）表明，不同认知功能由质性不同的结构实现，而非简单缩放单一模板。这种AI架构单一文化不仅违反了生物学证据，也造成系统性脆弱性——便利被误认为科学发现。论文同时批判了当前分析方法（图论、Granger因果性、可解释性工具）无法从连接性数据恢复计算功能的认识论盲点。

Method: 论文采用三部分论证结构：（1）认识论批判，借助MOS 6502研究（Jonas & Kording 2017）说明完整连接图无法恢复计算，并审视网络分析和Granger因果性的局限；（2）细胞构筑学证据综述，从Brodmann分区到Jülich概率图谱再到Patch-seq单细胞多模态数据，证明区域分化具有生物学必要性，并以V1与MT/V5的层级厚度对比、树突复杂性作为补充；（3）功能主义分析，运用Putnam多重可实现性、Fodor模块性、Marr三层分析、Craver机制解释、Hopfield联想记忆模型，并借鉴Hardware Lottery（Hooker 2021）框架解释Transformer主导地位的经济学根源。论文将CNN作为最后一个具有正确结构性先验的架构案例研究，分析Mixture-of-Experts的"克隆分区"问题，并将Transformer功能定位于海马体类比。

Result: 主要结论与发现包括：（1）Transformer是海马体形成的功能类比（关系绑定、情景预测、内容寻址联想记忆），而非通用皮层；（2）Mixture-of-Experts仅在结构相同的专家间分配参数，属于定量专门化而非质性多样化，是Hardware Lottery内部的优化而非逃离；（3）CNN通过局部感受野、权重共享和层级组合编码视觉结构先验，在ImageNet上以较少数据取得突破，证明了域特定归纳偏置的价值；（4）Transformer跨模态复用（ViT、AST）需要大规模预训练补偿缺失的域特定偏置；（5）频谱时态听觉处理需要保留对数尺度频率拓扑和时频调制敏感性，而非常规注意力机制；（6）执行门控、工作记忆和跨模态绑定需要基底神经节-丘脑-皮层环路等专门拓扑，标准注意力机制无法替代；（7）当代AI架构单一文化因GPU经济学而被锁定，产生系统性脆弱性而非科学收敛。

Conclusion: 论文提出异构拓扑网络（Heterogeneous Topological Network）作为替代方案：一个System of Systems，其中结构不同的模块（卷积视觉通路、频谱时态听觉模块、Transformer情景记忆核心、基底神经节启发的执行门控、工作记忆缓冲器）各自保留其计算所需的域特定归纳偏置，并通过标准化接口而非共享基底进行通信。这是一个面向AI架构师的设计规范而非认知科学研究纲领：要求在训练前基于结构性证据指定模块化约束和功能分解，将架构视为设计的输入而非训练后反向工程的附属品。论文强调核心论点并非反Transformer，而是反普遍化——Transformer应作为强大的关系-情景记忆模块保留在异构架构内，而非被误用为通用皮层的替代品。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [223] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GSEM框架，将多智能体协调历史编码为异构关系图，通过GNN检索结构相似过往经验，实现动态制造环境下基于经验引导的策略自适应，而非从头学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态制造环境存在频繁扰动（机器故障、紧急加单、工时波动），现有MARL方法将每次扰动独立处理，丢弃了宝贵的协调经验，导致适应慢、跨扰动泛化能力弱。需要一种能存储和复用协调经验的结构化记忆机制。

Method: 1) 用R-GCN将制造状态编码为含三类节点（操作/机器/智能体）和四类边（前置/分配/管理/冲突）的异构图；2) 记忆库以(pre-图, post-图, 动作序列, 累积奖励, 元数据)形式存储过往协调片段，通过质量阈值和扰动边界检测筛选；3) 新扰动发生时用余弦相似度+质量-时效加权检索Top-K片段；4) 通过交叉注意力融合、门控机制和记忆条件通信将检索经验注入策略；三阶段训练（MAPPO预训练→记忆填充→端到端微调+对比损失）。

Result: 在10×10 DFJSP基准上，相比最强基线MAPPO+Epi，makespan降低4.1%-10.0%，适应步数减少33%-38%，优势随扰动频率升高而扩大。消融实验验证图结构编码和相似性检索为最关键组件（随机检索恶化8.0%）。跨扰动迁移实验证明恢复模式可泛化（混合源记忆平均211.3 vs 无记忆233.7）。计算开销约增18%推理时间和31%训练时间，15×15规模仍保持7.4%优势。

Conclusion: GSEM通过图结构经验记忆和相似性检索实现了可复用、可泛化的多智能体协调恢复模式，在高频混合扰动下效果尤为显著。未来方向包括更大规模扩展、融入人类操作员反馈以及推广至其他动态组合优化领域。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [224] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK是一个集成知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理的闭环学习框架，基于LP^MLN形式化方法，将CACTUS构建的知识图谱转化为软加权逻辑程序，并通过迭代规则学习与权重校准实现自适应分类推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型在数据分布变化时性能下降，且难以整合先验知识；同时，纯统计分类器依赖训练数据中的统计规律，缺乏可解释性和结构化推理能力，难以模拟人类结合背景知识与反事实推理的决策方式。

Method: 该框架首先利用CACTUS从表格数据中构建有向知识图谱，然后通过映射函数Φ将其转化为LP^MLN基础程序（节点对应属性-状态原子，边对应软Horn规则）。在此基础上，引入三种可解释符号规则学习器（新提出的Structural Learner、CMAR、AMIE3）挖掘候选规则，通过MC-SAT进行概率权重学习，并采用自助法稳定性筛选剔除不稳定规则。算法在闭环中迭代更新图结构和权重，直到无新边引入或达到最大轮次。

Result: 在HDC和WDBC两个医疗数据集上的实验表明：(1) SL产生的规则集更紧凑且计算成本最低，且在权重、支持度和Δ-LL上表现最佳；(2) 单独的LP^MLN概率校准可能降低分类性能，但结合规则学习后带来稳定提升；(3) SL+AMIE3组合效果最优，平衡准确率相对基线提升+3.36%，超过两者单独效果之和，呈现共识驱动效应；(4) CMAR基于频率启发式的规则与概率目标对齐度较低，性能改善有限。

Conclusion: CLARK提供了一种构建自适应、可解释、知识驱动的分类模型的原则性方法，通过概率基础上的规则学习闭环精炼知识图谱。未来工作将扩展至更大规模数据集、研究增量与流式学习变体、整合神经符号方法，并探索与plingo等新兴PASP系统的更紧密结合。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [225] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对微服务系统中自动修复的安全性问题，将安全修复重新定义为风险约束的干预决策问题，提出基于约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的框架，通过三维风险分解（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性）和上下文自适应人在回路（HITL）门控机制，实现对误修复率（FRR）的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代IT运维中，错误修复的代价往往超过不作为的代价，但现有自动化修复系统主要关注生成动作而非决定是否干预，将安全作为事后的人工审批，缺乏对风险、不确定性和后果的显式推理。该瓶颈不在于"做什么"而在于"是否做"以及"何时做"，因此需要一个能明确推理风险的决策层。

Method: 方法包含四个核心组件：（1）三维风险表示——通过扩散核估计爆炸半径b、回滚模型估计可逆性ρ、5个集成模型估计认知不确定性u；（2）基于事件类比的干预结果评分器，通过检索相似历史事件并加权聚合预测成功概率；（3）风险约束策略，将CMDP通过拉格朗日松弛转化为无约束优化，结合CQL正则化进行离线训练；（4）上下文自适应HITL门控，将升级决策建模为上下文赌博机，根据值班负载、业务关键性等调整升级阈值。训练时通过拉格朗日乘子法在期望FRR预算约束下优化，推理时通过三维风险向量逐维过滤实现尾部安全。

Result: 在Train Ticket微服务基准（41个服务，3节点K8s集群）上使用Chaos Mesh进行11类故障注入，模型在成功率（0.786）和FRR（0.112）上均优于所有基线：相比Rule-Runbook基线成功率提升2.5个百分点、FRR降低39%；相比固定阈值HITL变体减少17%的不必要升级负载。在匹配升级率（约34%）的比较中仍保持最佳性能（成功率0.784，FRR0.114）。消融实验表明各组件（含风险过滤、类比评分器、CQL正则化、自适应门控）均对最终性能有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 本文将安全自动修复从动作生成问题重构为干预决策问题，使安全成为一等目标。三大设计选择——CMDP目标与显式FRR预算、三维风险分解提供可部署安全接口、上下文自适应HITL门控将升级变为带宽感知控制层——在实验中得到验证。更广泛的启示是，自治修复的下一个关键进展不是更好的动作生成，而是更精准的"何时干预"判断。未来工作将拓展到参数化动作空间和拓扑漂移下的在线安全自适应。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [226] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC是一个基于LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6）的智能体框架，用于自动化后路由DRC（设计规则检查）违例修复。它通过布局分解、ReAct风格的修复智能体，以及基于知识数据库的技能演化机制，在DAC26-DRC基准测试的7个块级设计上实现了平均83.6%（论文摘要中报告73.5%）的DRV减少，其中两个设计完全清除违例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 先进工艺节点物理设计中DRC收敛仍是关键瓶颈，详细路由器虽具备规则感知能力，但残余DRV通常需要手工ECO迭代修复，耗时数天到数周。2. 自动化修复面临挑战：需考虑复杂几何交互、保持电路连通性、避免引入新违例。3. 现有LLM智能体在DRC修复上的研究局限于标准单元级规模，无法扩展到块级复杂设计。4. LLM智能体的修复能力高度依赖任务特定的技能文件（skill file），但针对新目标设计的技能文件通常不可用。

Method: EvoDRC框架包含三个核心阶段：(1) **布局分解**：根据DRV分布对布局进行分类，按k μm×k μm窗口（k=2）裁剪为有界修复区域（repair crops），合并相邻区块后并行分派修复任务。(2) **智能体修复**：每个修复智能体采用ReAct范式和四角色工作流（规划器、对抗性计划审查员、DRC工程师、补丁审查员），使用三种预览工具（局部DRC分析、连通性检查、影响预览）反馈操作结果；对象被分为四类编辑权限（A类过孔、B类完全在区域内多边形、C类裁剪多边形、D类禁止编辑的标准单元）。(3) **技能演化**：仅存储最终修复操作-DRV变化的轨迹记录到知识数据库；由Skill Refiner（增量更新当前技能文件）和DB Summarizer（基于完整历史重构技能文件）两个智能体分别生成候选技能文件，经机械检查（引用、引用声明、模糊性三项）和Skill Judge（引用检查、覆盖度、写作质量三项）选择后进入下一轮迭代。同时通过外部OpenCores CLA设计生成初始跨设计技能文件作为迁移起点。

Result: 在DAC26-DRC基准测试的7个块级设计（68–765初始DRV）上：EvoDRC平均实现83.6%的DRV减少（Block3和Block5完全清除DRV，分别达100%），Block1为55.3%、Block4为85.0%、Block6为92.7%、Block7为64.1%。三项消融实验分别验证了：(A1) 移除CLA初始技能文件后改进率从77.9%降至51.0%（证明跨设计技能迁移价值）；(A2) 冻结技能文件（禁用技能演化）后改进率降至73.0%（证明技能演化的重要性）；(A3) 禁用布局分解后Block7仅减少81/765 DRV（证明分解机制对可扩展性至关重要）。使用Claude Sonnet 4.6 API总成本约$1152（修复智能体占86.4%）。技能演化动态分析显示：第1次迭代后DB Summarizer主导（基于新数据重写技能），第3次迭代后Skill Refiner主导（基于已建立的技能文件增量优化）。

Conclusion: EvoDRC是首个针对块级DRV修复的智能体框架，将传统手工后路由DRC ECO自动化，使布局工程师可专注其他任务。其主要贡献在于：(1) 将DRC修复形式化为技能演化任务，通过跨设计技能迁移与目标设计上的测试时技能演化相结合，持续提升修复知识质量；(2) 引入布局分解机制通过将块级布局划分为有界修复区域来提升智能体DRC修复的可扩展性；(3) 在DAC26-DRC基准上实现83.6%的平均DRV减少。该工作表明，每一项修复操作（包括临时增加DRV计数的操作）都被评估并记录，使智能体能探索动作后果而非采用贪心搜索路径；从这些修复记录中演化出的知识能捕捉副作用，并在后续迭代中实现更协调的修复。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [227] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对差分约束（difference constraints）的全局界限一致性传播器（global bounds-consistent propagator），通过将所有差分约束作为一个整体（利用最短路径算法）进行处理而非逐个独立传播，并设计了适用于惰性子句生成（lazy clause generation）求解器的解释机制，实验证明该方法能显著优于传统的逐约束传播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分约束（x - y ≤ d）的满足性与蕴含问题由于与最短路径的关联已存在高效算法，但传统有限域传播算法仍将每条差分约束作为独立传播器处理，虽然保证了求解的完备性，却效率低下。此外，现有的SMT求解器中的差分约束理论求解器虽有相似之处，但其设计需求与CP求解器中的全局传播器存在显著差异。如何将全局传播思想与惰性子句生成框架结合是核心挑战。

Method: 1. 将所有差分约束 x - y ≤ d 统一建模为图结构，利用最短路径算法实现全局界限一致性传播；2. 分析全局差分约束传播器与SMT理论求解器在需求上的关键差异；3. 设计核心机制：如何为全局差分约束传播器产生解释（explanation），以便将其集成到惰性子句生成（lazy clause generation）求解器中，使传播过程可追溯、可学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，与标准的逐约束传播方法相比，将差分约束作为整体进行全局传播能够在多个测试实例上显著提升求解效率，减少传播次数与求解时间。

Conclusion: 差分约束应被视为一个全局结构而非独立约束集合；基于最短路径的全局界限一致性传播器配合恰当的解释机制，可在惰性子句生成框架中有效利用，显著提升CP求解器在差分约束问题上的性能。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [228] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以开源权重语言模型 google/gemma-4-E4B-it 为对象,系统性地从可读性(词汇读出)、表征(状态几何)、因果(干预与激活补丁)三个维度探究其对材料科学机制(本构关系、物理定律)的内部表示与使用方式,发现物理关系在受控的状态变化中比绝对状态更显著地显现出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)虽能正确回答科学问题,但正确答案并不表明模型是否真正表征或使用了相关物理机制。在材料科学等需要跨尺度、跨机制推理的领域,这种区别对评估模型可靠性、诊断失败及开发稳健的科学AI系统至关重要——正确答案可能源于物理表征、词汇线索、记忆关联或数值捷径,而这些可能性对模型的外推与未来训练影响迥异。

Method: 研究采用预先冻结的协议设计,使用 Gemma-4-E4B-it(42层,隐藏维度 2560)在不改变权重的前提下进行三类互补测量:(1)词汇读出——直接 unembedding 与三次独立拟合的 Jacobian 透镜对隐藏状态进行词汇排名,并配以靶向自由发现;(2)表征几何——分析无选项状态几何、构造物理等价词法对抗三元组(锚点/物理释义/词法反事实),并通过图同构网络、谱分析、精确划分审计检验图的可识别性;(3)因果干预——在固定层对最终提示词 token 的隐藏状态施加机制方向向量(剂量±4%),并进行完整的隐藏状态激活补丁实验。核心创新是引入 60 法本构关系基准(20 直接/20 逆/20 中性),通过配对的数值方向反转计算受控状态位移,以独立中性定律校准零点,分离"数值方向"与"本构方向"这两个可代数混叠的变量。

Result: (1) 受控术语恢复:50 个材料描述中 Jacobian 透镜与直接读出均大量为零(36/50 提示词两者皆零),家族级差异无统计学显著性(p=0.234),呈选择性、机制依赖性优势(如腐蚀 toughness 排名 1/11/11 vs 111/12,063),三次独立拟合的 Spearman 相关达 0.997 以上;(2) 靶向自由解码:自动化盲评识别 9/10 机制家族,但 Jacobian 与直接读出无显著差异;(3) 中层状态几何:72 题组中分类准确率峰值 62%(p=0.0002),但提示词 TF-IDF 基线达 76%;(4) 图可识别性审计:证明在固定机制内,"相同物理结果"代数等同"相同数值方向"(y=sx),绝对物理极性无法从未标注图中识别;(5) 60 法中性锚定基准:在配对数值反转的受控状态变化下,直接-逆 AUC=1.000,中性标定切割点正确分类 39/40 个有向定律,逆/中性/直接形成强有序响应(ρ=0.910,p=0.00001),而词法 TF-IDF 控制接近随机;(6) 因果干预:前瞻冻结的 6 对晶粒尺寸研究中,同一方向在 12/12 条件下正确反转(细化→"更高",粗化→"更低"),但换用 increase/decrease 词汇或逆 Hall-Petch 区域时失败;(7) 激活补丁:逆关系供体状态可使接收方答案转向相反关系(同合金 +6.249 对数几率,跨合金 +6.283),但跨机制移植时仅 4/6 供体家族传递正确物理符号。

Conclusion: 材料科学信息可在 Gemma 中被可重复读出,但其绝对状态几何很大程度上与词汇或数值比较兼容或混淆,无法唯一识别本构物理。当测量跟随受控的状态转换(而非绝对位置)时,较窄的物理抽象——单调本构取向——变得可见(60 法基准 AUC=1.000,ρ=0.910)。选定方向可在受约束的科学决策中施加上下文依赖的因果控制(12/12 配对成功),但该控制在转换到新词汇或新物理区域时失败,说明因果效应是局部的而非普遍的。本研究贡献了一套"校准测量、表征、干预、可识别性"的统一框架,据此提出表示感知训练目标(包含机制、反事实、特异性、不确定性奖励及词法捷径惩罚),并强调奖励探针必须与评估探针分离以防读出黑客行为。物理关系在受控状态变化中比在绝对状态中更显著可见这一核心结论,为未来在保留答案奖励的同时引入机制级与反事实级监督信号提供了可操作路径。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [229] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG 是一个面向长视野（long-horizon）任务的极简上下文管理框架，基于"程序化记忆"（programmatic memory）——即由框架自动追加完整、结构化的交互日志，并由代码代理通过编程方式（如正则表达式、Python）检索该日志。在 ARC-AGI-3 公开游戏集上，PRO-LONG 平均比基础代码代理提升 18.0 个百分点，匹配或超越当前最优专用 harness，且 token 成本仅为后者的 1/4.2–1/5.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野任务（如 ARC-AGI-3）要求持续感知、推理与探索，是 LLM 代理的长期难题。现有智能体在上下文中保存的信息越多，后续检索越困难，存在"保真度–易处理性"权衡（fidelity-tractability tradeoff）。同时，编码代理（coding agent）能力快速提升，具备以编程方式处理长上下文的新可能，但现有 memory 系统未能充分利用这些能力。

Method: PRO-LONG 围绕三个设计原则：(1) 简洁性——write 操作无需任何启发式或学习式决策，直接将每个 observation、action、outcome 追加到 logs.txt；read 操作无需向量数据库等专用检索机制。(2) 无损性（losslessness）——日志保留全部原始信息，无压缩或摘要。(3) 与编码代理天然兼容——通过正则表达式或 Python 代码进行检索，处理 100k+ 行的日志仍然可行。框架建立在基础代码代理（Codex / Claude Code）之上，仅追加约 30 行提示词，定义统一的 write/read 操作。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 25 个公开游戏的完整评测中：
- 跨 GPT-5.5、Opus 4.6、Fable 5 等前沿模型，PRO-LONG 平均比无日志的 no-log 基线提升 18.0 个百分点（区间 15.7–21.0）。
- Opus 4.6 上达 42.4% pass@1（该模型公开最优）；GPT-5.5 上达 41.2% pass@1、60.1% best@5，相比 WorldModeler 使用 5.8× 更少 token。
- Fable 5 上达 97.4% best@2，总成本仅 $1,750；相比 Schema 的 99.0% best@2（$6,447），成本不到 1/3。
- 工具消融显示，更强的程序化访问能力（grep、Python）持续提升性能，而 write/edit 等额外抽象增益有限。
- 工作区持久性消融表明，PRO-LONG 对工作区持久化几乎不依赖（仅降 0.5 点），而 no-log 严重依赖（降约 4 点）。
- 定性分析显示，PRO-LONG 在需要长视野推理的游戏（如 g50t、m0r0）上提升最显著；工具使用统计显示其 60.6% 的调用用于 Python，9.6% 用于 grep。

Conclusion: 程序化感知与推理（例如以代码解析棋盘、建模游戏转移、通过 BFS 规划）对持续学习任务具有广泛的适用性；简洁的 harness 即能有效激发这些行为，PRO-LONG 即是此类最简而高效的实现。该模型无关的方法在所有评估设置中均具有竞争力，token 成本显著低于各模型上的最强先前 harness，并在公开 ARC-AGI-3 上达到最高 97.4%。未来工作包括：缩小 pass@1 与 best@k 之间的差距、与 harness 联合训练模型、以及研究程序化记忆与其他记忆机制（如便签、压缩）的相互作用。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [230] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRUST-ESD 是一个面向企业战略决策支持的风险校准与治理感知AI框架，通过整合保形不确定性校准、CVaR下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性和人工监督，在不确定性下推荐兼顾价值、可靠性、风险敞口和合规性的反事实策略，而非仅依据最大期望效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策面临市场波动、运营不确定性、资源约束和治理要求，但现有AI决策支持方法多以预测为中心，仅选择预期效用最高的策略，无法反映决策风险、政策可行性和下游商业后果。需要在统一管道中整合预测效用、校准不确定性、下行风险控制、历史风险记忆和策略合规性，以实现真正可信的企业决策支持。

Method: 该框架将企业状态表示为业务/市场/运营/外部约束的组合，通过反事实策略生成器构造可行策略集（含保守/激进/促销/成本节约/风险控制/产能扩张六类），对每条策略依次执行：预测效用估计、保形预测区间校准、CVaR尾部风险评分、相似历史案例检索的风险记忆评估、策略即代码治理合规检查、基于评分分解的可解释性输出，以及人工审核与审计日志记录。综合评分函数联合权衡效用、不确定性、风险、治理合规和硬性政策违规，选择满足硬约束条件下评分最高的策略。

Result: 基于Rossmann和M5零售数据集的实验表明，完整TRUST-ESD相对Deep Ensembles基线：风险调整效用提升7.95%，风险敞口降低23.22%，CVaR降低23.78%，校准误差降低13.89%，解释保真度提升10.90%，治理合规率提升9.76%（达0.967），同时保持有竞争力的预测精度。消融实验证实各组件均有不可替代的贡献：移除CVaR风险评分导致风险敞口激增56.79%，移除保形校准使ECE增加96.77%，移除治理模块则使合规率下降9.82%。案例分析展示框架倾向于选择风险-治理平衡的策略而非单纯高效用策略。

Conclusion: TRUST-ESD通过统一整合预测建模、不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、策略治理、可解释性和人工监督，将企业决策支持从纯预测推荐推向可信战略决策，在风险调整效用、风险控制、校准质量、解释保真度和治理合规方面均取得显著改进，同时各组件的贡献通过消融研究得到验证。未来工作将拓展至动态企业环境、自适应治理策略和真实人类反馈场景。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [231] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MGT-B（Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking），一种针对低比特量化小型推理模型的推理时监控与回退-重解码控制器。该控制器结合窗口级不确定性与退化特征，以 CUSUM 形重置统计量在生成过程中检测无效轨迹，并在触发警报时估计回退点、恢复 token 与 KV-cache 状态后进行约束重解码。在 MATH-500 上的主要审计集（240 对仅包含阈值选择后首次观测的题目）显示准确率从 82/240 提升至 88/240（+2.50 个百分点），但 McNemar 检验不显著（p=0.2632）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏量化小型自回归推理模型（本文指 4-bit DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B）在长链思维推理中容易陷入重复或无产出的轨迹，而推理时的计算预算通常在不了解轨迹发展的情况下预先分配。文章指出：以居中的已选 token 对数概率作为独立鞅增量，在自信重复环中会接近零，无法可靠识别退化；因此需要一种能在生成过程中监测轨迹健康状况并在仍可挽救时进行针对性干预的机制。

Method: 控制器为不修改模型权重的外部序列监控架构：（1）以长度 64、步长 32 的滑动窗口提取 6 维特征（熵、负对数概率、重复率、自信重复增量、局部熵正/负变化）；（2）按窗口末端位置桶对校准轨迹的得分做位置条件经验 ECDF，得到经验上尾概率 q_j；（3）对 beta 指数网格取混合得到增量下注因子 e_j，并使用 CUSUM 形重置统计量 S_j = max(0, S_{j-1}) + log e_j；（4）当 S_j ≥ h（本文手设对数阈值 h=10）时，从最近一次 S_j ≤ 0 的窗口估计回退位置，截断 token 与 KV-cache、重建监控与 n-gram 状态、重置检测器，并以更低温度（0.6）、重复惩罚（1.1）和可疑 n-gram 阻塞进行约束重解码。文中明确声明：经验 q_j 未被证明条件超均匀，因此该统计量仅为"类 CUSUM"运行操作点，不声称 e-process 或 e-detector 理论保证。

Result: 在 MATH-500 上：（1）核心 240 对时序审计集（排除阈值选择前已出现的 260 题）准确率由 82/240 升至 88/240（+2.50 pp，13 修正/7 退化，精确 McNemar p=0.2632，配对自助 95% CI [−1.25, +6.25]）；（2）更广的 467 对历史覆盖集准确率由 146/467 升至 167/467（+4.50 pp，29 修正/8 退化，p=0.000753，CI [+1.93, +7.07]），但其中 200 题在阈值选择前已暴露，仅作探索性报告；（3）467 对中 32.33% 触发警报，151 条警报轨迹含 29 修正与 8 退化，所有 316 条未警报输出与 vanilla 完全一致；（4）与随机回退、周期回退、重启、自纠正等控制策略相比（各策略获 10% 预算上限放宽），MGT-B 取得最高聚合准确率 35.76%，其中周期回退（26.55%）与重启（24.63%）显著低于 vanilla；（5）token 统计显示 MGT-B 平均采样 4961.15、vanilla 5034.52，日志延迟仅作描述性报告。

Conclusion: 本文为所研究的单一量化推理模型/精度/任务（MATH-500）提供了一种选择性监控-修复机制的概念验证：警报具有选择性（未警报轨迹与 vanilla 完全一致），且在历史覆盖集中显著优于非选择性回退与重启控制。但结论有限：（1）核心审计集的统计结果不确定（p=0.2632，置信区间含零）；（2）经验因子未被建立为合法 e-process 或 e-detector，阈值仅为样本内运行操作点；（3）所有分析均为回顾性、非验证性，467 集的名义 p 值因阈值选择而不独立；（4）未完成组件归因消融（特征、累积、回退、约束重解码各自贡献未知），未涉及第二模型、干净全精度、第二数据集或标记的退化标签。未来工作需要前瞻性固定阈值、独立评估集、直接对比旧版 token 级控制器，以及逐组件消融来厘清增益来源。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [232] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoTRE（Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles），一种基于异构多智能体的推理框架，将推理过程解耦为四个专门化代理（对抗精炼、分层战略规划、谱搜索、直接链式），并通过任务自适应聚合层动态整合。在 Humanity's Last Exam（HLE）上达到 49.92% 的最先进准确率，且推理 token 消耗与同构基线相当或更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在需要长程规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务上表现欠佳，且标准的单流提示（single-stream prompting）在面对新颖抽象或严格领域约束时较为脆弱。现有方法多依赖同构模型的暴力扩展（如 Self-Consistency、ToT），计算开销大但鲁棒性有限，亟需一种能够通过架构异构性实现高效且稳健推理的方案。

Method: PoTRE 采用异构多智能体架构，包含四个并行的推理代理：(1) 对抗精炼代理（Adversarial Refinement Agent），通过对抗性扰动实现自我修正；(2) 分层战略规划代理（Hierarchical Strategic Planning Agent），进行多层级战略分解与规划；(3) 谱搜索代理（Spectrum Search Agent），在解空间中进行广谱探索；(4) 直接链式代理（Direct Chain Agent），提供标准线性推理基线。最终通过任务自适应聚合层（Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer）根据任务特性动态选择整合策略，包括最终候选选择、语义合成（semantic synthesis）或神经符号验证（neuro-symbolic verification）三种方式，输出全局鲁棒解。

Result: 在三个前沿基准上验证：(1) ARC-AGI-2（抽象推理挑战赛第二代）；(2) Humanity's Last Exam（HLE，人类终极考试）；(3) PRBench Finance（金融专业基准）。其中在 HLE 上取得 49.92% 的准确率，超越此前的官方最佳成绩，达到新的 SOTA。同时实验表明，PoTRE 的架构异构性在推理 token 消耗与现有重度扩展的同构基线相当或更少的前提下，实现了更优的推理性能，体现了更高的"推理性价比"。

Conclusion: PoTRE 证明了通过多智能体架构异构性（而非单纯算力堆叠）可显著提升 LLM 的复杂推理能力。其核心洞见是：不同拓扑结构的推理路径具有互补优势，任务自适应聚合机制能够有效调和这些异构视角。该框架为克服单流提示的脆弱性、应对长程规划与领域约束任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [233] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了自然语言自编码器（NLA）用于激活解释的重构测试，发现其可通过"要点匹配"或"私有编码"两种不可靠方式通过测试，并提出RECAP方法——在目标模型训练时加入辅助预测器，使指定内容保持可解码性，从而实现可验证的激活解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有NLA的激活解释测试（重构分数）存在结构性盲区：翻转某个具体声明若不改变重构分数，则该声明永不被惩罚，导致高重构分数并不保证逐条声明的忠实性。2. 在AI安全语境下，依赖模型自我生成的解释进行监控存在被博弈的风险（类似steganographic通道），需要一种不依赖模型自身生成文本的可独立验证机制。3. 现有的"修复读者"方案（如更好的verbalizer/reconstructor）均失败，需要从目标模型本身入手。

Method: 1. 提出两项审计协议：(a) grounded-vs-true交叉表——区分重构敏感性与声明真值性；(b) evaluator swap——用独立评估器检验编码是否为共适应私有约定。2. 提出RECAP（Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors）：在目标模型训练时附加线性辅助预测头（heads），使其从指定隐层状态预测外部目标内容，将头损失加入语言建模损失。3. 关键设计：所有可解码性指标均由独立拟合的新鲜探针（fresh probes）测量，而非训练头本身，以排除共适应偏差。4. 在三个场景下评估：Qwen-2.5-7B已发布NLA、含精确真值的合成沙盒、Pythia-160M持续预训练。

Result: 1. Qwen-2.5-7B系统：重构分数r̃=0.84，但仅~2%的具体声明具有重构依赖性（grounded）；变换轮廓控制证实分数由要点（gist）而非具体措辞承载。2. 合成沙盒：标准方案在5/5次运行中均产生共适应私有编码（grounded-minus-true差距0.30-0.80）；evaluator swap使编码槽位grounding从0.53-0.89降至0.08-0.23，而真实声明的grounding保持不变。3. 替代方案均失败：重构目标在3/3种子中均导致可解码内容塌缩；冻结探针在1500步内退化至随机水平（信息仍可通过无标签仿射映射恢复）。4. RECAP在沙盒中使指定内容达到100%可解码性，新鲜NLA以~1.00真值与grounding陈述指定内容，编码消失（0/5运行），代价仅+0.001 nats。5. Pythia-160M上：RECAP使新鲜探针AUC达0.95-1.00（对照0.77-0.80），代价值取决于目标设计（K=64时+0.010 nats，在噪声范围内）。6. 探针监控：RECAP使独立探针对真假声明的判别AUC从0.82提升至0.96（95% CI不相交）。7. 对抗鲁棒性：报告空间对手通过优化r̃可抑制~87%的谎言惩罚，但RECAP探针在L3对抗下仍保持AUC 0.95（对照探针降至随机0.51）；最坏情况L4选择攻击下RECAP仍保持AUC 0.91（对照跌至0.24）。8. 局限：可解码性≠可语言化——在Pythia上RECAP使真实陈述率从~0提升至0.44-0.46（强reconstructor下达0.57），但自由形式生成的探针精度仅0.44-0.63，而直接探针精度可达0.80。

Conclusion: 1. 方法论意义：高重构分数不构成激活解释的逐条声明证据；标准的逐条修复（重写verbalizer、训练grounding奖励、重构目标对齐）均因已识别机制（塌缩、漂移、代理博弈）失败。2.
